### [1] [Unsupervised spiking feature learning for event-based pedestrian crossing detection: approaching supervised accuracy without labelled training data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31671)
*Henok Teklu,Mustafa Sakhai,Matej Mertik,Maciej Wielgosz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 首个在DVS-PedX行人穿越基准上的无监督脉冲神经网络检测方法，特征学习无需标签，仅用线性分类器作监督读出，精度接近有监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机适合行人穿越检测，脉冲神经网络（SNN）可原生处理其输出，但现有SNN检测器依赖监督反向传播，需要昂贵的帧级穿越标签。作者希望探索特征学习阶段完全无标签的检测方法。

Method: 用胜者通吃（WTA）脉冲时序依赖可塑性（STDP）训练单层脉冲层，从未标注的事件帧块中学习字典；帧通过余弦相似度编码并结合空间池化，最后由线性分类器读出（唯一监督部分）。

Result: 在24,454帧测试集上达90.3%准确率和0.936 AUROC（有监督端到端为92.0%和0.943），恶劣天气下AUROC为0.913。结果对可塑性规则选择不敏感，但强烈依赖读出协议：经典神经元分配读出仅0.70 AUROC。在转换为真实数据部分，读出重拟合将性能从随机水平（0.53）提升至0.67 AUROC，处于已发表有监督范围内。

Conclusion: 在该基准上，去除特征学习标签带来的精度损失很小；无监督脉冲网络被报道的弱点可能源于读出协议而非学习规则本身。

Abstract: Event cameras are well suited to pedestrian crossing detection, and spiking neural networks (SNNs) can process their output natively, but current SNN detectors are trained with supervised backpropagation and therefore require costly frame-level crossing labels. We investigate crossing detection with no labels in feature learning and report the first unsupervised results on the recent DVS-PedX pedestrian-crossing benchmark. A single spiking layer trained with winner-take-all spike-timing-dependent plasticity learns a dictionary from unlabelled event-frame patches; frames are encoded by cosine similarity to the learned filters with spatial pooling and read out by a linear classifier, the only supervised component. On the 24,454-frame test split the method attains 90.3% accuracy and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.936, compared with 92.0% and 0.943 for a supervised spiking network trained end-to-end on the same frames; under adverse weather the AUROC is 0.913. The result is insensitive to the choice of plasticity rule but depends strongly on the readout protocol: with the classical neuron-assignment readout the same network attains only 0.70 AUROC. On the benchmark's real converted portion, a readout refit lifts performance from chance (0.53) to 0.67 AUROC, within the published supervised range. These findings indicate that the accuracy cost of removing labels from feature learning is small on this benchmark, and that reported weaknesses of unsupervised spiking networks may be attributable to the readout protocol rather than to the learning rule.

</details>


### [2] [RemTraceNet: Few-Shot Forensic Detection of Invisible Watermark Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31694)
*Jidong Yang,Huaike Yu,Qi Li,Yuantian Miao,Wei Zong,Yang-Wai Chow,Willy Susilo,Chunpeng Wang,Suo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究水印去除攻击的取证痕迹检测问题，提出RemTraceNet，在有限监督下区分被攻击图像与干净/未攻击水印图像，证实"去除水印"与"去除去除痕迹"是不同难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去除隐形水印与隐藏取证证据是两个不同目标：破坏水印不代表去除过程不可被取证检测。当验证失败时，去除痕迹能为来源与所有权验证提供补充证据，其缺失则使失败原因模糊。现有方法通常只评估水印抑制与感知质量，很少考虑取证隐蔽性。

Method: 提出针对水印攻击的少样本取证设定：对每个已知pipeline，专家将其输出与成对的干净和未攻击水印对照样本区分。分别进行Attack-vs-Clean和Attack-vs-Watermarked评估以避免水印存在性捷径；对事后方案使用图像对齐对照，对生成器集成方案使用提示匹配对照。方法上提出RemTraceNet，在原生分辨率下融合约束残差、局部关系、FFT/Haar统计与块DCT证据。

Result: 在23种去除pipeline和10种水印配置上，评估256x256和512x512原生输入。每pipeline用100张受攻击训练图像时，三种子TPR@1%FPR在不同分辨率与对照类型下宏平均为82.75%至88.17%。相同标签与协议下，RemTraceNet比重新训练的SRNet、ZhuNet、SiaStegNet基线高10.80–15.68个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验表明，擦除水印与擦除其去除证据是两个不同挑战，且在有限监督下去除痕迹仍可学习。

Abstract: Removing an invisible watermark and concealing the forensic evidence are distinct objectives: successfully disrupting the embedded watermark does not imply that the removal process is forensically undetectable. When verification fails, removal traces can provide complementary evidence for provenance and ownership verification, whereas their absence leaves the cause of the failure ambiguous. Existing methods are typically evaluated by watermark suppression and perceptual quality, while forensic stealth is rarely considered. We therefore study watermark-attack-specific few-shot forensics: for each known pipeline, a specialist can separate its outputs from paired clean and unattacked watermarked controls. Separate Attack-vs-Clean and Attack-vs-Watermarked evaluations prevent watermark-presence shortcuts. Image-aligned and prompt-matched controls are used for post-hoc and generator-integrated schemes, respectively. In this work, we introduce RemTraceNet, which fuses constrained residuals, local relations, FFT/Haar statistics, and block-DCT evidence at native resolution. Across 23 removal pipelines and 10 watermark configurations, we evaluate native 256 x 256 and 512 x 512 inputs. With 100 attacked training images per pipeline, the three-seed TPR@1%FPR, macro-averaged over attacks and watermark configurations, ranges from 82.75% to 88.17% across resolutions and control types. Under the condition of same labels and protocol, RemTraceNet outperforms retrained SRNet, ZhuNet, and SiaStegNet baselines by 10.80--15.68 percentage points. Extensive experimental results show that erasing a watermark and erasing evidence of its removal are distinct challenges, and that removal traces remain learnable under limited supervision.

</details>


### [3] [CLC-YOLO: A Compact Channel-Gated Prototype Network for Real-Time Leakage-Aware Breast Ultrasound Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31702)
*M. Fazri Nizar,Muhammad Naufal Rachmatullah,Julian Supardi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量通道局部对比（CLC）模块，作为YOLO26分割头改进，提升乳腺超声病灶分割精度且开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乳腺超声病灶分割需要准确边界，同时评估时应避免患者或重复图像跨数据划分，以得到可靠结果。

Method: CLC是对YOLO26分割原型头的紧凑改进，加入固定局部高通残差，由64个零初始化、有界通道门控控制。在四个数据集上以五折匹配比较基线与CLC：BUS-BRA用患者分离外测与内验证，BUS-UCLM/BrEaST用患者分组验证折，BUSI因无患者标识用重复组件分组。

Result: group-macro Dice在BUS-BRA、BUS-UCLM、BUSI、BrEaST分别提升1.68、3.11、1.12、2.45个百分点，仅BUS-BRA配对95%置信区间不含零。CLC仅增加64参数和0.0049 GFLOPs。640像素、单T4、batch=1、FP16 TensorRT推理时间CLC 2.1478 ms，基线2.0512 ms，前向额外开销0.0966 ms。

Conclusion: CLC在四个数据集均提升group-macro Dice，且T4前向开销仅0.0966 ms，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reliable breast ultrasound lesion segmentation requires accurate boundaries and evaluation that prevents patients or duplicate images from crossing data splits. We propose Channel Local Contrast (CLC), a compact refinement of the YOLO26 segmentation prototype head. CLC adds a fixed local high-pass residual controlled by 64 zero-initialized, bounded channel gates. Baseline and CLC were compared in five matched folds on each of four breast ultrasound datasets. BUS-BRA used patient-disjoint outer tests with separate inner validation. BUS-UCLM and BrEaST used patient-grouped validation folds; BUSI used duplicate-component groups because patient identifiers are unavailable. Group-macro Dice increased by 1.68, 3.11, 1.12, and 2.45 percentage points on BUS-BRA, BUS-UCLM, BUSI, and BrEaST, respectively. Only the BUS-BRA paired 95% confidence interval excluded zero. CLC adds 64 parameters and 0.0049 giga floating-point operations (GFLOPs). At 640 pixels, single-T4, batch-one, 16-bit floating-point (FP16) TensorRT graph times were 2.1478 ms for CLC and 2.0512 ms for baseline, excluding preprocessing and postprocessing. CLC increased group-macro Dice across all four datasets with a measured T4 forward-pass overhead of 0.0966 ms. Code: https://github.com/mfazrinizar/CLC-YOLO

</details>


### [4] [Statistical Testing for Multiple Instance Learning via Selective Inference with Applications to Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31712)
*Noriaki Hashimoto,Shuichi Nishino,Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Daiki Miwa,Hiroyuki Hanada,Jun Sakuma,Hidekata Hontani,Hiroaki Miyoshi,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将注意力MIL中高注意力实例的可靠性评估建模为统计假设检验问题，并引入选择性推断框架解决数据依赖选择导致的检验失效问题，实现有效的选择性p值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在注意力MIL中，高注意力分数实例常被视为诊断重要区域并用作视觉解释，但注意力分数本身无法判断所选实例是否显著偏离正常实例，限制了注意力解释的可靠性。

Method: 将高注意力实例评估形式化为统计假设检验：判断所选高注意力实例是否显著偏离基于特征相似度选出的代表性正常参考实例。由于目标实例和参考实例均经数据依赖过程选出，标准检验失效，因此提出选择性推断（SI）框架，显式建模注意力实例选择与自适应参考选择引起的选择事件，计算条件于这些事件的有效选择性p值。

Result: 在合成数据和基于MNIST的数据上验证了Type-I错误控制，并展示了在病理WSI上的实用性，统计功效高于传统过条件化方法。

Conclusion: 所提选择性推断框架为注意力MIL的高注意力实例提供了有效的统计检验与更可靠的解释，且比传统方法具有更高统计功效。

Abstract: Multiple instance learning (MIL) is widely used in computational pathology because it enables weakly supervised analysis of whole-slide images (WSIs) without requiring patch-level annotations. In attention-based MIL, instances with high attention scores are often interpreted as diagnostically important regions and used as visual explanations. However, attention scores alone cannot determine whether selected high-attention instances are significantly different from normal instances, limiting the reliability of attention-based explanations. In this paper, we formulate the evaluation of high-attention instances as a statistical hypothesis testing problem. Specifically, we assess whether a selected high-attention instance significantly deviates from a representative normal reference instance selected based on feature similarity. A major challenge is that both the target instance and the reference instance are selected through data-dependent procedures, rendering standard hypothesis testing invalid. To address this issue, we introduce a selective inference (SI) framework that explicitly accounts for the selection events induced by attention-based instance selection and adaptive reference selection, thereby enabling the computation of valid selective $p$-values conditional on these events. Experiments demonstrate Type-I error control on synthetic and MNIST-based data and practical applicability to pathological WSIs, with higher statistical power than the conventional over-conditioning approach.

</details>


### [5] [Fusion Under Component Failure: Negative Results and Failure Modes in Ensemble AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31749)
*Suraj Singh,Tushar Verma,Pragyan Singh,Shaurya Bhav,Shivam Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该文对已部署的AI生成图像检测堆叠集成进行三项对照检验，并纠正此前结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署的融合系统需经受对照检验，包括在目标域重拟合、多语料评估与弃权（缺失数据）处理。

Method: 用三个开放检测器的五个分数经梯度提升元学习器融合，将检测器失败视为缺失；随后做域内重拟合、80张截图评估、弃权/法定人数规则三项对照。

Result: 跨域元学习器不优于最佳单成员（2000张StyleGAN人脸 AUC 0.9896 vs 0.9961）；域内重拟合后胜出校准单检测器（ΔAUC=+0.0025, p=3.4e-4）。80张截图上各模型AUC区间含0.5。全NaN输入得P(AI)=0.9985，三个AIDE检查点同因共享预处理而同时失效，双架构法定人数规则可无重训防止。语料A存在类条件JPEG偏置。

Conclusion: 融合仅在同域重拟合时才值其成本；单一语料不足以评估；弃权真实且相关需妥善处理；已发布代码、工具与所有更正。

Abstract: We built an ordinary stacking ensemble for AI-generated image detection -- three open detectors producing five scores, fused by a gradient-boosted meta-learner that treats a detector's failure as missing data -- deployed it, and then evaluated it against three controls it should have faced first. This paper reports what the controls found, including where they overturned our own earlier conclusions.
  Fusion is worth its cost only when refitted on the target domain. The shipped meta-learner, fitted on a separate corpus, does not beat its best single member on 2000 StyleGAN faces (AUC 0.9896 vs 0.9961; McNemar p = 1.000). But a stacker refitted in-domain beats that member plus a post-hoc calibrator (Delta-AUC = +0.0025, [+0.0014, +0.0038]; p = 3.4e-4). An earlier draft claimed the calibrated single detector won outright; that comparison mixed regimes and we correct it here.
  One corpus is not an evaluation. On 80 screenshots every model's AUC interval contains 0.5. We can say nothing stronger: the difference between the ensemble's drop and its best member's is [-0.185, +0.115].
  Abstention is real, correlated, and mishandled. With four of five detectors silent and the survivor reporting "real", the system returns P(AI) = 0.9985, because an all-NaN input scores 0.9995 in a learner never fitted with missingness. The three AIDE checkpoints fail together, sharing one preprocessing path. A quorum rule requiring two distinct architectures prevents both failures with no retraining.
  We also find Corpus A carries a class-conditional JPEG bias severe enough to separate the classes from the header alone, which limits every in-domain number we report. Code, harness, hash-identified artifacts and all corrections are released.

</details>


### [6] [KeyRec: Bounded Visual Memory for Streaming and Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32182)
*Zihan Chen,Xuejian Rong,Xiaojuan Wang,Boqing Gong,Adi Zicher,Yael Pritch,Nikhil Karnad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeyRec: 免训练框架，用有界视觉记忆压缩长视频视觉token，在仅用10%预算下取得最佳压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频/连续流中视觉token随时长累积，长上下文推理成本过高；现有免训练token选择方法会丢失连贯事件证据，且无法区分近期细节观测与长期历史。

Method: 免训练有界视觉记忆构建：(1) 查询无关写入，近期细粒度观测存入视觉缓存，历史证据组织为结构化事件库，基于相对新颖性提出候选事件并用在线add-merge-evict更新；(2) 查询到来时，仅文本路由器在固定读出预算下自适应分配近期与事件记忆，不重处理历史帧。作用于模型侧视觉嵌入，支持模块化编码器-投影器VLM及无编码器投影器的NEO-ov架构。

Result: 在4个流式/长视频基准、3个VLM骨干上，15个设置中13个取得最佳压缩性能，仅用10%密集解码器侧视觉token预算；实时问题比最强压缩基线高2.21–18.37分；6个长视频设置中5个最佳；所有NEO-ov 2B设置均最佳。

Conclusion: KeyRec作为免训练有界视觉记忆框架，在极低token预算下显著优于现有压缩基线，兼容多种VLM架构，验证了事件级记忆与自适应读写分配的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used to understand long videos and continuous streams. However, dense visual tokens accumulate with video duration, making long-context inference prohibitively expensive. Existing training-free visual-token selection methods reduce this cost by retaining informative tokens, but may lose coherent event evidence and fail to distinguish detailed recent observations from long-range history. We propose \textbf{KeyRec}, a training-free framework for constructing bounded visual memory. During query-agnostic writing, KeyRec preserves fine-grained recent observations in a visual cache and organizes historical evidence into a structured event bank. Candidate events are proposed according to their novelty relative to previously stored events and maintained through an online add--merge--evict update. When a question arrives, a text-only router adaptively allocates a fixed readout budget between recent and event memory, without reprocessing historical frames. KeyRec operates on model-facing visual embeddings and supports both modular encoder--projector VLMs and the encoder- and projector-free NEO-ov architecture. Across four streaming and long-video benchmarks and three VLM backbones, KeyRec achieves the best compressed performance in 13 of 15 settings using only 10\% of the dense decoder-facing visual-token budget. It outperforms the strongest compressed baseline by 2.21--18.37 points on real-time questions, achieves the best compressed result in five of six long-video settings, and performs best in every NEO-ov 2B setting.

</details>


### [7] [Toward On-Chip Training of Spiking Neural Networks for Dense Event-Based Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32405)
*Maxime Vaillant,Axel Carlier,Lai Xing Ng,Christophe Hurter,Benoit R. Cottereau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DELL 提出一种分块局部学习方案，用可学习的空间结构化局部头对稠密事件视觉任务（光流、语义分割）进行逐块监督，替代全局 BPTT，显著降低训练内存并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机适合资源受限机器人，SNN 天然处理事件流，但 BPTT 训练深层 SNN 内存开销大且难适配神经形态硬件；现有局部学习方法多针对分类，缺乏稠密预测方案。

Method: DELL 采用分块（block-wise）方案，用局部稠密监督替代全局梯度传播；每个块配有可学习、空间结构化的局部头，在合适分辨率上监督该块并保留块内时间动态；在完全脉冲的 U 形架构上评估，用于光流回归与语义分割。

Result: DSEC 光流上，DELL 相比端到端 BPTT 峰值内存降低 39.6% 且精度更高（测试集 EPE 1.670px 对 1.941px）；DECOLLE 基线因固定随机局部读出 EPE 高 3.9 倍，可学习局部头可弥补并全面超越端到端；分割任务恢复大部分差距但仍落后端到端几 mIoU；仅 2.3M 参数（比最强 SNN 基线少 24 倍）仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: DELL 将局部学习扩展到稠密事件预测任务，在保证与 SNN 前沿相当性能的同时大幅降低训练内存，块分离更像正则化而非约束。

Abstract: Event cameras provide low-latency, asynchronous visual sensing for resource-constrained robotics. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) process event streams naturally, but training deep SNNs with backpropagation through time (BPTT) requires substantial memory and remains difficult on neuromorphic hardware. Local learning avoids this by restricting error propagation to local blocks, but existing methods mainly target classification rather than dense prediction. We introduce DELL (Dense Event-driven Local Learning), a block wise scheme for dense event-based vision that replaces global gradient propagation with local dense supervision. Learnable, spatially structured local heads supervise each block at its appropriate resolution while preserving temporal dynamics within blocks. We evaluate DELL on optical-flow regression and semantic segmentation with a fully spiking U-shaped architecture. On DSEC optical flow, DELL reduces peak training memory by 39.6% relative to end-to-end BPTT while improving accuracy, reaching 1.670 px endpoint error on the official test benchmark versus 1.941 px for the same backbone trained end-to-end. Block detachment behaves more like a regularizer than a constraint. DECOLLE, the existing local-learning baseline, relies on fixed random local read-outs poorly suited to dense regression, resulting in a 3.9x higher endpoint error; learnable local heads recover this loss and outperform end-to-end training across all optical-flow metrics. On segmentation, they recover most of the performance gap, although DELL remains a few mIoU points behind end-to-end training. With 2.3M parameters, 24x fewer than the strongest SNN baseline, the backbone remains competitive with the SNN state of the art on DSEC. These results extend local learning to dense event-based prediction while substantially reducing training memory.

</details>


### [8] [RIPE-MambaSpike: Resolution-Independent Spiking-State-Space Interfaces for Parameter-Efficient Event-Based Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32537)
*Md Muhiminul Islam,Shoaib Ahmed Dipu,Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RIPE-MambaSpike，通过分辨率无关的固定通道宽度多分辨率桥接替换参数量巨大的投影层，将部署参数从3600万降至87万，并在事件视觉基准上取得帕累托最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲-Mamba混合模型在事件视觉上精度强，但参数常达数千万。作者发现主要开销来自脉冲前端到状态空间主干的分辨率依赖投影，而非混合架构本身——示例模型中单一投影就占36.25M中的33.55M。

Method: 用固定通道宽度的分层多分辨率桥接替换分辨率依赖投影；结合重参数化脉冲阶段、时间解耦调制和动态凸包约束双流膜电位注意力。部署参数0.870M，在43倍输入面积范围内保持恒定。

Result: 在CIFAR10-DVS、N-Caltech101、DailyDVS-200上达帕累托最优。DailyDVS-200(200类)上，8.04M配置达45.7% top-1，为该基准最佳脉冲结果，参数量比稠密ANN少3.0-15.1倍。

Conclusion: 高效的基于事件识别不必然需要随分辨率增长的参数量，紧凑设计仍可保持竞争力。

Abstract: Spiking-Mamba hybrids reach strong accuracy on event-based vision, but existing designs often require tens of millions of parameters. Much of that cost comes from how the spiking front-end is connected to the state-space backbone rather than from the hybrid architecture itself. In a representative model, a single resolution-dependent projection accounts for 33.55M of 36.25M parameters. To that end, we introduce RIPE-MambaSpike (Resolution-Independent, Parameter-Efficient), which replaces that projection with a hierarchical multi-resolution bridge of fixed channel width. Its deployed footprint is 0.870M parameters, constant at fixed time steps and widths across a 43x range of input areas. Reparameterized spiking stages, temporal decoupled modulation, and a dynamic convex-hull-bounded dual-stream membrane-potential attention preserve accuracy under this compact design. Result-wise, RIPE-MambaSpike is pareto-optimal on CIFAR10-DVS, N-Caltech101, and DailyDVS-200. Notably, on the 200-class DailyDVS-200, a scaled 8.04M configuration achieves 45.7% top-1 accuracy, the best reported spiking result on that benchmark, and outperforms prior spiking methods with 3.0-15.1x fewer parameters than dense ANNs. Overall, our findings demonstrate that competitive event-based recognition does not require resolution-dependent parameter growth. Code is available at https://github.com/MuhiminOsim/RIPE-MambaSpike.

</details>


### [9] [Harnessing Coupled Stream Completion For Human-Object Ineraction Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32551)
*Dawei Guan,Di Yang,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRACE是一个连续潜变量框架，通过分离身体、物体、手部运动流的潜状态并耦合其更新，结合几何损失约束接触与相对运动，实现文本条件下的HOI生成与理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件下的HOI生成需协调身体运动、物体轨迹与旋转、手部关节，这些成分尺度和动态各异但须在接触、相对位姿和时序上一致。共享表示会限制各流的独特结构，独立生成则无法相互响应，仅用潜监督也难以直接约束解码后的接触。

Method: TRACE将身体、物体、手部运动编码为独立潜变量，从完整当前交互状态预测每流的velocity更新；在解码运动上施加几何损失以约束接触与物体相对运动；同一模型支持从两流补全任一缺失流；冻结的flow特征作为语言模型输入用于HOI理解。

Result: 在InterAct、OMOMO和BEHAVE上，联合补全训练提升生成效果，冻结flow特征相比原始运动编码提升理解性能；在InterAct上，TRACE在接触精度、召回率和F1上均优于对比方法。

Conclusion: 分离流潜状态并耦合更新、结合几何监督，能在保持各流结构的同时促进跨流一致性，统一支持HOI生成与理解任务。

Abstract: Text-conditioned human-object interaction (HOI) generation requires body motion, object trajectories & rotations, and hand articulation to remain coordinated. These components differ in scale and dynamics, but must agree on contact, relative pose, and timing. A shared representation may limit the distinct structure of each stream, while independent generation prevents each stream from responding to changes in the others. Latent supervision alone also does not directly constrain contact after decoding. We propose TRACE, a continuous latent framework that keeps stream states separate and couples their updates. TRACE encodes body, object, and hand motion into separate latents and predicts each stream velocity from the complete current interaction state. Geometric losses on decoded motion further constrain contact and object-relative motion over time. The same model supports completion of any single absent stream from the other two. Frozen flow features also serve as input to a language model for HOI understanding. Experiments on InterAct, OMOMO, and BEHAVE show that joint completion training improves generation and that frozen flow features improve understanding over raw-motion encoding. On InterAct, TRACE achieves the highest contact precision, recall, and F1 among the compared methods.

</details>


### [10] [Retrieved but Not Delivered: Multimodal Memory Delivery for Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32590)
*Yuhang Jiang,Qingwei Liao,Kaize Yin,Xingling Liu,Luca Cuomo,Silvio Bacci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出"memory delivery"（记忆传递）阶段，指检索到的记忆以何种形式送达模型，并在MemLens上证明其比提升检索更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态记忆研究聚焦于写什么、更新什么、检索什么，但忽略了"检索到答案之间"的一个阶段：检索到的记忆有多少、以何种形式到达模型。作者称其为delivery，并发现这里有更大改进空间。

Method: 通过MemLens上的受控分解实验：固定检索证据集，对比传递原始像素vs不传递，以及检索完美化的增益。提出DeliverMem，将delivery形式化为三个决策：保留原始模态、给每项可读身份、标注观看时间，并配检索端适配器。

Result: 固定证据下传像素比不传提升13.87分（8B骨干），完美检索仅提升2.31分。DeliverMem在全部骨干、四个上下文长度上领先最强已发布记忆智能体和DMV-Bench最强方法，且输入量仅为1/10至1/70。

Conclusion: delivery是当前记忆系统的主要瓶颈且随骨干增强而扩大。三个决策各自只在问题缺失对应信息时起作用，单一固定配置即可跨基准领先，无需训练或修改存储记录。

Abstract: Work on memory for multimodal agents optimizes what is written, updated and retrieved. Between retrieval and the answer, however, is a stage that multimodal memory evaluations do not isolate: what of the retrieved memory reaches the model, and in what form. We call it delivery, and a controlled decomposition on MemLens locates the remaining room there. With the retrieved evidence set exactly fixed, delivering the original pixels instead of withholding them raises accuracy by 13.87 points on an 8B backbone, whereas making retrieval perfect on those same messages improves it by 2.31. Delivery is the larger term on all three MemLens backbones and grows with backbone strength; retrieval grows too, without closing the gap. We propose DeliverMem, an instantiation of delivery as three decisions: keep the original modality, give each item a readable identity, and state when it was seen, with a retrieval-side adapter for the one property delivery cannot supply. Each is measured against a delivery-matched control that alters only its own variable. DeliverMem leads the strongest published memory agent on MemLens at all four context lengths, and beats DMV-Bench's own strongest method at every setting on both backbones. On MemLens it does this on a tenth to a seventieth of the input. Each decision helps only where the question lacks what it supplies, and is null elsewhere. A single fixed configuration nonetheless leads both benchmarks, without training any component or modifying the stored records. Project page: https://avalon-s.github.io/DeliverMem/

</details>


### [11] [LoCoVSR: Local Context Diffusion Posterior Sampling for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32742)
*Matan Ben Chorin,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LoCoVSR是一个基于像素空间扩散概率模型的视频超分框架，结合扩散后验采样与时空上下文学习，以移动平均方式在局部窗口内处理帧，无需光流估计，在VFHQ上取得与近期扩散方法相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频超分是不适定逆问题。现有方法依赖光流估计或潜在空间处理，前者复杂、后者会带来信息损失，且递归处理易导致误差累积并限制视频长度。

Method: 采用像素空间去噪扩散概率模型，将扩散后验采样与时空上下文学习结合并以移动平均形式运行；用相邻LR帧的局部窗口恢复中心帧，所有帧共享同一噪声轨迹，支持长视频、并行推理并避免误差累积。

Result: 在VFHQ人脸数据集上训练，实现准确、时序一致的高质量放大，结果与近期扩散基VSR方法相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: LoCoVSR提供了一种简单而有效的VSR方案，规避了光流估计与潜在空间信息损失问题，具备处理长视频的扩展性。

Abstract: Video super-resolution (VSR) is an ill-posed inverse problem that aims to reconstruct a high-resolution (HR) video from a noisy, low-resolution (LR) version of it. We present LoCoVSR, a diffusion-based VSR framework that leverages pixel-space denoising diffusion probabilistic models. LoCoVSR integrates the Diffusion Posterior Sampling technique with spatio-temporal context learning, operating in a moving-average form. A localized window of adjacent LR frames is used for recovering each center frame, while applying a shared noise trajectory across all frames. The localized windowing enables processing of long videos without length limitations, supports parallel inference, and prevents error accumulation that may occur in recursive processing. Unlike prior methods, LoCoVSR offers a simple yet very effective VSR solution, avoiding explicit optical flow estimation, or information loss caused by latent space processing. Trained on the VFHQ face dataset, LoCoVSR achieves accurate, temporally consistent and high-quality upscaling with competitive results against recent diffusion-based VSR approaches.

</details>


### [12] [PolyTopoBench: A Benchmark for Complex Vector Polygon Generation from Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32856)
*Zeping Liu,Ni Lao,Weiwei Sun,Gil Wolff,Yiqun Xie,Liang Zhao,Junfeng Jiao,Gengchen Mai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyTopoBench，一个面向遥感图像矢量多边形生成的统一评测框架，重点关注复杂多边形（带孔洞与多环）与外环/内环的联合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量多边形生成对自动驾驶、矢量地图构建等应用至关重要。早期"栅格掩码+后处理"难以端到端优化，易漏小目标和顶点不准；现有直接生成矢量多边形的方法多只处理简单外轮廓，难以表示或保持带孔洞复杂多边形的拓扑结构，且缺乏统一的复杂多边形评测基准。

Method: 构建统一评测框架PolyTopoBench，显式强调复杂多边形；同时评估外环与内环，覆盖建筑、道路、植被与无植被区域；在两个遥感数据集上对11种代表性方法进行基准测试，包括基于分割的多边形化流程、视觉基础模型基线和专用矢量多边形生成器。

Result: 实验显示现有方法通常能较好恢复简单外边界，但在带孔洞或多个环的多边形上性能显著下降，表明复杂多边形生成仍是未解决的挑战。

Conclusion: 复杂多边形生成的困难凸显了拓扑感知评测与模型设计的必要性；PolyTopoBench提供了统一基准，代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Vector polygon generation converts visual inputs, e.g., remote sensing (RS) images, into vectorized polygonal geometries, supporting applications such as autonomous driving, vector map construction, and remote sensing. Early pipelines predict raster masks and post-process them into polygons, which prevents end-to-end optimization and may miss small objects or introduce inaccurate vertices. Recent methods directly generate vector polygons, but most focus on simple exterior contours, while they either cannot represent complex polygons with holes or fail to preserve their topology. In this paper, we propose PolyTopoBench, a unified evaluation framework for vector polygon generation from RS images with explicit emphasis on complex polygons. PolyTopoBench evaluates both exterior and interior rings, and benchmarks 11 representative methods, including segmentation-based polygonization pipelines, vision foundation model baselines, and specialized vector polygon generators, on two RS-image datasets covering buildings, roads, vegetation, and unvegetated regions. Experiments show that existing methods often recover simple exterior boundaries but degrade substantially on polygons with holes or multiple rings. These results reveal complex polygon generation as an unresolved challenge and motivate topology-aware benchmarks and model designs. Code and data are available at https://github.com/seai-lab/PolyTopoBench.

</details>


### [13] [Rethinking the Fully Hyperbolic Vision Transformer in Polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32899)
*Ahmad Bdeir,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出极坐标下的全双曲Transformer，避免洛伦兹坐标数值不稳定，在视觉任务上超越欧氏和双曲基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洛伦兹Transformer在环境坐标中构建，洛伦兹内积的数值不稳定性导致大半径处误差增大，模型被迫限制半径，无法利用双曲空间大体积区域（这正是采用该几何的动机）。

Method: 重新用极坐标表述Transformer组件：1) 极坐标全连接层，分别映射方向与半径，使半径可学习而非由线性映射范数决定；2) 引入horospherical shifts作为相对位置编码，使query-key距离随token间隔对数增长；3) 将残差连接重构为平均半径洛伦兹boost。组合成全双曲Transformer。

Result: 在标准视觉任务上显著优于欧氏与双曲基线；在ImageNet上评估，并证明可用预训练权重泛化到其他数据集，类似欧氏模型。

Conclusion: 极坐标表述消除了环境坐标的数值抵消问题，使全双曲Transformer能充分利用双曲空间，性能与泛化能力媲美欧氏对应模型。

Abstract: Hyperbolic space can embed intrinsic hierarchies in data with low distortion due to the exponential growth of volume with distance from the origin. However, current Lorentz transformer blocks are formulated in ambient coordinates, where numerical errors increase at large radii due to instability in the Lorentzian inner product, leading many models to limit the radius to avoid this issue. This prevents us from utilizing the regions of hyperbolic space that motivate the geometry. To address this, we revisit the components of the transformer block in polar coordinates, where hyperbolic operations such as distance calculation and attention centroids can be computed without the numerical cancellation in their ambient-coordinate formulations. Specifically, we propose a polar fully connected layer that separately maps an embedding's direction and radius, allowing the radius of a feature to be learned rather than determined by the norm of a linear map. We additionally introduce horospherical shifts as relative positional encodings whose query-key distances grow logarithmically with the token gap. Finally, we reformulate the residual connection as average radius Lorentz boosts. Combining these components, we develop a fully hyperbolic transformer that substantially improves performance over Euclidean and hyperbolic baselines on standard vision tasks. We further evaluate our model on ImageNet and demonstrate its ability to generalize to other datasets using pre-trained weights, similar to Euclidean counterparts.

</details>


### [14] [dKFD: Phase-Structured Evidence Allocation for Fixed-Budget Localized Event Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33083)
*Aditya Bagri,Ashutosh Kumar,Chaitanya Lakhchaura,Avinash Anand,Zhengkui Wang,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文研究固定预算的稀疏视频证据选择，提出相位结构化可微选择器dKFD，在局部事件视频上显著优于全局Top-K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数稀疏选择器采用全局预算分配，让所有帧相互竞争，这对时间局部化事件是不良归纳偏置：有用证据分布在事件前上下文、事件本身和事件后果中。作者研究固定预算下的证据分配问题。

Method: 提出dKFD：在完整序列时序编码后，为事件前、事件、事件后三个阶段分别预留证据容量，并使用相位监督的可微选择器进行训练（相分离预算+相位监督）。

Result: 在DoTA视频异常识别上，K=12时全局Top-K帧AUC仅51.5±10.8；dKFD提升+30.97（p<0.01），识别有微小但显著增益，时序定位相当。消融显示相位监督是关键：去除后帧AUC降至41.1±12.0。VRU-Accident上跨VLM家族优于学习式全局Top-K；密集描述任务中均匀采样仍具竞争力。

Conclusion: 相位结构化分配是面向事件中心稀疏证据选择的有效可控固定预算方法，但并非通用视频摘要策略，存在边界条件。

Abstract: Sparse video understanding often requires selecting a small set of visual evidence under a fixed frame budget. Most sparse selectors allocate this budget globally, allowing all frames to compete with one another. For temporally localized events, this can be a poor inductive bias: useful evidence is often distributed across pre-event context, the event itself, and post-event consequences. We study fixed-budget evidence allocation for localized event videos and show that globally competitive Top-$K$ selectors can preserve recognition and grounding while producing unstable event evidence. On DoTA Video Anomaly Recognition, Global Top-$K$ obtains competitive recognition and temporal grounding, but low selector-event alignment at $K=12$ (Frame AUC $51.5 \pm 10.8$). We propose dKFD, a phase-structured differentiable selector that reserves evidence capacity across pre-event, event, and post-event phases after full-sequence temporal encoding. Under matched-budget multi-seed evaluation, dKFD improves Frame AUC by $+30.97$ over a matched Global Top-$K$ selector at $K=12$ ($p<0.01$), while yielding modest but statistically significant recognition gains and comparable temporal grounding. Mechanism ablations show that phase supervision is load-bearing: removing it reduces Frame AUC to $41.1 \pm 12.0$ even when phase-partitioned budgets are retained. Downstream diagnostics on VRU-Accident show consistent gains over learned Global Top-$K$ across VLM families, while dense captioning reveals a boundary condition where uniform sampling remains competitive. These results support phase-structured allocation as a controlled fixed-budget approach for event-centric sparse evidence selection, not as a universal video summarization strategy.

</details>


### [15] [Octree-based Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33100)
*Rungui Zhou,Chuanzhi Zhou,Yuk-Kit Hou,Peng-Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OctVideo用八叉树近似视频片段，递归划分时空体，平滑区粗、细节区细，配合Conv1D VAE与残差实现高效视频表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频视觉复杂度在时空上变化很大，但现有视频模型普遍使用均匀网格，造成计算冗余和不必要的开销。

Method: 把视频片段建模为八叉树，递归将时空体划分为8个子体；叶子存储局部RGB值与时空中梯度，并用轻量学习残差补充。重建时用Conv1D VAE将序列化单元映射到规则潜网格，并在解码中按需细化细节。

Result: 在K400上达到36.12 dB PSNR，38.2M参数，每片段189.4 GFLOPs；零样本泛化到DAVIS 2016，重建质量与最佳模型相当。在两个数据集上FLOPs最少，编解码最快；支持视频理解，少输入token即可从零训练取得有竞争力识别性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用视频信号本身的冗余并高效处理稀疏结构，OctVideo为视频提供了一种高效表示。

Abstract: Video models commonly use uniform grids even though visual complexity varies substantially across space and time. We introduce OctVideo, which approximates a video clip with an octree. This hierarchy recursively partitions a spatio-temporal volume into eight subvolumes, so that smooth regions remain coarse while detailed regions receive finer cells. Each leaf stores local RGB values and spatio-temporal gradients, supplemented by a lightweight learned residual. For reconstruction, a Conv1D VAE maps the serialized cells to a regular latent grid and selectively refines details during decoding. Our VAE achieves 36.12 dB PSNR with 38.2M parameters and 189.4 GFLOPs per clip on Kinetics-400 (K400). It also generalizes zero-shot to the high-resolution Densely Annotated VIdeo Segmentation (DAVIS) 2016 dataset with reconstruction quality comparable to the best evaluated models. On both datasets, it requires the fewest model FLOPs and achieves the fastest encoding and decoding among the evaluated models. OctVideo also supports video understanding, achieving competitive recognition performance with few input tokens when trained from scratch. By exploiting the redundancy already present in video signals and efficiently processing sparse structures, OctVideo provides an efficient representation for video.

</details>


### [16] [PARSEE-VAD: Efficient Training-Free Online Video Anomaly Detection via Proposition-Aware Reasoning and Streaming Evidence Escalation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33236)
*Ji Wang,Shuangqing Zhang,Guo-Sen Xie,Fang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PARSEE-VAD，一种免训练在线视频异常检测框架，将语义证据获取与分数状态演化分离，通过PAR提取命题证据、SEE传播紧凑分数状态，实现高效流式多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练在线VAD在冻结多模态语言模型下面临两个耦合挑战：因果与计算约束下提取当前窗口可靠语义，以及在不反复传输高维历史的情况下保持时间连续性。用文本编码历史会压缩视觉证据并引入语义偏差，而保留视觉历史则膨胀多模态上下文。论文提倡"当前优先"原则。

Method: PARSEE-VAD含两个模块：Proposition-Aware Reasoning（PAR）从当前因果窗口提取结构化命题证据，粗证据不足时条件激活更具体查询，并通过共享可复用因果视觉前缀减少冗余计算（选择性执行）；Streaming Evidence Escalation（SEE）将命题证据映射为紧凑的分数域事件状态，通过当前证据升级，仅传播有界状态以维持时间连续性。

Result: 在四个基准上，方法展现强免训练在线性能，选择性路由降低专家计算量，分数状态传播保持稀疏。

Conclusion: 结果支持流式多模态推理的"当前优先"原则：先解决当前语义，再仅用紧凑历史状态修补残余的连续性缺口。

Abstract: Training-free online video anomaly detection (VAD) with frozen multimodal language models faces two coupled challenges: extracting reliable current-window semantics under causal and computational constraints, and maintaining temporal continuity without repeatedly transmitting high-dimensional history. Encoding history through text can compress visual evidence and introduce semantic bias, whereas retaining visual history expands multimodal context. We introduce PARSEE-VAD, a two-module framework that separates semantic evidence acquisition from score-state evolution. Proposition-Aware Reasoning (PAR) extracts structured propositional evidence from the current causal window and conditionally activates more specific queries when coarse evidence warrants further refinement. By sharing a reusable causal visual prefix across queries, PAR reduces redundant computation through selective execution. Streaming Evidence Escalation (SEE) maps the acquired proposition evidence into a compact score-domain event state through current evidence escalation, then propagates only the resulting bounded state across decisions to support temporal continuity. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate strong training-free online performance while selective routing reduces specialist computation and score-state propagation remains sparse. These results support a current-first principle for streaming multimodal inference: resolve present semantics first, then use compact historical state only to repair residual continuity gaps.

</details>


### [17] [Printability-Constrained Adversarial Decals for Near-Nadir Aerial Perception: Measured Ink Gamuts, Nested Realism Constraints, and a Physical-World Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33513)
*Sandesh Shrestha,K. T. Yasas Mahima,Asanka G. Perera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究在优化阶段即引入打印机色域、面板尺寸与高度成像细节损失三项物理约束，比较均匀色彩立方与实测色域两种墨色集对航空感知对抗贴片的影响，发现色域效果依赖受害模型，且色域/色彩度并非性能差异的主因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空感知对抗贴片多以数字合成方式评估，打印环节被视为实现细节，而这对真实攻击有效性至关重要。作者希望在优化而非事后引入可打印性等物理约束，并厘清"可打印调色板色彩度和频率较低"这一常见解释是否成立。

Method: 在优化过程中施加三项物理约束：打印机可复现的颜色范围、车辆可用平板尺寸、从空中成像带来的细节损失。核心对比仅隔离墨色集：两贴片共享全部17项优化设置，仅颜色可选范围不同（均匀色彩立方 vs. 通过打印并扫描216色卡测得的色域），各用3个随机种子优化，并在13种受害条件下评估，所有成功率对照尺寸匹配的优化控制组。随后用另外11个贴片检验"色彩度/频率"解释，并做打印贴花的物理评估。

Result: 实测色域的效果依赖受害模型而非统一：在6个闭集分割模型中的3个上净攻击成功率上升，且这些模型的两种墨色集种子区间不相交（DeepLabv3-R101 +0.120，SegFormer-B0 +0.041）；色彩立方贴片在开放词汇分割器及4个检测器中的2个上更强，但检测器净增益均不超过+0.026。额外11个贴片否定了"可打印调色板色彩度/频率低"的解释：基数匹配后与立方同等色彩度的调色板攻击效果相同；基数从3到32无趋势；在基数、明度、色度匹配而仅色相分布不同的调色板间，成功率跨度为0.035至0.136。物理打印贴花评估未检测到迁移，将迁移率上界界定为0.133，不排除模拟值0.121。

Conclusion: 在优化中加入物理约束后，墨色集对攻击成功率的影响因受害模型而异，并非简单由色彩度或频率差异解释；色相分布等其他因素可能更重要。物理评估未检测到迁移，但置信上界仍兼容模拟结果，说明需要更大规模物理实验来确认真实可迁移性。

Abstract: Adversarial patches for aerial perception are typically evaluated as digital composites, with printing left as an implementation detail. This study imposes three physical constraints during optimization rather than after it: the color range a particular printer can reproduce, the size of the flat panel a vehicle offers, and the loss of fine detail incurred when the patch is imaged from altitude. The principal comparison isolates the ink set. Two patches share all seventeen recorded optimization settings and differ only in the colors available to them. One is constrained to a uniform color cube; the other to a gamut measured by printing and scanning a 216-patch chart. Each was optimized at three seeds and evaluated against thirteen victim conditions, with every rate reported against a size-matched optimized control. The effect of the measured gamut is victim-dependent rather than uniform. Net attack success rises on three of six closed-set segmentation victims, and for these the seed ranges of the two ink sets are disjoint: $+0.120$ on DeepLabv3-R101 and $+0.041$ on SegFormer-B0. The color-cube patch is consistently stronger on the open-vocabulary segmenter and on two of four detectors, though no detector exceeds a net of $+0.026$ under either ink set. The natural explanation is that a printable palette is simply less chromatic and lower in frequency than a digital one. Eleven further patches test this account and it does not hold. Once cardinality is matched, a palette as chromatic as the cube attacks equally well. Cardinality itself shows no trend from three inks to thirty-two. Palettes matched on cardinality, lightness and chroma, and differing only in hue placement, span $0.035$ to $0.136$. A physical evaluation with printed decals did not detect transfer; it bounds the transferred rate at $0.133$, which does not exclude the simulated value of $0.121$.

</details>


### [18] [Dexterous Tactile World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34286)
*Ziyao Zeng,Xiatao Sun,Hao Wang,Yueyang Pan,Zhengxiang Yu,Fengyu Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zhiwen Fan,Daniel Rakita*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DTWM将触觉信号融入预训练视频扩散Transformer，用于自我中心操作视频的未来帧预测，显著降低手部运动低估和感知误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作中决定事件走向的接触建立与释放等难以从视频观察，更易通过触摸感知，因此需要触觉增强的世界模型。

Method: 在双手手套触觉信号条件下，通过零初始化残差将触觉注入预训练视频扩散Transformer对应手部token位置，因果掩码防止预测帧访问未来信息。

Result: 相比视觉-only基线，手部运动低估从23%降至9%，手部区域感知误差降7.4%；预测后期提升约为早期4.1倍；优于其他视觉-触觉世界模型；仅用二值接触状态效果更差。

Conclusion: 触觉提升未来帧预测，力的幅度与空间位置均重要，力的变化过程可指示交互是否持续或改变，且训练时触觉有助于推理无触觉场景。

Abstract: World models for manipulation are typically trained from video, yet the events that determine how manipulation unfolds, such as making and releasing contact, are difficult to observe visually and are often easier to sense through touch. We present the Dexterous Tactile World Model (DTWM), a video world model for future-frame prediction of egocentric manipulation from both observed video and tactile signals from a glove worn on each hand. We condition a pretrained video diffusion transformer on each hand's tactile signal through a zero-initialized residual at the corresponding hand location in the video tokens, while a causal mask prevents predicted frames from accessing future information. Compared with a vision-only model matched in architecture, parameters, and training, DTWM reduces the underestimation of hand motion from 23% to 9%, while reducing the perceptual error in the hand region by 7.4% across three training runs per model. The benefit also increases over the prediction horizon, with the improvement in the later predicted chunks being about 4.1x larger than in the first. DTWM also outperforms other visual-tactile world models under the same setting, and training with touch improves future-frame prediction even when no touch is available at inference. Ablations show that the model benefits from both the magnitude and spatial location of force: replacing the tactile signal with binary contact states, either per hand or per location, increases prediction error. The observed course of the force indicates whether the interaction will persist or change.

</details>


### [19] [ECHO: Event-Augmented Context with Hindsight and Outlook for Wrist-Only Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34893)
*Xinyue Wang,Yicheng Jiang,Zesen Gan,Junhao He,Jiaxu Wang,Junhao Li,Jingtao Zhang,Tianlun He,Jianan Wang,Isabel Guan,Qiming Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECHO是一种仅用腕部事件相机的潜在世界动作模型，通过hindsight与outlook模块提供时空上下文，在极端曝光下显著优于RGB及RGB+事件基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于RGB相机的操作策略在极端曝光下观测退化；事件相机虽具高动态范围，但其观测强烈依赖相机放置——固定相机遗漏静态场景内容，腕部相机运动又使已访问区域脱离视野，存在时空局限。

Method: 提出ECHO（事件增强上下文，含回溯与外瞻），仅用腕部事件编码为紧凑运动表示。利用预训练事件编码器解释帧间视觉特征变化；hindsight模块以过往事件流保留夹爪轨迹作为可寻址的离屏上下文；outlook模块引入可学习事件前瞻查询，监督其预测未来动作的事件窗口。

Result: 在仅腕部RLBench任务上，正常光照下较RGB和RGB+事件基线分别高20.6和12.0个百分点，严重曝光下降下高14.6和11.3个百分点，并超过使用第三人称相机的RGB参考。真实世界腕部事件相机实验在多种任务与正常、极暗光照下均优于基线。

Conclusion: ECHO通过结合回溯与外瞻的事件上下文，有效克服了腕部事件相机的时空局限，在极端光照下实现鲁棒的操控策略。

Abstract: Learning-based manipulation policies relying on RGB cameras often suffer from degraded observations under extreme exposure. Event cameras mitigate this degradation by asynchronously detecting pixel-level intensity changes to offer a high dynamic range. However, their observations heavily depend on camera placement, as fixed cameras miss static scene content while wrist-mounted camera motion causes previously visited regions to leave the field of view. To address these spatial-temporal limitations, we present ECHO (Event-augmented Context with Hindsight and Outlook), a wrist-only latent world action model that encodes wrist events into compact motion representations to provide temporal and spatial context for policy reasoning. Specifically, ECHO utilizes a pretrained event encoder to explain visual-feature changes between frames. Its hindsight module preserves the gripper trajectory with past event stream as addressable off-camera context. Concurrently, the outlook module introduces learnable event foresight queries supervised to anticipate the event window for future actions, enabling the policy to predict upcoming scene changes. Evaluated on wrist-only RLBench tasks, ECHO outperforms RGB and RGB+event baselines by 20.6 and 12.0 percentage points under normal lighting, and by 14.6 and 11.3 points under severe exposure drops, respectively, while also surpassing RGB references using a third-person camera. Real-world experiments with a wrist-mounted event camera validate that ECHO outperforms RGB-only and RGB+event baselines across multiple tasks under both nominal and severely dark lighting. Project page is at https://echo-wam.github.io/.

</details>


### [20] [SpatialSpeak: QA-Native Reconstruction with Local and Global Context for Spatial Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33616)
*Yang Cao,Jiaxin Zhang,Dave Zhenyu Chen,Yingji Zhong,Ruiyuan Gao,Lanqing Hong,Dan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialSpeak两阶段框架，先通过QA式多视图重建预训练学习局部几何与全局场景上下文，再进行空间思维链学习，显著提升多视图空间推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可借助几何先验进行多视图空间推理，但仅答案训练无法直接监督中间几何估计及其在推导定量空间答案中的作用，需探索更有效的空间思维链监督方式。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I为QA-Native重建预训练(QA-RP)，结合标记点3D查询(局部几何)与物体中心查询(全局场景)，均以文本问答形式统一自回归接口；Stage II为空间思维链与视觉补偿(CoT-VC)，训练模型表达问题相关几何估计并据此推导答案，辅以可靠性评估与视觉补偿精炼。

Result: 在ReVSI上QA-RP将CoT-VC增益从2.6提升至6.9分；消融表明局部与全局重建监督均有增益。在ReVSI、VSI-Bench、SPAR-Bench上达SOTA，ReVSI得分62.8，超最强基线8.7分。

Conclusion: 先联合学习局部几何与全局场景上下文可增强空间思维链监督效果，SpatialSpeak在多视图空间推理任务上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can benefit from geometric priors for multi-view spatial reasoning, yet answer-only training does not directly supervise the intermediate geometric estimates and their use in deriving quantitative spatial answers. We hypothesize that spatial chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision becomes more effective when the VLM first jointly learns complementary local geometry and global scene context through multi-view reconstruction. We introduce SpatialSpeak, a two-stage framework that connects QA-native reconstruction pretraining with spatial CoT learning. In Stage I, QA-Native Reconstruction Pretraining (QA-RP) combines marked-point 3D queries for fine-grained local geometry with object-center queries for global scene context across views. Both tasks are formulated as text-based question answering, allowing geometric estimation and subsequent reasoning to share the same autoregressive output interface. In Stage II, spatial CoT with Visual Compensation (CoT-VC) trains the model to express question-relevant geometric estimates and use them to derive answers, with reliability assessment and visual compensation supporting answer refinement when needed. On ReVSI, QA-RP increases the gain from CoT-VC from 2.6 to 6.9 points, and ablations show that both local and global reconstruction supervision are beneficial. SpatialSpeak achieves state-of-the-art results on ReVSI, VSI-Bench, and SPAR-Bench, with a ReVSI score of 62.8 that exceeds the strongest compared baseline by 8.7 points.

</details>


### [21] [StoryEngine: A State-Grounded Agentic Framework for Video Storytelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33627)
*Yingrui Wang,Zeqing Wang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StoryEngine——一个以状态为基的智能体视频叙事框架，通过分离语义计划与视觉观测，维护结构化实体/状态表示并传播事件变化，实现连贯一致的长视频故事生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体多镜头视频生成依赖文本镜头计划或已生成像素，缺乏显式机制传播故事事件的后果并维护跨镜头的视频世界状态，导致视觉细节重建不准、视觉漂移跨镜头传播，破坏叙事连贯性与视觉一致性。

Method: StoryEngine分离权威语义计划与不可靠视觉观测：(1)维护实体放置与故事相关状态的结构化表示，传播事件引发的变化以定义每镜头的起始/结束状态；(2)为反复出现的实体与环境构建规范参考，将状态与视觉约束编译为可执行渲染计划；(3)引入有界评估引导的修复循环，纠正局部状态不一致。

Result: 构建了覆盖多样场景与视觉风格的长篇叙事基准，评估故事情节质量、叙事连贯性与视觉一致性；实验表明StoryEngine在所有评估维度上均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 以状态为基的智能体框架能有效保持因果故事进展、防止局部视觉错误跨镜头传播，验证了其在连贯一致视频叙事上的有效性。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in agentic multi-shot video generation, producing coherent and consistent long-form stories remains challenging. Existing agentic pipelines typically rely on textual shot plans or previously generated pixels, yet lack an explicit mechanism for propagating the consequences of story events and maintaining the video world state across shots. As a result, missing visual details may be reconstructed inaccurately, while visual drift may propagate across subsequent shots, undermining both narrative coherence and visual consistency. To address these challenges, we propose StoryEngine, a state-grounded agentic framework for video storytelling. StoryEngine establishes a separation between authoritative semantic plans and unreliable visual observations. Specifically, StoryEngine maintains a structured representation of entity placement and story-relevant states, and propagates event-induced changes to define the intended start and end states of each shot. To visually realize these states, StoryEngine constructs canonical references for recurring entities and environments, and compiles state and visual constraints into executable render plans. Meanwhile, to realize these states correctly, a bounded evaluation-guided repair loop further corrects local state inconsistencies. Together, these mechanisms preserve causal story progression and prevent local visual errors from propagating across shots. To comprehensively evaluate long-form storytelling, we construct a benchmark across diverse scenarios and visual styles, with metrics assessing storytelling quality, narrative coherence, and visual consistency. Experimental results demonstrate that StoryEngine consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all evaluation dimensions, validating its effectiveness for coherent and consistent video storytelling.

</details>


### [22] [Video, Ergo Genero: Unifying Video Tasks via Spatiotemporal Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33935)
*Chia-Hsiang Kao,Belinda Zeng,Bharath Hariharan,Menglin Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ViGeo 框架，将视觉类比（visual in-context learning）扩展到视频领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型适配新任务通常依赖专门的数据整理与微调；视觉类比虽可免训练指定任务，但局限于图像领域。

Method: ViGeo 通过“时空画布补全”（spatiotemporal canvas completion）实现视频领域的上下文学习。

Result: 在多样化任务分类体系和严格训练-测试划分下，ViGeo 可泛化到未见过的视频操作及零样本模态（如事件相机）。

Conclusion: 论文发现“任务内化”现象：与预训练任务相关的查询格式会覆盖示范，少量任务无关数据可消除该捷径，表明需将提示格式与任务身份解耦。

Abstract: Adapting video models to new tasks typically requires dedicated data curation and fine-tuning. While visual analogy provides a training-free alternative by specifying tasks in-context, it remains restricted to the image domain. To explore whether analogy-based methods can unify diverse video tasks and generalize to out-of-distribution scenarios, we introduce ViGeo, a framework that extends visual in-context learning to the video domain via spatiotemporal canvas completion. Evaluated on a diverse task taxonomy with a strict train-test split, ViGeo generalizes to unseen video manipulations and zero-shot modalities (e.g., event cameras). Finally, we identify task internalization, where a query format associated with a pretrained task overrides the demonstration, and show that this shortcut can be removed with a small amount of task-unrelated data, highlighting the need to decorrelate prompt format from task identity.

</details>


### [23] [UnfoldCRF: Structured Mask Refinement with Image-Conditioned Latent Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33996)
*Chunming He,Rihan Zhang,Lei Xu,Guanyi Qin,Chengyu Fang,Longxiang Tang,Fengyang Xiao,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UnfoldCRF将掩码细化建模为CRF推断，通过展开阻尼均值场更新实现，验证其结构（而非额外容量）带来分割精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已学习的掩码细化器虽提升分割精度，但难以区分提升来自显式结构还是额外容量，也难以判断掩码生成器或错误分布变化时是否仍有效。

Method: 将细化视为像素标签与潜在区域变量上的条件随机场推断；能量包含校正的一元项、学习的局部成对交互、图像条件潜在区域一致性项（含null状态），并展开阻尼均值场更新进行推断。与同输入、同参数预算、同阶段数和监督的循环黑箱细化器对比。

Result: COD10K上比最强匹配对照高1.0 Fωβ，四项COD指标均提升，变差图像比例由11.7%降至8.5%；五次数据集train-once协议下2.6M参数变体平均ΔIoU增益4.2（对照2.0）；基于冻结DINOv2特征的变体以约1/7参数匹配最强基础模型细化器；未见掩码生成器上增益2.0 Fωβ（对照0.6）。消融显示成对消息主要修边界，区域消息主要修非边界错误。

Conclusion: 结构化的展开CRF细化优于同等容量的黑箱循环细化器，在多个数据集、掩码源和未见生成器上均稳健有效，且参数高效。

Abstract: Learned mask refiners improve segmentation accuracy, but it is hard to tell how much of the improvement comes from explicit structure rather than from extra capacity, and whether it holds up when the mask generator or its error distribution changes. UnfoldCRF treats refinement as inference in a conditional random field over pixel labels and latent region variables. Its energy has a corrected unary term, learned local pairwise interactions, and image-conditioned latent-region consistency, with a null state that lets a region with weak label agreement withdraw from the consistency term; inference unrolls damped mean-field updates on this one energy. To isolate the effect of structure, we compare against recurrent black-box refiners that read the same inputs and receive the same parameter budget, stage count, and supervision. On COD10K, UnfoldCRF beats the strongest matched control by 1.0 $F^ω_β$ point, improves all four COD metrics, and lowers the fraction of images made worse from 11.7\% to 8.5\%. Under a train-once protocol over five datasets and several mask sources, the 2.6M-parameter variant gains 4.2 mean $Δ$IoU against 2.0 for its control, and a variant built on frozen DINOv2 features matches the strongest foundation-model refiner with about a seventh of its resident parameters while staying ahead of its own control. On mask generators never seen in training, the gain is 2.0 $F^ω_β$ points against 0.6 for the control. Zeroing individual messages shows where the corrections come from: the pairwise messages mostly fix boundaries, the region messages mostly fix non-boundary errors. Code and supporting materials will be publicly released.

</details>


### [24] [SpatialSkill: Self-Evolving Skills for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34124)
*Ruifan Zuo,Guocheng Hu,Wanshui Gan,Junyi Wang,Xiang Lei,Tian Gan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialSkill：无需更新权重的框架，让冻结的视觉语言模型从离线轨迹中积累显式自然语言推理技能，在CityCube上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视角空间推理对VLM仍困难。现有方法通过更新模型权重改进空间推理，但知识隐式且绑定特定骨干网络。

Method: 提出SpatialSkill，无权重更新。通过视觉接地与可执行性检查筛选候选技能，约束手动演化防止退化，并按空间推理类别路由技能以减少负迁移。

Result: 在CityCube上，四种冻结执行器均获一致增益；配备SpatialSkill的9B执行器超越最强闭源参考。

Conclusion: 技能存于版本化自然语言手册，推理策略显式可审计，无需修改模型参数。

Abstract: Cross-view spatial reasoning requires a model to align different viewpoints into a coherent spatial representation, yet this ability remains challenging for vision-language models despite being natural to humans. Existing methods typically improve spatial reasoning by updating model weights, which keeps the acquired knowledge implicit and tied to a specific backbone. We propose \textit{SpatialSkill}, a weight-update-free framework that enables a frozen vision-language model to accumulate explicit natural-language reasoning skills from offline trajectories. Unlike symbolic tasks, perceptual skills cannot be reliably verified simply by executing them: a plausible spatial rule may lack visual support or require transformations that the frozen model cannot perform. SpatialSkill therefore admits candidate skills only after visual-grounding and executability checks, constrains manual evolution to prevent harmful regressions, and routes skills by spatial-reasoning category to reduce negative transfer. On CityCube, across four frozen executors, SpatialSkill yields consistent gains, and a 9B executor equipped with SpatialSkill surpasses the strongest closed-source reference in our evaluation. The skills are stored in a versioned natural-language manual, making the reasoning strategies explicit and auditable without modifying model parameters. Code at https://github.com/vindahi/SpatialSkill.

</details>


### [25] [E-WAVE: Event-based Continuous Optical Flow via Warping-Aligned Visual Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34346)
*Jiale Wu,Xiaoyang Bai,Haoming Yu,Yiwei Chen,Yifan Peng,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出E-WAVE，一个无相关体的高时间分辨率事件相机光流估计框架，用全局注意力与贝塞尔曲线轨迹引导形变，实现任意时间戳查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR/AR中快速头、手、物体运动需要连续高时间分辨率光流，但现有帧方法受时间分辨率与计算成本权衡限制；事件相机虽高时间分辨率、低能耗，但现有事件方法依赖相关体（correlation volume）捕捉成对体素对应，内存与计算开销大。

Method: E-WAVE摒弃全对相关体，采用全局注意力机制建模长程特征依赖，并用贝塞尔曲线进行轨迹引导的特征形变；通过迭代更新预测轨迹，可在任意时间戳查询而无需重复推理。

Result: 在MultiFlow与DSEC-Flow上，相比SOTA基线轨迹误差降低25%，端点光流精度相当；在头戴原型自采数据上的评估验证其在真实挑战场景下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: E-WAVE以无相关体设计高效实现高时间分辨率事件光流估计，兼顾精度与鲁棒性，适用于VR/AR动态感知。

Abstract: Temporally dense optical flow is essential for dynamic perception in immersive VR/AR systems, where rapid head, hand, and object motion must be continuously captured and tracked. Existing frame-based optical flow estimation methods are constrained by the tradeoff between temporal resolution and computational cost; while event cameras, with their high temporal resolution and energy efficiency, serve as a natural solution to the dilemma. However, event-based approaches commonly rely on correlation volumes to capture pairwise voxel correspondences, which incur substantial memory and computation overhead. We present E-WAVE, a correlation-free framework for high-temporal-resolution (HTR) optical flow estimation from event streams. Instead of constructing all-pairs correlation volumes, E-WAVE employs global attention mechanism to model long-range feature dependencies and performs trajectory guided feature warping using Bézier curve. Through iterative updates, it predicts trajectories that allow for querying at arbitrary timestamps without repeated inference. Experiments on MultiFlow and DSEC-Flow demonstrate a 25% lower trajectory error and comparable endpoint flow estimation accuracy relative to state-of-the art baselines. Additional evaluations on self-captured data using a head-mounted prototype validate that E-WAVE remains robust under challenging real-world conditions.

</details>


### [26] [From Static to Dynamic: On-Policy Distillation from Image to Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34371)
*Bingqing Jiang,Li Luo,Zichao Yu,Yujin Han,Zhaolong Su,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 MILD 框架，将专用图像专家的能力蒸馏到视频扩散模型，同时保持预训练视频动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频专家作为教师代价高（数据、训练、延迟大），而图像专家更廉价易得，尤其适合美学、OCR 等时间无关能力；但图像与视频隐空间异质，且图像专家缺乏跨帧运动监督，直接蒸馏会破坏时间一致性。

Method: 采用可学习线性连接器，对齐学生隐状态与预测更新和图像专家，实现跨异质隐空间的监督迁移；将图像引导的修正约束在预训练学生预测附近以保留视频动态；引入基于光流的运动奖励提升运动质量与时间一致性。

Result: 在多种图像专家和多个视频学生骨干上，方法稳定优于视频教师 OPD 基线；进一步实验显示可跨连接器设计与异质架构有效迁移。

Conclusion: 图像到视频蒸馏是借助图像生成生态多样且不断演进能力来提升视频生成的有效途径。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) specializes pretrained video diffusion models through teacher supervision along the student's own generation trajectory. Although large video models are natural teachers, developing specialized video experts can require costly video data and training, while querying them incurs substantially higher latency than querying image experts. More readily available and cheaper to query, image experts offer a cost-effective alternative, particularly for largely temporal-agnostic capabilities such as aesthetics and OCR that admit frame-level supervision. However, heterogeneous image and video latent spaces prevent direct supervision of intermediate student states, while image experts lack cross-frame motion supervision, making temporal consistency vulnerable to frame-level improvements. In this paper, we propose MILD, a Motion-Preserving Image-to-Video Latent Distillation framework that transfers specialized image expertise while preserving pretrained video dynamics. MILD uses a learnable linear connector that aligns student latent states and predicted updates with those of image experts, enabling supervision transfer across heterogeneous latent spaces. We further constrain image-guided corrections around the pretrained student's predictions to preserve video dynamics and incorporate an optical-flow-based motion reward to improve motion quality and temporal consistency. Across specialized image experts and multiple video-student backbones, our method consistently outperforms video-teacher OPD baselines, with further studies demonstrating effective transfer across connector designs and heterogeneous architectures. These results establish image-to-video distillation as an effective route to improving video generation by drawing on the diverse and evolving capabilities of the image-generation ecosystem.

</details>


### [27] [HPMD: A Historical Persian Manuscript Dataset for Word Spotting with Line-Level Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34490)
*Saeid Firouzi Daghigh,Majid Iranpour Mobarakeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发布了一个历史波斯语手写数据集（223页/3678行/37631词），并提出仅用行级标注、却能返回词级定位的word spotting基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史波斯语手稿已数字化，但检索仍依赖人工。该任务受限于两点：(1) 缺乏公开的历史波斯语手写数据集，OpenITI MAKHZAN中波斯语部分很少；(2) word spotting模型通常需要昂贵的词级边界框标注。

Method: 提出数据集，包含诗歌与散文类历史波斯语书籍，提供区域、行、文本三级标注。基线方法：微调行检测器定位文本行；微调基于CTC的CRNN识别器为每行生成逐帧字符后验矩阵；不逐帧解码最可能字符，而是将查询直接与该矩阵打分，从而缓解波斯语中形近字符（如be与pe）的硬错误；帧对齐同时给出词在行内的水平位置。

Result: 测试集上：微调行检测器F1为0.892；基于后验的检索相较解码文本上的精确匹配，word spotting F1从0.487提升到0.558（阈值在验证集上选取）。额外使用oracle词边界的PHOC属性嵌入基线F1为0.449，低于所提方法。此外还报告了数据集的分布分析、检索错误分类体系及分条件性能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了历史波斯语手写word spotting的公开数据资源，并证明仅需行级标注、直接在CTC后验上评分的检索方法优于需词级边界的属性嵌入基线。

Abstract: Large collections of historical Persian manuscripts have been digitized, but searching them is still slow and mostly manual. Historians usually want to find where a specific name, date, event, or topic appears, which is a word spotting problem. Progress on this task is limited by two things. First, there is almost no public dataset of historical Persian handwriting; the only notable resource, OpenITI MAKHZAN, contains a relatively small Persian portion. Second, word spotting models usually need word-level bounding boxes, which are very expensive to annotate. In this paper we introduce a new dataset of 223 pages, 3,678 lines, 37,631 words, and 130,630 characters, collected from diverse historical Persian books of poetry and prose and annotated at the region, line, and text level. We also propose a baseline that is trained only with line-level annotations but returns word-level locations. A fine-tuned line detector finds text lines, and a fine-tuned CRNN recognizer trained with CTC produces a frame-by-character posterior matrix for each line. Instead of decoding the most probable character at each frame, the query is scored directly against this matrix, so visually similar characters in Persian such as be and pe no longer cause hard failures. The frame alignment also gives the horizontal position of the word inside the line. On the test set, the fine-tuned line detector reaches an F1 of 0.892, and posterior-based search raises the word spotting F1 from 0.487 to 0.558 compared with exact matching on the decoded text, with the decision threshold selected on a held-out validation set. A PHOC attribute-embedding baseline that additionally receives oracle word boundaries at test time reaches an F1 of 0.449, below the proposed method. We also report a distributional analysis of the dataset, a taxonomy of retrieval errors, and a per-conditionbreakdown of performance.

</details>


### [28] [Can Attack Difficulty Be Characterized Before Optimization? A Study of Pre-optimization Difficulty in Person-Vanishing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34501)
*Jingyao Xu,Dongdong Wang,Siyang Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"优化前攻击难度"概念，并设计Quad-CLEVER估计器，在优化前预测不同图像的行人消失攻击难度，并据此实现难度感知的自适应攻击框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对抗攻击研究从优化视角出发，攻击难度被视为优化后观察到的结果，无法在优化前刻画不同输入的内在难度差异。本文探究能否在优化开始前预测攻击的相对难度。

Method: 提出Quad-CLEVER：基于沿最攻击相关方向的局部行人消失margin的二次近似，构建高效的几何估计器，在优化前估计潜在攻击难度。进而提出难度感知攻击框架，按估计难度在固定预算下自适应分配优化预算。

Result: 多种攻击算法实验表明Quad-CLEVER与观测优化成本持续相关。BDD100K上图像级攻击成功率提升最高5.78%，平均优化成本减少最多11.42次迭代；EventPed上节省2.25次迭代且攻击性能相当。

Conclusion: 攻击难度在优化前具有可预测结构，可被有意义地估计，并可用于构建更计算高效的对抗攻击。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks against object detectors are traditionally studied from an optimization perspective, where attack difficulty is regarded as an outcome observed only after adversarial optimization. This raises a fundamental question: \emph{can the relative attack difficulty of different inputs be characterized before optimization begins?} In this paper, we investigate this question for person-vanishing attacks by introducing the concept of pre-optimization attack difficulty, which captures intrinsic differences in optimization effort across input images. To estimate this latent difficulty before optimization, we propose Quad-CLEVER, an efficient geometry-based estimator derived from a quadratic approximation of the local person-vanishing margin along the most attack-relevant direction. Extensive experiments across multiple attack algorithms demonstrate that Quad-CLEVER consistently correlates with the observed optimization cost, providing empirical evidence that attack difficulty exhibits a predictable pre-optimization structure. Building upon this finding, we further propose a difficulty-aware attack framework that leverages the estimated difficulty to adaptively allocate optimization budgets for a base attack under a fixed computational budget. On BDD100K, the proposed framework improves the image-level attack success rate by up to 5.78$\%$ while reducing the average optimization cost by up to 11.42 iterations. On the more challenging EventPed dataset, it saves 2.25 optimization iterations while maintaining comparable attack performance. These results demonstrate that attack difficulty can be meaningfully estimated before optimization and that exploiting such estimates enables more computationally efficient adversarial attacks.

</details>


### [29] [Counterfactual Attention Policy Distillation for Temporal Video Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34581)
*Shaobo Ju,Haiyang Yu,Xuecheng Wu,Qiong Wu,Jiacong Wang,Fan Shi,Peng Jun,Yiyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出反事实注意力策略蒸馏(CAPD)，通过掩码时间组测量反事实影响来校准教师注意力，提升MLLM时序视频定位能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中重复动作与视觉相似上下文，使MLLM时序视频定位困难。现有时序蒸馏(next-token)难以识别支撑每个时间戳的特定视频片段。

Method: 基于On-policy distillation(OPD)，CAPD通过掩码每个时间组来测量教师输出分布的变化，用得到的反事实影响校准教师注意力，并加权token级蒸馏，引导学生学习影响边界预测的时序证据。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct上仅用2500样本训练1个epoch。TimeLens上相比GRPO平均召回率提升12.0%，同时保持通用视频理解，准确率与基座模型相当。

Conclusion: CAPD为MLLM时序视频定位提供有效训练范式，将反事实注意力校准融入策略蒸馏，兼顾任务性能与通用理解力。

Abstract: Temporal video grounding is a key capability of advanced \emph{Multimodal Large Language Models} (MLLMs) for the thorough understanding of video events, which is however often limited by repeated actions and visually similar contexts in long videos. In this paper, we study this issue from the perspective of \emph{On-policy distillation} (OPD) and propose a new training regime for MLLMs termed \emph{Counterfactual Attention Policy Distillation} (CAPD). In particular, OPD is a viable solution for MLLMs via providing dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. But its next-token based teacher-student distillation is hard to identify the specific video segments supporting each predicted timestamp, which is critical for temporal grounding. In this case, CAPD measures how masking each temporal group changes the teacher's output distribution. The resulting counterfactual influence calibrates the teacher's attention and weights token-level distillation, allowing the student to learn the temporal evidence that affects boundary prediction. To validate CAPD, we trained it on Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct using only 2,500 samples for one epoch, and evaluated it on the TimeLens and multiple general video benchmarks. Experimental results show that CAPD improves average recall by 12.0\% relative to GRPO on TimeLens while preserving general video understanding, achieving comparable accuracy to the base model.

</details>


### [30] [Summarize Before Grounding: Query-Guided Chunk Condensation for Long-Video Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34598)
*Nanxing Hu,Xiaoyue Duan,Qiwei Yan,Kailin Lyu,Jinchao Zhang,Guoliang Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出"先总结后定位"框架SumGround，通过查询引导的分块浓缩与强化学习，实现长视频时序定位，性能超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频含大量冗余信息干扰LVLMs定位查询相关证据；稠密采样内存开销过大，而现有RLVR方法用稀疏采样虽可行但会遗漏关键证据。

Method: 将视频分块并做两级浓缩：1）查询引导的潜摘要，用查询引导提示的KV状态把冗余视觉token压缩为紧凑的查询相关块摘要；2）关联摘要检索，对最可能含事件区间的块摘要排序选择。两者均由RLVR驱动，并用长度感知梯度门控模块选择性停止向视觉token回传梯度以降低内存。

Result: 在多个下游数据集上优于此前SOTA方法，在长视频上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 查询引导的块浓缩能有效挖掘证据、降低冗余，SumGround为长视频时序定位提供高效方案。

Abstract: Video temporal grounding (VTG) aims to localize the video interval corresponding to a language query. Recent large vision-language models (LVLMs) show great potential in solving such a multi-modal reasoning task. However, long videos often contain large amounts of redundant information that disturbs LVLMs to mine query-relevant evidence. Instead of dense frame sampling which incurs prohibitive training memory, previous reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) works typically utilize sparse sampling, which makes training feasible but may miss critical evidence. In this paper, we propose a ``summarize before grounding'' framework (named ``SumGround'') for long-video temporal grounding. The key of SumGround is to perform query-guided chunk condensation to aggregate and retrieve query-relevant evidence. Specifically, we split the video into several chunks and perform two-level chunk condensation. First, we introduce query-guided latent summaries, which is represented as KV states of query-guided prompts, to compress redundant visual tokens into compact query-relevant chunk summaries. Furthermore, we design an associative summary retrieval scheme to rank and select chunk summaries that are most likely to contain the event interval. Both query-guided latent summary and associative summary retrieval schemes are enabled by RLVR. To reduce memory consumption, we propose a length-aware gradient gating module to selectively stop gradient back-propagated to visual tokens. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SumGround performs favorably against previous state-of-the-art methods across multiple downstream datasets, with remarkable gains on long videos.

</details>


### [31] [CapField-OPD: Learning Continuous Capability Fields via Joint-Anchored Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation for Flow Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34658)
*Pengyang Ling,Yujie Zhou,Jiazi Bu,Yibin Wang,Xiaoxiao Ma,Yi Jin,Huaian Chen,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 CapField-OPD 框架，通过显式能力坐标将多教师整合为连续能力场，实现与提示内容解耦的能力调用与连续控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于语义类别路由单个教师的多教师在线蒸馏(OPD)将期望能力与提示内容隐式绑定，导致能力调用易受提示扰动影响，且无法在推理时显式调节能力强度。

Method: 以教师模型为锚点构建连续能力场，由能力坐标决定教师输出的组合方式，每个能力配置获得唯一监督目标；并在小规模校准集上对学到的能力场进行剖析，以平均奖励最高的坐标作为推荐默认值，频繁最优的坐标构成候选集用于测试时缩放。

Result: 在组合生成、文本渲染与视觉美感任务上，CapField-OPD 将多个专家教师整合为单一学生并保持或超越其性能，在语义保持的提示扰动下可靠调用目标能力，并支持连续能力控制与基于坐标的测试时缩放。

Conclusion: 通过显式能力坐标解耦能力与提示语义，CapField-OPD 实现了更鲁棒、可控的多教师能力整合，为基于流的生成模型提供连续能力控制与测试时缩放能力。

Abstract: Reward-specialized post-training produces strong experts for flow-based generative models, while multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD) consolidates their capabilities into a single student. Existing methods, however, route each prompt to a single teacher according to its semantic category, implicitly binding the desired capability to prompt content. This coupling makes capability invocation vulnerable to prompt perturbations and prevents users from explicitly adjusting the strength of the desired capability at inference time. In this work, we introduce CapField-OPD, an OPD framework that integrates multiple teachers into a continuous capability field through explicit capability coordinates. We use teacher models as anchors to construct this field, with the coordinates determining how their outputs are combined. Each capability configuration thus receives a unique supervision target, and capability control no longer depends on prompt semantics. Since the training anchors may not be optimal at inference time, we further profile the learned field on a small calibration set. The coordinate with the highest mean reward serves as the recommended default, while coordinates that are frequently optimal offer a promising candidate set for test-time scaling. Extensive experiments on compositional generation, text rendering, and visual aesthetics demonstrate that CapField-OPD consolidates multiple specialized teachers into a single student while preserving or surpassing their performance, reliably invokes the desired capabilities under semantics-preserving prompt variations, and supports continuous capability control and coordinate-based test-time scaling.

</details>


### [32] [SurgGMF: Fully Causal Gaussian Motion Forecasting for Anticipatory Surgical Scene Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34733)
*Jingqian Sun,Yichao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgGMF：一种全因果高斯运动预测框架，实现前瞻性手术场景渲染，从历史高斯运动场预测未来高斯状态（位置/尺度/旋转残差），在12个视频片段上优于经典动力学基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经渲染方法主要聚焦于已观测帧的重建，而非预测未来场景状态；而动态手术场景建模对机器人感知、仿真与决策支持至关重要，因此需要面向未来的预测能力。

Method: 不直接预测未来RGB图像，而是从历史高斯运动场预测未来高斯运动状态（位置、尺度、旋转残差X/S/R）。提出"全因果-末帧"渲染协议，在未来高斯状态渲染时禁止访问目标帧高斯属性，同时保持因果外观传播，以防止目标泄漏。

Result: 在12个EndoNeRF和StereoMIS视频片段上，使用神经时序学习器与经典动力学基线在统一预测协议下评估。学习式高斯运动预测在渲染空间持续优于经典动力学基线；延迟分析显示精度-效率权衡：TKAN精度最高，GRU和LSTM模块级延迟更优。

Conclusion: SurgGMF为因果高斯运动预测提供了可复现框架，推动手术高斯表示从回溯式重建迈向预测式场景建模。

Abstract: Dynamic surgical scene modeling is essential for robotic perception, simulation, and decision support. Although existing neural rendering methods enable efficient reconstruction and rendering of deformable surgical scenes, they remain primarily focused on observed-frame reconstruction rather than forecasting future scene states. To this end, we present SurgGMF, a fully causal Gaussian motion forecasting framework for anticipatory surgical scene rendering. Rather than predicting future RGB images directly, SurgGMF forecasts future Gaussian motion states represented by position, scale, and rotation residuals (X/S/R) from historical Gaussian motion fields. To prevent target leakage, we introduce a full-causal-last rendering protocol, where future Gaussian states are rendered without accessing target-frame Gaussian attributes while preserving causal appearance propagation. We evaluate SurgGMF on 12 EndoNeRF and StereoMIS video slices using neural temporal learners and classical dynamics baselines under a unified forecasting protocol. Learned Gaussian motion forecasting consistently outperforms classical dynamics baselines in render space, demonstrating gains beyond hand-crafted state extrapolation. Latency analysis further reveals an accuracy--efficiency trade-off: under the current implementations, TKAN achieves the highest accuracy, whereas GRU and LSTM provide more favorable module-level latency profiles. These results establish SurgGMF as a reproducible framework for causal Gaussian motion forecasting and advance surgical Gaussian representations from retrospective reconstruction toward predictive scene modeling.

</details>


### [33] [Detection of Adversarial Attacks on Super-Resolvers Using Spectral Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35022)
*Emma J. Reid,Haley Duba-Sullivan,Tony G. Allen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于频谱的检测方法，用于识别超分辨率模型权重中嵌入的对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型（如超分辨率）被集成到图像预处理流程中，为攻击者提供了针对下游任务的攻击向量，但目前对预处理模型中的对抗行为检测研究尚不充分。

Method: 使用径向平均功率谱密度（radially-averaged power spectral density）作为判别特征，训练XGBoost检测器，并与基于幅度和相位的傅里叶频谱检测器进行对比。

Result: 在多数训练和跨架构场景下，所提检测器性能优于对比方法，且高频特征对检测跨SR架构的AdvSR攻击最具信息量。

Conclusion: 频谱特征（尤其高频）可有效检测超分辨率网络中的模型级对抗威胁，提升关键成像流程的可信度。

Abstract: The integration of deep learning models into image preprocessing pipelines such as super-resolution introduces a largely unexplored attack vector for adversaries targeting downstream tasks. To ensure trustworthiness of critical imaging pipelines, we must be able to detect adversarial behavior within preprocessing models. In this paper, we propose a spectral-based detection method for identifying adversarial attacks embedded in super-resolution model weights. More specifically, we use the radially-averaged power spectral density as a discriminative feature to train an extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) detector, demonstrating detectability of model-level threats in super-resolution networks. We further benchmark our detector against magnitude- and phase-based Fourier spectrum detectors, evaluating each method across a range of training and cross-architecture scenarios. Our proposed detector out-performs the comparison detectors in most of these scenarios and indicates that high-frequency features are most informative for detecting AdvSR attacks across SR architectures.

</details>


### [34] [Mixed-Prior Decision Risk for Open-Set Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35043)
*L. A. Erlygin,P. D. Proskura,A. A. Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MPRisk，一种基于混合先验后验决策风险的不确定性评分，用于开放集识别，无需非线性校准器即可优于 HolUE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集识别中存在误接受、误拒绝和误识别三类错误，而现有贝叶斯模型 HolUE 用 KL 散度分量总结后验并以监督非线性校准器映射为不确定性分数，其 KL 摘要并非一般意义上对决策风险单调，线性融合在不同基准上甚至出现负筛选质量。

Method: 保留相同贝叶斯后验，将未知身份建模为连续分量，直接对所选决策对应的错误事件打分：误接受风险、误识别风险、误拒绝风险，并加入拒绝的非特异性惩罚；仅用验证集上调整的四个非负权重完成排序，无需非线性监督模型。

Result: 在九个图像、音频和文本基准上，MPRisk 在图像与音频的每个操作点以及多数文本操作点取得最佳或并列最佳 Prediction Rejection Ratio；在五个基准上以 bootstrap 确认相对 HolUE 有提升（最高 +0.19 PRR），运行时间相当或更低。

Conclusion: MPRisk 表明直接对决策错误事件建模的后验风险评分可替代非线性校准，在多种模态基准上提供更优的选择性识别排序且计算开销不增。

Abstract: In open-set recognition (OSR), a probe must either be identified as one of the known gallery classes or rejected as unknown, so three error types coexist: false acceptance, false rejection, and misidentification. An uncertainty score for selective recognition should rank probes by the risk of the decision the system has made. Bayesian gallery-aware models such as Holistic Uncertainty Estimation (HolUE) summarize the posterior over known and unknown classes by Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence components and map them to an uncertainty score with a supervised nonlinear calibrator. We show that the KL summary is not generally monotone in decision risk: linear fusion of the KL components tuned on validation data yields negative filtering quality on several benchmarks. We propose MPRisk, a mixed-prior posterior decision-risk score that keeps the same Bayesian posterior but directly scores the error events associated with the selected decision: false-acceptance, misidentification, and false-rejection risks, plus a non-specificity penalty for rejections, enabled by modeling unknown identities as a continuous component. Four nonnegative weights tuned on a validation set suffice for ranking; no nonlinear supervised model is required. Across nine image, audio, and text benchmarks, MPRisk achieves the best or tied-best Prediction Rejection Ratio at every operating point on the image and audio benchmarks and on most text operating points, with bootstrap-confirmed gains over HolUE on five benchmarks (up to $+0.19$ PRR) at comparable or lower runtime.

</details>


### [35] [Beyond Saying Less: Fine-Grained Alignment for Informative and Faithful Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35294)
*Xingming Long,Jie Zhang,Yuecong Min,Shiguang Shan,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出细粒度对齐框架，通过数据层面的密集奖励信号(DOPA数据集)与算法层面的精确信用分配(SCAPO)解决多模态大模型的对象幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大视觉语言模型存在对象幻觉问题。off-policy偏好优化有效，但on-policy强化学习更有前景，因其直接针对模型当前失败模式。然而作者发现缺乏细粒度奖励制定与分配时，on-policy优化易走捷径——通过"少说"减少幻觉，即减少有效主张。

Method: 1) 构建DOPA(密集对象存在与缺失)数据集，对扩展词表中每个概念的确定存在与缺失进行穷尽标注，提升on-policy rollout中可靠奖励信号密度；2) 提出SCAPO(子句级信用分配)，基于对象主张对每个子句独立分配信用，防止响应级共享优势使局部幻觉损害其他有效输出；3) 利用生成的忠实图像描述作为辅助上下文，将生成增益迁移到判别任务。

Result: 实验证明该方法在生成任务中产生高度信息丰富、忠实的描述，同时在判别评估中获得明显性能提升。
