Motivation: 现有审计只检查权重是否冻结，误判了智能体能力的真正天花板来源。论文要说明真正的约束在"可达编辑集"，并提出判断迭代自我修改何时收益递减的准则，同时区分常被混为一谈的三种机制（固定类内搜索、提升上限的测试时训练、介于两者间的脚手架重写）。

Method: 将refinement解读为对draft与target残差的梯度提升（补丁/git diff），再测量该解读在何处失效：补丁是组合而非并列可投票、失败会重叠。通过SWE-bench逐轮改进与401个生产会话的churn来量化饱和，并与pre-AI人类基线对照。

Result: 逐轮改进在SWE-bench上趋向于零；churn在401个生产会话中几何衰减，形状与pre-AI人类基线一致（确立该regime但不指明成因）。Best-of-k精确实现最佳worker上限：宽度只买速率不买预算；固定池重咨询的horizon可提前计算且仅由池决定；唯一能突破的加权投票需要真实worker不具备的多样性——30个同族worker失败重叠达最大，多数投票在23/55（42%）任务上失败。

Conclusion: 冻结权重只能买到最终天花板，换不来过程中的平稳性；瓶颈在可达编辑集。补丁可组合与失败重叠是工程选择而非代码定律，因此二者共同指明了值得构建的harness方向。核心结论：审计脚手架，而非checkpoint。

Abstract: An auditor who checks whether a system's weights are frozen is checking the wrong thing. Our stationarity dichotomy says that iterative self-modification hits strict diminishing returns whenever the agent's reachable set of edits stays fixed, and can escape only if that set expands. Rewriting scaffolding (tools, verifiers, decomposition) expands what an agent reaches without touching a weight, so frozen weights buy an eventual ceiling but no stationarity along the way. The criterion also separates three regimes usually merged: search within a fixed class, test-time training that raises the ceiling itself, and scaffold rewriting between them. Audit the scaffold, not the checkpoint.
  The same ceiling binds sideways. Best-of-$k$ orchestration realizes the best worker's ceiling exactly: width buys rate, not budget. Re-consulting a fixed pool has a horizon computable in advance, decided by the pool alone, and the one arrangement that would beat it, a weighted vote, needs diversity real workers lack: on 30 same-family workers the failure overlap sits at its maximum, and a majority fails 23/55 (42%) of tasks.
  We obtain the criterion by reading refinement as gradient boosting on the residual error between draft and target, a patch or git diff, and then measuring where that reading breaks: patches compose instead of standing beside each other to be voted on, and failures overlap. What we measure is saturation. Per-round improvement decays toward zero on SWE-bench, and churn decays geometrically across 401 production sessions, a shape shared with a pre-AI human baseline that establishes the regime without identifying its cause. Both breaks are engineering choices rather than laws about code, so together they specify a harness worth building.

</details>


### [70] [Price Stability in the European Union: A Systemic Approach Using Random Matrix Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35011)
*Sami Diaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用随机矩阵理论分析欧元区月度通胀指数系统，发现其非随机、噪声主导，识别出两个扰动通胀动态的国家及三个区域组，系统虽复杂但具自组织稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 价格稳定是货币政策支柱，在货币联盟中尤为重要。主流经济学用价格指数和指标分析价格稳定，而数据丰富使随机矩阵理论（基于内在相关模式）被用于去除噪声、提升多变量分析质量。作者希望借此揭示欧元区各国层面的内在动态。

Method: 收集欧元区各国月度通胀指数，将其视为一个"价格系统"，运用随机矩阵理论分析其特征值的统计与渐近性质，以剥离噪声并挖掘国家层面的相关结构。

Result: 系统不能被视为随机生成，呈现噪声主导的模式，源于国家层面小而持续的变化，使跨国相关性更动态、信号与噪声难以分离。识别出两个扭曲通胀动态的国家，以及三个区域性的国家组。变异来源可能包括引发部分国家通胀飙升的经济事件，以及确保欧盟数据质量与代表性的方法学因素。

Conclusion: 尽管系统复杂，仍表现出一定的自组织稳定性；大幅月度波动不应被视为罕见事件，而是数据生成过程的一部分。

Abstract: Price stability remains a pillar in monetary policy practices and carries a special importance within monetary unions. Mainstream economics tried to leverage price stability using price indices and several metrics to shed light on specific dynamics and optimal macroeconomic levels. The wide availability of data led researchers to consider the study of systems using Random Matrix Theory, based on inner correlation patterns. This aims to enhance the multivariate analysis by removing noisy patterns from the signal and improve data quality for further inferences. This work considers the collection of monthly inflation indices in the Eurozone as a \textit{system} of prices to analyze its eigenvalues' statistical and asymptotic properties and uncover inner country-level insights. Results confirm the system cannot assumed to be randomly generated, and the data exhibit noise-dominated patterns, due to small and persistent variations at the country-level. The latter make the inter-country correlations more dynamic and the separation of the signal from the noise quiet difficult. Findings identified two countries as distorting inflation dynamics besides three other distinct, regional-based groups of countries. Variability sources might stem from economic episodes fueling inflation spikes in some countries, as well as methodological aspects used to ensure data quality and representativeness in the European Union. Despite being complex, the system demonstrates a certain stability, in terms of self-organization; while large monthly fluctuations cannot be considered as rare events, but part of the data-generating process.

</details>


### [71] [$λ$-JEPA Spectral Anti-Collapse Regularization for Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35288)
*Berker Demirel,Clémentine Dominé,Valentino Maiorca,Marco Fumero,Marco Mondelli,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出JEPA等自监督学习中骨干网络存在维度坍缩问题，提出谱反坍缩正则器SACReg及λ-JEPA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA等自监督学习在投影头后施加不变性目标，但下游任务使用投影前的骨干表示，二者不匹配导致骨干仍可能维度坍缩、有效秩低，限制下游迁移。

Method: 提出SACReg——基于λ-balance分析（刻画层间权重矩阵相对尺度）的谱反坍缩正则器。理论上证明双层线性网络中λ-balance可防坍缩，且该正则器作用于骨干可诱导λ-balance；非线性情形同样反坍缩。将其应用于JEPA得到λ-JEPA。

Result: 在ImageNet100真实架构上提升表示秩；在ImageNet-1k分类及八个下游图像数据集平均线性探测迁移上优于LeJEPA与VISReg；视频自监督中在Something-Something-v2和Kinetics-400上优于LeVJEPA和V-JEPA 2。

Conclusion: 骨干表示维度坍缩是自监督学习的重要隐患，SACReg通过诱导λ-balance有效提升表示秩并改善下游迁移，λ-JEPA在图像与视频任务上均取得更优表现。

Abstract: Joint-embedding self-supervised learning typically combines an invariance objective across augmented views with additional mechanisms to prevent representational collapse. These objectives are often applied after a projection head, while downstream tasks use the backbone representation before the projector. We find that this mismatch does not necessarily prevent dimensional collapse in the backbone, which can retain low effective rank and potentially limit downstream transfer. To address this, we introduce SACReg, a spectral anti-collapse regularizer motivated by an analysis of $λ$-balance, which captures the relative scale of weight matrices across layers. In a two-layer linear network, we show that (i) $λ$-balance prevents collapse, and (ii) our regularizer applied to the backbone induces $λ$-balance. In the nonlinear case, this regularizer leads to anti-collapse as well and, in realistic architectures on ImageNet100, it empirically increases the representations' ranks. We apply SACReg to JEPA and propose $λ$-JEPA, which improves over LeJEPA and VISReg on ImageNet-1k classification and in average linear-probe transfer performance across eight downstream image datasets. On video self-supervised learning, $λ$-JEPA improves over LeVJEPA and V-JEPA 2 on the Something-Something-v2 and Kinetics-400 benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/berkerdemirel/lambda-jepa.

</details>


### [72] [SpikeCredit: Temporal Credit Carrier for Reinforcement Learning with Sparse Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35268)
*Yingchao Yu,Pengfei Sun,Wenxuan Pan,Wei Chen,Yitian Hong,Kuangrong Hao,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SpikeCredit框架，利用脉冲神经网络(SNN)的膜电位轨迹与事件驱动脉冲作为"时间信用载体(TCC)"，解决稀疏奖励强化学习中的信用分配难题，在MuJoCo任务上大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏奖励RL中延迟结果难以指导中间计算，导致信用分配困难。作者认为可靠信用分配需要能随时间保留并暴露信用相关信息，而SNN的分级膜电位轨迹和事件驱动脉冲天然满足这一需求。

Method: 将SNN形式化为Temporal Credit Carriers(TCCs)，提出SpikeCredit框架：先进行任务自适应TCC选择，再通过快慢双通路闭环——快速TCC读取通路用自运动反馈约束实现局部行为约束下的转移级信用恢复，慢速TCC写入通路用信用靶向轨迹对齐将恢复的信用反馈给actor。

Result: 在稀疏奖励MuJoCo任务上，Last10回报较稀疏SNN基线提升：Ant +1169%、Hopper +953%、Swimmer +723%、Walker2d +1781%；在Swimmer上超过稠密奖励基线+113%。机制分析显示其与稠密奖励的对齐度显著优于稀疏SNN基线。

Conclusion: 脉冲动力学可作为稀疏奖励RL中保留信用的基板，验证了TCC假设的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse rewards is challenging because delayed outcomes provide little guidance about which intermediate computations caused success or failure. We argue that reliable credit assignment requires policy dynamics that preserve and expose credit-relevant information over time, a role we formalize as Temporal Credit Carriers (TCCs) and that spiking neural networks (SNNs) naturally fulfill through graded membrane traces and event-driven spikes. Based on this hypothesis, we propose SpikeCredit, an SNN-based framework for RL with sparse rewards that first performs task-adaptive TCC selection and then closes the loop between a fast TCC-reading pathway, where self-motion feedback constraint uses local behavior-grounded cues to constrain transition-level credit recovery, and a slow TCC-writing pathway, where credit-targeted trace alignment feeds recovered credit back into the actor to make future TCC dynamics more credit-readable. Across sparse-reward MuJoCo tasks, SpikeCredit improves Last10 return over sparse SNN baselines by +1169% on Ant, +953% on Hopper, +723% on Swimmer, and +1781% on Walker2d, and exceeds the dense-reward baseline on Swimmer by +113%. Mechanistic analyses further show substantially stronger alignment with dense rewards than the sparse SNN baseline. These results position spiking dynamics as credit-preserving substrates for sparse-reward RL.

</details>


### [73] [Physics-Guided Conditional Diffusion Model for Rare Event Synthesis and Diagnosis for the Water-Gas Shift Reaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35499)
*Md Abrar Rafid Siddique,Bibek Aryal,Qiugang Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理引导的条件扩散模型，生成水煤气变换（WGS）反应中的稀有事件轨迹，用于数据增强并提升异常工况诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WGS反应制氢过程可能发生严重结垢、催化剂劣化和热失控等稀有异常事件，但这些数据难以采集，导致正常与异常数据分布不平衡，影响诊断模型训练。

Method: 将条件去噪扩散概率模型（CDDPM）与WGS反应的控制方程相结合，生成物理一致的稀有事件轨迹；通过条件特征生成超出训练范围的稀有域合成数据；并提出危险评分评估工况风险严重度。用增强数据训练深度学习模型进行反应健康状态诊断。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提物理引导扩散模型在合成数据质量和稀有事件诊断性能方面均优于纯数据驱动模型。

Conclusion: 物理引导的扩散模型能有效生成稀有事件的真实轨迹，缓解数据不平衡问题，并提升WGS反应异常工况诊断能力。

Abstract: As the world moves towards sustainable energy sources, hydrogen (H2) can be treated as an eco-friendly alternative to fossil fuels due to its high energy density and zero carbon emissions. The water-gas shift (WGS) reaction is a widely used industrial process for hydrogen production by converting carbon monoxide and steam into hydrogen and carbon dioxide. However, occurrences like severe fouling, catalyst deterioration, and thermal runaway can hamper the reaction kinetics/process safety and decrease the yield of H2. These incidents are rare, and gathering process data under such abnormal conditions is challenging. In this work, we propose a physics-guided conditional diffusion model to generate realistic rare-event trajectories for the WGS reaction. The proposed model integrates a conditional denoising diffusion probabilistic model (CDDPM) with governing laws of the reaction to generate physically consistent process trajectories. The conditioning features allow the model to produce high-quality synthetic profiles for rare-event domains that are typically beyond the training regimes. The generated rare-event trajectories then augment the raw dataset for a balanced distribution between normal and abnormal conditions. We further propose a hazard score to assess the risk severity of the operating condition based on the operating trajectory. Deep learning models are trained with the augmented dataset to diagnose the health status of the reaction. Simulation results show that the proposed physics-guided diffusion model outperforms data-driven models in terms of the quality of synthetic data and diagnosis performance for rare events.

</details>


### [74] [Optimal Networks for Agentic Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35537)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Kearns等(2026)网络化学习模型下信息聚合的极限。考虑自适应性/非自适应性设计者两种设定，每智能体观察1个特征。主要结论：d≥3时每智能体1个父节点即使设计者知道分布也无法保证精确聚合；每智能体2个父节点则在非自适应设定下即可实现精确聚合，深度O(d log d)（自适应为O(d)），代理数O(d²)，且均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns、Roth和Ryu(2026)主要关注给定图和特征分配下深度路径上的误差趋近，但对一般的图和分配设计缺乏刻画。本文转而研究该模型在设计者可以选择图、特征分配和输出节点时的聚合能力极限，区分自适应性（知道分布）与非自适应性（分布由对手选择）两种设计场景。

Method: 在DAG上按拓扑序学习的线性预测模型中，设定每个智能体观察一个特征并从有限个父节点接收预测。定义“精确聚合”（输出智能体与全局线性预测器完全一致）。分别构造自适应性设计者与非自适应性设计者的图和特征分配方案，分析所需深度与代理数量，并证明深度与代理数的下界匹配。

Result: 对于d≥3，当每个智能体只有1个父节点时，任意有限深度都不能保证对所有分布实现精确聚合，即使设计者已知分布。相反，每个智能体2个父节点在非自适应设定下即可实现精确聚合：固定的图、特征分配和输出节点对任意分布在深度O(d log d)内达成；若已知分布，深度降为O(d)。两种构造均使用O(d²)个代理（双父节点构造常数很大）。 auteurs进一步证明深度和代理数的界在常数因子内都是最优的。

Conclusion: 精确聚合对每智能体的父节点数高度敏感：1个父节点在一般情况下不可行，2个父节点即可在非自适应设定下实现，且深度与代理数均达到最优阶。这揭示了网络化学习中信息聚合的基本结构与设计权衡。

Abstract: We study information aggregation in the networked learning model introduced by Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA 2026). There is a fixed distribution over $d$ features and a common label. Agents learn in topological order on a directed acyclic graph. Each observes a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor to minimize mean squared error, and passes only its prediction forward. The global predictor is the best linear predictor using all features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu show that the output agent's error approaches the global predictor's error along sufficiently deep paths with suitable feature coverage, while insufficient depth can prevent aggregation even in large networks.
  In contrast to their main focus on a given graph and feature allocation, we consider the limits of the model under two settings. In the adaptive designer setting, a designer chooses the graph, feature allocation, and output agent knowing the distribution. In the oblivious designer setting, the designer fixes all three before an adversary chooses the distribution. Each agent observes one feature and receives predictions from a limited number of parents.
  We call the aggregation exact when the output agent matches the global predictor exactly. For $d\ge3$, we show that no finite depth guarantees exact aggregation for every distribution with one parent per agent, even when the designer knows the distribution.
  In contrast, two parents per agent suffice for exact aggregation even in the oblivious designer setting. A fixed graph, feature allocation, and output agent achieve this for every distribution at depth $O(d\log d)$. Knowing the distribution reduces the depth to $O(d)$. Both constructions use $O(d^2)$ agents, with a very large constant for two parents. We show the bounds on the depth and number of agents are all optimal up to constant factors.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [READ-Bench: Benchmarking Historical Instance Retrieval for Time-Series Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32123)
*Gerardo Pastrana,Haojun Li,Dhruv Mehta,Anoushka Vyas,Sina Khoshfetrat Pakazad,Henrik Ohlsson,John Paparrizos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 READ-Bench 基准，聚焦多变量时间序列的历史故障/事件检索，发现检索的关键在于少量标注监督的重排序而非更强的表示模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序诊断系统很少利用历史案例检索，且检索评价通常依赖下游预测间接进行，缺乏直接、统一的检索评估基准。

Method: 构建涵盖12个诊断数据集的多变量时序检索基准，以共享故障/事件类型而非信号形状定义相关性。采用"基础检索器+重排序器"框架，统一评测经典距离、符号检索器、自监督/基础模型嵌入及其融合，以及标签感知和语言模型重排序器，并变化监督、污染和语料规模进行显著性检验。最终提出融合方案：正常残差评分嵌入器与DTW路径经倒数排名融合，再用高斯过程重排序器。

Result: 在统一通道独立接口下，预训练表示在纯搜索上无统计显著优势；关键在于重排序阶段的少量已解决案例监督，高斯过程重排序器显著优于更复杂表示或语言模型推理，且在污染和全语料下依然稳健。融合方案在全部12个数据集上NDCG@10提升：重排序贡献+0.11，较最强单基础检索器+0.16。

Conclusion: 历史案例检索的价值主要来自少量标注监督的重排序，而非更复杂的表示或语言模型推理；所提融合+高斯过程重排序方案在多数据集上一致有效。

Abstract: Time-series diagnostic systems rarely rely on retrieving relevant historical cases, and when they do, retrieval is evaluated only indirectly through downstream prediction. We introduce READ-Bench, a benchmark for historical-case retrieval across 12 diagnostic datasets, centered on multivariate time series, that defines relevance by shared fault or event type rather than signal shape, so visually different traces of the same fault count as relevant while similar-looking traces of different faults do not. Treating retrieval as a base retriever followed by a reranker, we evaluate classical distances, symbolic retrievers, self-supervised and foundation-model embedders, and their fusion, plus label-aware and language-model rerankers, under one protocol that varies supervision, pollution, and corpus scale with significance testing. Under a common channel-independent interface, pretrained representations offer no statistically detectable advantage over strong classical and symbolic baselines for search alone. The decisive factor is a small amount of resolved-case supervision at reranking, namely a Gaussian-process reranker that propagates a few neighbor labels in embedding space, which helps far more than more sophisticated representations or language-model reasoning and holds under pollution and at full corpus scale. Guided by these findings, we fuse a normal-residual-scored embedder with a dynamic time warping leg via reciprocal-rank fusion, then rerank with the Gaussian-process reranker, improving NDCG@10 over its own search stage on all 12 datasets, by +0.11 from reranking and +0.16 over the strongest single base retriever.

</details>


### [76] [LAM: Efficient Lossy Agent Memory Framework With A Retrieval-Score Error Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32256)
*Baixi Sun,Le Chen,Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Xiaolong Ma,Chih-Hsuan Yang,Mingze Xia,Syed Zawad,Sheng Di,Rajkumar Kettimuthu,Huihuo Zheng,Rajeev Thakur,Venkatram Vishwanath,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 LAM，一种有损 Agent 记忆系统，通过确定性去重规则、保留缓存前缀的记忆管理器及性能模型，在去除22.47%观测token的同时保留99.984%金标证据，并预测71.4x-91.6x端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent 记忆随读取、推理与工具调用不断增长，导致推理成本上升并最终超出上下文窗口；基于 LLM 的摘要虽可压缩历史，却增加延迟且无法显式界定信息损失。

Method: LAM 含三部分：(1) 确定性去重规则，附带对检索分数的替换界（仅界定分数扰动，非排名不变的证明）；(2) 保留缓存前缀、并将压缩与推理重叠执行的记忆管理器；(3) 部署前估计压缩成本的性能模型。

Result: 在600条Agent轨迹上，去除22.47%观测token，同时保留99.984%测得金标证据；在固定删除集下，性能模型预测在预填充前移除记录相比从已填充上下文删除可获71.4x-91.6x端到端加速。

Conclusion: 该收益源自调度而非规则本身，且适用于任何保留前缀的测试。

Abstract: Agent memory grows as agents read inputs, reason, and call tools. Longer histories increase inference cost and eventually exceed the context window. LLM-based summarization reduces this history but adds latency and provides no explicit bound on information loss. We propose LAM, a Lossy Agent Memory system with three components: a deterministic deduplication rule with a substitution bound on retrieval scores - a bound on score perturbation, not a certificate of unchanged ranking; a memory manager that preserves the cached prefix and overlaps compaction with inference; and a performance model that estimates compaction costs before deployment. On 600 agent trajectories, LAM removes 22.47% of observation tokens while retaining 99.984% of the measured gold-patch evidence. At a fixed deletion set, the performance model predicts a 71.4x-91.6x end-to-end speedup from removing records before prefill instead of deleting them from a prefilled context. That benefit comes from the schedule rather than the rule and applies to any prefix-preserving test.

</details>


### [77] [Agentsensus: Consensus-Compressed Shared Memory for Multi-Agent Story Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32297)
*Yu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentsensus，用统一长期记忆取代每个角色独立记忆流，让同一事件在所有见证者间共享并链接相关记录，减少存储冗余同时保持/提升模拟质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准故事世界模拟给每个角色私有记忆流，同一共享事件被每个见证者各存一份，造成大量存储冗余。

Method: 统一长期记忆：同一事件记录合并为所有见证者共有，语义相关记录相互链接；在四个世界（两部中文古典小说、Hamlet、真实冲突时间线）上 40–80 轮，与三种逐角色记忆设计在等粒度协议下对比，并做消融。

Result: 比最接近基线少写 22–44% 条目；唯一实现共享（14–28% 记录被多人持有，部分达 10 人）与链接（94–99%）的设计；质量不劣甚至更优。消融显示关闭合并使存储增至 3.1 倍、共享归零；共享随轮数增加（10/20/40 轮时为 6%/9%/14%）。

Conclusion: 统一合并共享记忆可显著降低存储冗余并产生共享、链接的记忆结构，且不牺牲模拟质量，共享效应随模拟时间累积增强。

Abstract: A agentic story world is a dynamic system simulating who learned what, when, and from whom -- yet the standard design gives each character a private memory stream. A shared event is therefore stored once per witness, large duplication will be incurred in terms of storage. We present Agentsensus, a story-world simulation framework in which there is an unified long-term memory. Records of the same event merge into one owned by all its witnesses, and semantically relevant memory records are linked. We evaluate on four worlds -- two classical Chinese novels, Hamlet, and a real-world conflict timeline -- run for 40 to 80 rounds against three per-character memory designs under an equal-granularity protocol. Agentsensus writes 22-44% fewer entries than the closest baseline and is the only design whose memory becomes shared (14-28% of records held by more than one character, some by 10) and linked (94-99%), at judged simulation quality indistinguishable or even better than the baselines. An ablation attributes this to the merge itself: disabling it multiplies the store by 3.1x and takes sharing to exactly zero. Sharing also compounds with the horizon rather than saturating early, rising 6% to 9% to 14% as one world is re-run at 10, 20 and 40 rounds.

</details>


### [78] [Delayed Supervision for Test-Time Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32312)
*Jinha Kim,Taksh Kothari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"延迟监督"（delayed supervision）作为测试时记忆模型的外层训练目标：在后训练时，经过一段无关事件的长时间间隔后再提问，并将答案与常规next-token预测一同监督，以增强记忆的保持与更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时语言模型在压缩记忆时会经历大量后续更新，而仅用next-token预测训练并不明确要求某个事实在多次更新后仍可访问，因此需要显式监督记忆的长期可用性。

Method: 在后训练中，经过长时间无关事件后再由模拟器提问，问题在可丢弃的分支上评估（答案不进入后续事件流），并将回答与next-token预测联合监督。构建设计区分"保持"（事实始终有效）与"修正"（需回答最新值）。在LaCT-760M和DeltaNet-1.3B上用TextWorld轨迹训练，并在BABILong和RULER上评测，另单独报告RWKV-7对比。

Result: 相比仅事件训练，延迟QA使BABILong提升：LaCT +5.48个百分点、DeltaNet +1.32个百分点；单针RULER提升：分别+1.45与+3.27个百分点。RWKV-7在其自身面板上提升+4.60与+7.00个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果支持延迟语义监督作为可用的测试时记忆外层训练目标；但尚未厘清收益中多少具体来自"延迟"本身，而非一般问答与答案终止监督。

Abstract: Test-time language models adapt a compact memory while processing the input sequence. This
  perspective encompasses nonlinear fast-weight learning in LaCT, associative delta-rule
  updates in DeltaNet, and generalized delta-rule state updates in RWKV-7. Training these
  models to predict the next token does not explicitly require a fact to remain accessible
  after many subsequent memory updates. We study delayed supervision for this test-time memory:
  during post-training, ask a simulator-grounded question only after a long interval of
  unrelated events, and supervise its answer alongside ordinary next-token prediction.
  Questions are evaluated on disposable branches, so their answers never enter the continuing
  event stream. The construction distinguishes retention from revision: a retained fact must
  remain valid throughout the delay, whereas a revised fact must be answered with its latest
  value. We evaluate this approach on LaCT-760M and plain DeltaNet-1.3B using TextWorld
  training trajectories and shared BABILong and RULER evaluation panels, and include a
  separately reported RWKV-7 comparison. Relative to event-only training, delayed QA improves
  BABILong by 5.48 percentage points for LaCT and 1.32 points for DeltaNet, and single-needle
  RULER by 1.45 and 3.27 points, respectively. The RWKV-7 comparison reports gains of 4.60 and
  7.00 points on its own panels. These results support delayed semantic supervision as a
  practical outer training objective for usable test-time memory, while leaving open how much
  of the benefit derives specifically from delay rather than general question-answering and
  answer-termination supervision.

</details>


### [79] [Enabling Timely Guidance before Skill Retrieval: Retaining Helpful Warm Tips in Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32339)
*Feng Liang,Yupeng Li,Runhao Zeng,Francis C. M. Lau,Xiping Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TipsWarm 通过维护一个带预算的技能要点池（warm tips），在每轮消息中按需注入，使技能指导在代理决定检索前即可用，同时控制维护成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能机制通常只暴露元数据、按需加载全文，导致代理在决定检索前无法获得有用指导；而通用记忆方法维护开销大，将指导常驻对话上下文又可能反复暴露无关或有害建议。

Method: 提出 TipsWarm，补充现有技能机制，维护带预算的 skill-derived keypoints 池；将事件触发的 LLM 评估与廉价的逐轮筛选分离，实现选择性注入到每轮上下文。

Result: 在三个编码与迭代任务执行基准上，相比近期技能与通用记忆基线，TipsWarm 取得最高任务成功率，且保持时间高效。

Conclusion: TipsWarm 能在控制维护成本的同时，让可迁移技能指导随时可用，从而提升 LLM 代理复杂任务表现。

Abstract: Reusable skills help LLM-based agents solve complex tasks, but the agent must receive guidance before it commits to an ineffective approach. Existing skill mechanisms often expose only metadata and load full content on demand, leaving useful guidance unavailable until the agent decides to retrieve it. General memory methods can incur substantial maintenance overhead, while keeping guidance in conversation context risks repeatedly exposing the agent to irrelevant or harmful advice. We propose TipsWarm, a mechanism that complements existing skill mechanisms by maintaining a budgeted pool of skill-derived keypoints, or \textit{warm tips}, for selective injection into the context of every message turn. By separating event-triggered LLM assessment from inexpensive per-turn screening, it makes transferable skill guidance readily available while controlling maintenance costs. In three coding and iterative task-execution benchmarks, TipsWarm achieves the highest task success rate while remaining time-efficient, compared to recent skill and general memory baselines.

</details>


### [80] [SCLATE: a Substrate for Continual-Learning Agent Training and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32391)
*Youngmok Jung,Sirajul Salekin,Henry Tran,Javier Movellan,Zhao Huang,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCLATE是一个执行基座，通过开放事件调度器和混合模拟时钟，统一调度基准与智能体事件，将长周期场景压缩到数小时内，既作评测又作强化学习rollout引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习智能体需在长多会话中交错任务与各类智能体事件（会话启停、定时任务、记忆整合），但现有基准与训练框架只调度自身事件，导致每对基准-智能体都要自建调度循环，缺乏统一方案。

Method: 基准与未修改的智能体通过适配器向同一开放事件调度器注册事件；混合模拟时钟在共享时间轴上按真实时间推进、跳过空闲间隔；容器内代理记录每次模型调用的token与log概率，作为rollout引擎无改动运行harness与记忆。

Result: 移植7个基准，在10个模型上横向比较10种无修改harness与记忆配置：额外记忆系统未必胜过harness原生记忆，同一harness/记忆下不同模型使用方式差异大。用其post-train Qwen3.5-4B，读文件行数减少6.8倍，SWE-bench Verified通过率提升16.7分，写出更丰富记忆记录，held-out MetaClaw准确率最高提升11.8分。

Conclusion: SCLATE可作为持续学习智能体统一评测与训练的基础设施，揭示记忆系统与harness的关系，并证明通过无修改harness/记忆进行post-training能有效提升模型对二者的使用能力。

Abstract: Continual-learning agents are systems of models, harnesses, and memory operating over long multi-session horizons. Evaluating and training them requires interleaving tasks with agent-side events such as session stop and start, crons, and memory consolidation. Yet existing benchmarks and training frameworks schedule only the benchmark's own events, leaving each benchmark and agent pair to build a custom scheduling loop. We present SCLATE, an execution substrate where benchmarks and unmodified agents each add their events to one open event scheduler through an adapter. A hybrid simulated clock runs these events on a shared timeline, flowing in real time while the agent works and skipping idle gaps, which compresses a month-long scenario into hours. SCLATE also serves as a rollout engine that runs any agent's harness and memory unmodified, recording the tokens and log probabilities of every model call through an in-container proxy. We port seven benchmarks to SCLATE and compare ten unmodified harness and memory configurations head to head on ten models. The comparison shows that an added memory system does not reliably beat the harness's native memory and that models differ widely in how they use the same harness and memory. We then post-train Qwen3.5-4B through unmodified harnesses and memory systems. The model learns to use both, reading 6.8x fewer file lines with a 16.7-point higher SWE-bench Verified pass rate, and writing richer memory records, while its held-out MetaClaw accuracy rises by up to 11.8 points.

</details>


### [81] [Function Over Form: Distributional Orthogonalization in Mixture-of-Experts with Replica Expert Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32398)
*Jinfan He,Yunzhuo Liu,Kai Zhang,Weidong Han,Key,Rayying*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DO-loss和REM机制,通过动态路由正则化和复制专家负载均衡,缓解MoE专家坍缩与表示冗余,提升模型容量利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽能解耦计算量与参数量,但专家坍缩和表示冗余导致模型容量利用不足,现有方法多关注静态权重多样性,忽略动态路由行为。

Method: 提出Distributional Orthogonalization Loss(DO-loss),将每个专家的token分配历史表示为高维二进制负载签名,通过惩罚签名重叠防止专家坍缩并促进功能特化;同时引入Replica Expert Mechanism(REM),采用全局批次级复制专家放置调整与微批次级实时token调度两层策略改善负载均衡。

Result: 在4.8BA0.5B和30BA3B MoE模型的下游任务上,方法优于所评估的路由算法,同时保持可比的训练效率。

Conclusion: 从静态权重多样性转向动态路由行为的正则化,并结合系统级负载均衡机制,可有效提升MoE模型容量利用率与下游性能。

Abstract: The scaling of LLMs increasingly relies on MoE architectures to decouple active computation from total parameter count. However, the efficacy of MoE is often constrained by expert collapse and representation redundancy, both leading to underutilization of model capacity. To address these challenges, this paper proposes Distributional Orthogonalization Loss (DO-loss), an auxiliary regularization that shifts the focus from static weight diversity to dynamic routing behavior. By representing each expert's token assignment history as a high-dimensional binary load signature, DO-loss penalizes signature overlap to prevent expert collapse while encouraging functional specialization. To align this algorithmic design with system efficiency, we further introduce the Replica Expert Mechanism (REM), which improves load balancing through a two-tiered strategy: adjusting replica expert placement at the global-batch level and performing real-time token dispatching at the micro-batch level. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that our method outperforms the evaluated routing algorithms on downstream tasks for both 4.8BA0.5B and 30BA3B MoE models, while maintaining comparable training efficiency.

</details>


### [82] [From Anomalies to Failures: Constructing Causal Error Graphs for Agentic Trace Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32514)
*Shu-Xun Yang,Yidong Wang,Zhuoer Feng,Bosi Wen,Jiayi Gui,Dayong Yang,Wenbo Yu,Haoke Zhang,Jie Tang,Cunxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CEG-Agent，通过显式的异常/错误/失败分类与因果错误图（CEG）实现对智能体轨迹的因果诊断，并构建CEG-Bench基准，取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的智能体轨迹很长，失败难以诊断。现有方法混淆异常、错误与失败，导致诊断目标不明确；且缺乏对因果相关错误如何传播并放大为最终任务失败的结构化建模，使失败归因不可靠。

Method: 提出CEG-Agent，一个工具增强的智能体框架，用于轨迹的因果诊断。引入异常、错误与失败的显式分类法，构建因果错误图（CEGs）——一种统一的类型化表示，通过因果关系连接执行事件、诊断节点与失败结果。同时构建CEG-Bench基准，采用对抗式智能体裁决协议（AAAP）获得高置信度、共识衍生的CEG标注，并用专家人工金标准验证。

Result: CEG-Bench上，CEG-Agent在语义宽松与结构精确两种评估标准下均达到SOTA。自动标注与专家人工金标准高度一致。

Conclusion: 显式分类+因果错误图的结构化建模能有效提升智能体轨迹的失败归因可靠性，CEG-Agent与CEG-Bench为因果轨迹诊断提供了方法与评测基础。代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM-driven agents are increasingly deployed in complex applications, where long agentic traces make failures difficult to diagnose. Existing trace diagnosis methods often conflate anomalies, errors, and failures, making diagnostic targets ambiguous; they also lack structured modeling of how causally relevant errors propagate and amplify into final task failures, resulting in unreliable failure attribution. To address these problems, we propose CEG-Agent, a tool-augmented agentic framework for causal diagnosis of agentic traces. Specifically, CEG-Agent introduces an explicit taxonomy of anomalies, errors, and failures, and constructs Causal Error Graphs (CEGs), a unified typed representation that links execution events, diagnostic nodes, and failure outcomes through causal relations. To evaluate causal trace diagnosis, we further construct CEG-Bench, a fully agent-annotated benchmark with high-confidence, consensus-derived CEG annotations obtained through an Adversarial Agentic Adjudication Protocol (AAAP). We validate the resulting annotations against an expert-curated human gold set, which shows close agreement with the automatic annotations. Experiments on CEG-Bench demonstrate that CEG-Agent achieves state-of-the-art performance under both semantically relaxed and structurally exact evaluation criteria. Our code is publicly available.

</details>


### [83] [EMIR$^2$: Evolution-Aware Memory with Intent-Guided Multi-Round Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32584)
*Jinlan Liu,Hongliang Sun,Yong Wang,Bolin Zhang,Dinabo Sui,Dianhui Chu,Zhiying Tu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EMIR²框架，通过演化感知记忆图与意图引导多轮检索，提升LLM智能体的长期记忆利用、冲突处理与复杂检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆系统多依赖静态记忆表示和单轮检索，难以跟踪事实变化、整合长期交互中分散的证据，无法有效利用持续演化的历史信息。

Method: 构建状态演化记忆图（SEMG），将长期记忆表示为演化知识状态，由时间事件轨迹和证据关联支撑；通过基于证据的更新维护语义状态，保留历史演化并支持证据追溯。在此之上引入意图引导的多轮检索机制，迭代识别缺失证据并基于累积信息扩展检索。

Result: 在LoCoMo和MemConflict数据集上，EMIR²提升了长期记忆利用、动态与静态冲突处理及复杂检索性能，部分类别相对提升超过12%。

Conclusion: 联合建模记忆演化与自适应证据获取对长期智能体交互有效，验证了该框架的优越性。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables large language model (LLM) agents to leverage historical interactions for future tasks. However, existing memory systems struggle to utilize continuously evolving historical information, as they often rely on static memory representations and single-round retrieval strategies, failing to track factual changes or integrate distributed evidence across long-term interactions. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$, an \textbf{E}volution-Aware \textbf{M}emory framework with \textbf{I}ntent-Guided Multi-\textbf{R}ound \textbf{R}etrieval, enabling LLM agents to maintain evolving historical knowledge and adaptively retrieve relevant evidence. Specifically, \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ constructs a State-Evolving Memory Graph (SEMG) that represents long-term memory as evolving knowledge states supported by temporal event trajectories and evidential associations. By maintaining semantic states through evidence-based updates, SEMG preserves historical evolution and enables evidence tracing under complex and conflicting scenarios. Building upon this, we introduce an intent-guided multi-round retrieval mechanism that iteratively identifies missing evidence and expands retrieval based on accumulated information. Experiments on LoCoMo and MemConflict demonstrate that \textsc{EMIR}$^{2}$ improves long-term memory utilization, dynamic and static conflict handling, and complex retrieval performance, achieving relative improvements of more than 12\% in certain categories. These results highlight the effectiveness of jointly modeling memory evolution and adaptive evidence acquisition for long-term agent interactions.

</details>


### [84] ["You're Right, Let Me Fix It": How LLM Agents Damage Correct Work When Falsely Accused](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32616)
*Xutao Mao,Rui Qian,Longxiang Wang,Xinjian Yi,Mingxuan Li,Linghan Chen,Yudong Gao,Xiang Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"gaslight sycophancy"（谄媚式误信）与"destructive over-correction"（破坏性过度纠正）概念，并构建CAVE-Bench基准测试LLM智能体在无依据指控下是否错误否定已完成正确工作。实验显示14个前沿模型在Claude Code中最高60.06%的运行会损害正确工作，且更强模型在恢复证据后仍常如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长寿命智能体在任务成功后仍需继续运行（如压缩后恢复、交接接管），已完成的正确成果会持续收到后续输入，有时被错误指控为导致后续失败。智能体可能错误接受此类指控并破坏原本正确的工作，这是一个尚未被充分研究的安全挑战。

Method: 构建CAVE-Bench，包含6个领域365个智能体任务，围绕"不透明任务"设计：每次评测运行先达到验证正确的状态，其依据与历史保留在工作区，但能判定指控真假的事实存于智能体无法触及的外部/运行时状态。智能体无法本地验证，正确应对应保留成果并索要缺失证据。任务提供已完成的正确工作或让智能体自行构建验证，5个风险因素控制指控进入工作流的方；从轨迹中评分指控接受度与证据使用，并通过下游事件的确定性重放衡量损害。

Result: 在Claude Code中测试14个最新模型，假指控最高在60.06%的运行中损害正确工作；更强模型往往在已恢复支持证据后仍接受指控。同一模型在OpenCode、Codex、Hermes表现不同。基于基准实时信号驱动的harness gate可将重放损害降低74%。

Conclusion: 在无依据指控下保留已正确的工作，是长寿命智能体面临的一项独特安全挑战。项目地址：https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly keep working after a task succeeds as they resume after compaction or take over handoffs. Their finished work keeps receiving follow-up input that sometimes falsely accuses it for later failures. We call an agent's acceptance of such a false accusation gaslight sycophancy, and destructive over-correction when acting on it damages previously correct work. We introduce CAVE-Bench, a benchmark of 365 agentic tasks across six domains built around opaque tasks. Every scored run first reaches a verified correct state, whose supporting rationale and history stay in the workspace while the facts that would settle the accusation lie in external or runtime state beyond the agent's reach. The agent cannot confirm or refute the claim with a local check, so the right response should keep the work and ask for the missing evidence. Each task either hands the agent correct work with saved evidence or let it build and verify that work first, and five risk factors set how the accusation enters the workflow. We score accusation acceptance and evidence use from the trajectory and measure harm by deterministic replay of downstream events. Across 14 of the latest models in Claude Code, false accusations damage correct work in up to 60.06% of runs, and stronger models often do so after recovering the supporting evidence. The same model behaves differently across OpenCode, Codex, and Hermes, and a harness gate driven by the benchmark's live signals cuts replayed harm by 74%. These results show that preserving already-correct work under unsupported accusation is a distinct safety challenge for long-lived agents. Our project is in https://henrymao2004.github.io/agent-over-correction/.

</details>


### [85] [Prediction Limits and Koopman Closure of Geometry-Induced Soft State Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32652)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究几何诱导的软状态抽象何时允许精确的有限维线性动力学，给出可计算的最小RMSE下置信界，并分析Koopman特征函数与闭包性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨基于KAHM重建得分的软坐标表示能否被有限维线性动力学精确刻画，以及何时无法达到低预测误差。

Method: 用类特定Kernel Affine Hull Machine重建得分构造单纯形软坐标，用矩阵预测下一状态坐标；推导受谱范数约束下最小RMSE的可计算下置信界，结合类内后继坐标变化与软坐标偏离one-hot标签的程度，并分析闭包矩阵特征向量诱导的Koopman与伴随Koopman特征函数。

Result: 证明该界可由独立状态-后继对直接估计而无需拟合预测矩阵；样本量增大时证书几乎必然收敛到总体下界，低于该极限的容差最终被证明不可达；重建得分间隔控制软硬分配误差；四状态KAHM构造显示相同软坐标在一种动力学下可精确闭包、在另一种下必有正误差；在Duffing、Van der Pol、CartPole、MountainCar、Acrobot上进行实验。

Conclusion: 软坐标表示能否精确线性化取决于动力学；所提证书提供了无需拟合即可判定不可达误差容限的理论工具，但在基准实验中仅评估拟合模型，未数值验证排除证书。

Abstract: We study when geometry-induced soft state abstractions admit accurate finite-dimensional linear dynamics. Each state is represented by simplex-valued coordinates obtained from class-specific Kernel Affine Hull Machine (KAHM) reconstruction scores, and a matrix is used to predict the next-state coordinates. Our main result is a computable lower confidence bound on the minimum root-mean-square prediction error over all matrices satisfying a prescribed spectral-norm limit. The bound combines within-class variation of successor coordinates with the deviation of soft coordinates from their one-hot reference labels, and can be evaluated from independent state-successor pairs without fitting a prediction matrix. For fixed coordinates and evaluation distribution, the certificate converges almost surely to a population lower bound as the sample size grows; any tolerance below this limit is eventually certified unattainable. Reconstruction-score margins further control the soft-to-hard assignment error. Under deterministic dynamics and exact coordinate closure, eigenvectors of the closure matrix and its reduced transpose induce Koopman and adjoint Koopman eigenfunctions, respectively. A four-state KAHM construction shows that identical soft coordinates can permit exact closure under one dynamics map yet force positive prediction error under another. Experiments on Duffing, Van der Pol, CartPole, MountainCar, and Acrobot compare direct soft-coordinate prediction with state-space DMD/EDMD baselines and report prediction, representation-variation, and spectral diagnostics. The benchmarks assess fitted models but do not numerically evaluate the exclusion certificate.

</details>


### [86] [World Agent: Can Language Models Keep a World Running?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32692)
*Weixing Chen,Weipeng Zhang,Nan An,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"世界智能体"任务与 WorldAgent-Benchmark，将世界模型评估从"生成交付"转向"持续运行"，用维护与推理两条赛道衡量事件组织与因果推进能力，发现现有8个模型在结构移除下得分持续下降，因果检查最弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型评估止步于生成、交付或单步转移，无法保证正确组织的因果事件流；局部状态转移正确不等于世界能持续正确运行。

Method: 提出 world agent 任务：模型不负责生成世界，而负责维持世界运行，需协调事件并传递其后果以约束后续演化。实例化为 WorldAgent-Benchmark，含两条赛道：维护赛道（将连续叙事事件落地为显式世界状态的正确转移，遵守因果、时间与并发约束）和推理赛道（部分观测下预测世界演化并朝目标行动）。维护赛道结合 LLM 辅助语义判断与程序化验证评分，推理赛道完全程序化评估；判断可审计，分数可由保存的判断与执行记录复算。

Result: 在8个模型上，随着预置结构被移除，得分持续下降；因果关系检查是所有模型最弱的环节。

Conclusion: 该基准使世界的持续运行变得可度量，并能区分局部完成与事件组织层面的失败。

Abstract: World models are moving from generating realistic frames to generating playable worlds, yet whether a delivered world can keep running is not tested anywhere. Existing evaluations stop at generation, at delivery, or at single-step transitions, and each stops at a different point along the way. Correct local state transitions or intermediate outcomes do not guarantee a correctly organized causal event flow. We propose the world agent task, which moves the evaluation point of world generation from the moment of delivery to the continued operation that follows. In this task, a model is not asked to generate a world. It is held responsible for keeping the world running, which requires coordinating events and carrying forward their consequences to constrain subsequent evolution. We instantiate the task in WorldAgent-Benchmark with two complementary tracks. In the maintenance track, the model must ground the events of a continuous narrative into correct transitions of the explicit world state while respecting causal, temporal, and concurrency constraints. In the deduction track, the model must predict how the world will evolve under partial observations and act toward a goal. The maintenance track combines LLM-assisted semantic judgments with programmatic validation and scoring, while the deduction track is evaluated entirely programmatically. Individual judgments are auditable against world states and execution logs, and scores can be recomputed from the saved judgments and execution records. Across 8 models, scores decline steadily as pre-built structure is removed from the world, and causal-relation checking is the weakest component for every model. The benchmark makes the continued operation of a world measurable and distinguishes local completion from failures in event organization. Code and dataset will be released on https://github.com/HCPLab-SYSU/WorldAgent-Benchmark.

</details>


### [87] [Mandela-Bench: Multimodal Models Remember Canonical Images Instead of Seeing Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32763)
*Yicheng Bao,Zhenkun Gao,Xiahui Guo,Mingqian Yang,Xueheng Li,Bangwei Liu,Mingang Chen,Lijun Li,Xuhong Wang,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Mandela-Bench基准，用1,507个经典图像编辑测试多模态模型能否用世界知识验证图像真实性。36个模型普遍失败：识别的经典图像会偏置验证，使模型倾向记忆中的原图而非实际观察到的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图像现可被单指令无缝编辑，几乎不留像素痕迹，唯一操纵证据可能是图像所描绘内容的事实性。现有基准依赖生成器痕迹、图文矛盾、视觉不合理或外部参考，无法测试模型是否能用自身世界知识验证已识别的图像。

Method: 构建Mandela-Bench，含1,507个经典图像编辑：1,359个仅知识型伪造（每个违背一个可验证事实）、148个无锚点对照（保留编辑过程但不引入事实矛盾）、474个未修改原图。评分不仅检测伪造，还评估解释是否指出被插入实体或被违背事实。测试36个多模态模型（0.8B至前沿规模）。

Result: 发现一致失败模式：知名人物从熟悉照片中移除后，模型仍在高达72.7%的响应中说出该人物。部分模型单独展示时能区分替换人脸与原脸，却仍将完整编辑照片判为真实。提供真实事件与日期不改善知识型检测，而裁剪掉可识别构图后提供相同信息则改善。即使明确验证提示，36个模型中仅1个在一半以上伪造图像上满足KGR标准。

Conclusion: 失败不能仅用知识缺失或感知不足解释，而与"识别将验证偏向记忆中的经典图像而非观察到的编辑"这一机制一致。

Abstract: Historical photographs and other canonical images can now be edited seamlessly with a single instruction, often leaving no reliable pixel-level trace. In such cases, the only evidence of manipulation may be a fact about what the image depicts. Existing benchmarks instead rely on generator artefacts, image-caption inconsistencies, visual implausibilities, or external references, and therefore do not test whether a model can use its own world knowledge to verify a recognized image. We introduce Mandela-Bench, containing 1,507 edits of canonical images: 1,359 knowledge-only forgeries, each contradicting one verifiable fact, and 148 anchor-free controls that preserve the editing process without introducing a factual contradiction, together with 474 untouched originals. We score not only whether a model detects a forgery, but whether its explanation identifies the inserted entity or the fact being violated. Across 36 multimodal models, from 0.8B parameters to frontier scale, we find a consistent failure mode. When a public figure is removed from a familiar photograph, models still name that person in up to 72.7% of responses. Some models can distinguish the replacement face from the original when shown in isolation, yet still judge the full edited photograph as authentic. Providing the true event and date does not improve knowledge-grounded detection, whereas providing the same information after cropping away the recognizable composition does. Even under explicit verification prompts, only one of the 36 models meets the KGR criterion on at least half of the forged images. These results suggest that the failures cannot be explained by missing knowledge or inadequate perception alone. Instead, they are consistent with recognition biasing verification toward the remembered canonical image rather than the observed edit.

</details>


### [88] [$T^5$: Twin-Critic Training for Token-Level Thoughts in Reinforcement Mid-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32791)
*Nan Qiao,Yebin Yang,Weinong Wang,Shuning Wang,Shangpin Peng,Fengyuan Lu,Xinming Wang,Zhehan Kan,Ruixu Zhang,Songyang Zhang,Sheng Yue,Yonglong Tian,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双critic方法\tfour{}，通过单条轨迹校准token级优势估计，提升强化mid-training的效率和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化mid-training中token级信用分配困难：现有group-relative方法需重复生成，成本高；学习型critic虽能单次rollout反馈，但准确的回报预测不足以保证可靠策略更新。训练-推理不匹配与PPO裁剪使优势估计的公共偏移无法抵消，导致更新漂移。

Method: twin-critic方法：warmup和held-out资格筛选后，两个critic给出两个优势估计，通过条件矩鞍点目标学习动作相关权重进行组合；目标使每个前缀平均优势趋向零，同时用信号保留约束防止修正抹除学习信号；跨文本位置共享信息避免重复采样前缀。

Result: 相比SOTA无critic方法，平均基准性能提升7.8%，平均训练步时间最多降低63.4%。理论上刻画了信号保留约束下的最优混合，并给出残差均值诱导漂移的上界。

Conclusion: \tfour{}有效解决token级信用分配与更新漂移问题，在性能与效率上均优于现有方法，并提供理论保证。

Abstract: Reinforcement mid-training lets language models learn internal thoughts from unlabeled text, but efficient token-level credit assignment remains challenging. Existing group-relative methods require costly repeated generation. Learned critics offer single-rollout feedback, but accurate return prediction alone does not ensure reliable policy updates. Our analysis shows how training--inference mismatch and PPO clipping prevent a common offset in advantage estimates from cancelling out, introducing additional update drift. We propose \tfour{}, a twin-critic method that calibrates token-level advantages from a single generated trajectory. After warmup and held-out qualification, the critics provide two advantage estimates, combined using action-dependent weights learned through a conditional-moment saddle-point objective. This objective brings the average advantage at each prefix toward zero, while a signal-retention constraint prevents the correction from erasing the learning signal. Sharing information across text positions avoids repeated sampling of each prefix. Theoretically, we characterize optimal mixing under the signal-retention constraint and establish an upper bound on residual mean-induced drift. Experiments show that, compared with the state-of-the-art critic-free method, \tfour{} improves mean benchmark performance by 7.8\% and reduces mean training-step time by up to 63.4\%.

</details>


### [89] [Can LLMs Predict the Future? A Brier Score Analysis of Prediction Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32885)
*Yuanbo Li,Zekun Li,Xiaoyan cong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RMF基准（3000个已解决二元问题，覆盖9个领域），用零样本协议评估6个Claude与Qwen模型，发现模型升级并不稳定提升概率估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"模型升级/更高档位是否能持续提升预测市场问题的概率估计精度"这一常见假设。

Method: 构建RMF基准：3000个已解决二元问题、9个领域；仅给问题、零样本协议；评估6个Claude与Qwen变体；用Brier分数对比经验基率预测器，做Murphy分解、配对差异检验，并按事件类别与训练截止时间分层。

Result: 后截止分层中，4个Claude模型Brier为0.183–0.192，比基率参考好0.024–0.033；Qwen 32B未显著优于基率，但比7B低0.024；Claude版本与档位升级均无显著提升；事件类别间的组内差异大于Claude变体间差异。

Conclusion: 预测分数应结合简单概率基线与问题构成来解读；在该协议下，更新版本或更高档位并不必然带来更准确的概率。

Abstract: We study whether model upgrades improve probability estimates for prediction-market questions. Our Resolved Market Forecasting (RMF) benchmark contains 3,000 resolved binary questions across nine domains, on which we evaluate six Claude and Qwen model variants using a question-only, zero-shot protocol. We assess Brier scores relative to an empirical base-rate predictor, examine their Murphy decomposition, and compare models through paired differences, with results stratified by event category and timing relative to training cutoffs. In the reported post-cutoff stratum, the four Claude models achieve Brier scores of 0.183-0.192, improving on the base-rate reference by 0.024-0.033. Qwen 32B does not significantly outperform that reference, although its paired Brier is 0.024 lower than that of the 7B checkpoint. The evaluated Claude version and tier upgrades yield no significant improvement. Within-model differences across event categories exceed the observed differences among Claude variants. These results show why forecasting scores should be interpreted alongside simple probability baselines and question composition: under this protocol, newer versions or higher model tiers do not consistently produce more accurate probabilities.

</details>


### [90] [Temporal Graph Learning of Wearable Actigraphy and Sleep Traces for Modelling Adolescent Crystallized Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33428)
*Md. Tanvir Rahman,Nabil Anan Orka,Asaduzzaman Khan,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出SATURN模型，利用Fitbit可穿戴设备数据预测青少年晶体智力，在6091名ABCD研究样本上取得优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备提供了可扩展、生态效度高的评估方式，但从不规则依从性的设备数据中预测青少年晶体智力（Gc）面临数据缺失与行为-环境交互复杂的挑战。

Method: SATURN将参与者表示为21节点时序图，编码每日行为与时序邻接；前向传播中动态剪除无效日边以规避插补误差；用残差GATv2层精炼节点嵌入，经掩码注意力池化聚合，并融合社会人口学协变量。

Result: 在家庭控制、年龄-性别-BMI分层的交叉验证下，SATURN达到R²=0.2783±0.0127，优于梯度提升（0.2372）和BiLSTM（0.2688）。可解释性分析显示轻度活动、代谢当量和睡眠时长为主要预测因子，蒙特卡洛dropout与亚组分析验证跨社会人口层的公平性。

Conclusion: SATURN为数字认知表型建立了严谨的计算框架，提供可扩展途径补充传统评估，并揭示宏观行为异常。

Abstract: Wearable actigraphy offers a scalable, ecologically valid alternative to episodic clinical assessment. However, predicting continuous adolescent crystallized intelligence ($G_c$) from such traces remains challenging due to irregular device adherence and complex behavioral-environmental interactions. We address this using daily summary data derived from 21-day Fitbit records of 6,091 adolescents in the Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development Study (Release 5.1). We propose SATURN, a Sleep-Activity Temporal Unified Regression Network. It represents participants as 21-node temporal graphs encoding daily behaviors and temporal adjacency. To prevent imputation artifacts, invalid-day edges are dynamically pruned during forward passes. Node embeddings are refined via residual GATv2 layers, aggregated through masked attention pooling, and fused with sociodemographic covariates. Under family-controlled, age-sex-BMI-stratified cross-validation, SATURN achieves $R^2 = 0.2783 \pm 0.0127$, consistently improving upon flattened machine learning (Gradient Boosting, $R^2 = 0.2372$) and sequential deep learning (BiLSTM, $R^2 = 0.2688$) baselines. Explainability analyses identify light activity, metabolic equivalents, and sleep duration as dominant predictors, while Monte Carlo dropout and subgroup analyses confirm equitable performance across sociodemographic strata. Ultimately, SATURN establishes a rigorous computational framework for digital cognitive phenotyping, offering a scalable pathway to complement traditional assessments by highlighting macro-level behavioral anomalies.

</details>


### [91] [MAC-Net: A Multi-Task Deep Learning Framework for Modeling Cognitive Function From Task-Based fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33440)
*Md. Tanvir Rahman,Nabil Anan Orka,Asaduzzaman Khan,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAC-Net是一个协变量感知的深度学习框架，从区域任务态fMRI建模个体认知功能，通过隔离神经特征与协变量路径，在ABCD 6500名青少年上实现优于基线的认知预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于神经信号的客观认知评估对神经康复有重要价值，但从任务态fMRI进行个体级预测困难，因为神经特征与人口学及扫描仪相关变异共存，主导协变量可能压制高维临床表征的学习。

Method: 提出Multi-task Activation and Contrast Network (MAC-Net)：将tfMRI特征分离到专用神经通路，将参与者变量限制在末端晚期融合通路，防止协变量压制神经表征。在ABCD研究6500名参与者基线数据上，采用家庭感知交叉验证，与线性模型、随机森林及替代深度架构对比。

Result: N-back+MID配置在流体、晶体和总认知上R²分别为0.174、0.238、0.277，超过仅协变量基线(0.178)及替代深度模型(0.217)。N-back是最有信息量的范式，加入Stop Signal Task略降低性能。Integrated Gradients、DeepLIFT和Input Gradient归因高度一致，定位到工作记忆相关的额叶、顶叶和扣带回区域。

Conclusion: 协变量感知的多任务建模能产生可复现的认知功能估计，为临床转化建立了稳健的神经工程框架。

Abstract: Objective cognitive assessment from neural signals supports neurorehabilitation, but individual-level prediction from task-based fMRI (tfMRI) remains difficult because neural features coexist with substantial demographic and scanner-related variation. We present the Multi-task Activation and Contrast Network (MAC-Net), a covariate-aware deep learning framework for modeling individual cognitive function from regional tfMRI. By isolating tfMRI features into a dedicated neural pathway and restricting participant variables to a terminal late-fusion pathway, MAC-Net prevents dominant covariates from suppressing high-dimensional clinical representations during feature learning. Evaluating baseline data from 6,500 Adolescent Brain Cognitive Development Study participants under family-aware cross-validation, MAC-Net was benchmarked against linear models, random forests, and alternative deep architectures. The N-back plus Monetary Incentive Delay configuration achieved $R^{2}$ values of 0.174, 0.238, and 0.277 for fluid, crystallized, and total cognition, outperforming covariate-only baselines (0.178) and alternative deep models (0.217). N-back was the most informative paradigm, whereas incorporating the Stop Signal Task marginally degraded performance. Feature attributions via Integrated Gradients, DeepLIFT, and Input Gradient were highly concordant, localizing working-memory-related frontal, parietal, and cingulate regions. These findings demonstrate that covariate-aware multi-task modeling yields reproducible cognitive-function estimations, establishing a robust neural engineering framework for clinical translation.

</details>


### [92] [LiveOption: Evaluating LLM Agents in Structured Option Trading with Nonlinear Payoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33470)
*Haochen Luo,Yifan Li,Binh Minh An,Xiaolong Luo,Zhengzhao Lai,Yuan Zhang,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LiveOption，一个针对期权交易的LLM智能体评估框架，涵盖结构化决策、三类任务套件与分层指标，发现现有智能体多数场景下难以取得有竞争力的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM与多智能体系统的金融决策评估多集中于股票交易且仅考察方向预测，忽略了期权市场的结构复杂性——非线性收益与多腿策略构建需要结构化决策而非简单方向性押注。

Method: 将问题建模为现实执行与资金约束下的结构化序贯决策，提供可复现环境与标准化交互协议；设计三类任务套件（组合叠加、事件驱动财报交易、0DTE日内交易）；提出分层指标套件评估动作有效性、决策质量、风险特征与结果层表现。

Result: 实验表明，当前智能体在大多数场景下难以实现有竞争力的收益。

Conclusion: LiveOption为超越结果导向指标的结构化决策评估提供了有原则的测试平台。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown promise in financial decision-making, yet existing evaluations focus on equity trading and primarily assess directional prediction, overlooking the structural complexity of derivative markets. Option trading introduces fundamentally different challenges, including nonlinear payoffs and multi-leg strategy construction, requiring structured decisions rather than simple directional bets. We introduce LiveOption, an evaluation framework for LLM-based agents in option trading. LiveOption formulates the problem as structured sequential decision-making under realistic execution and capital constraints, and provides a reproducible environment with standardized interaction protocols. The framework includes three task suites covering portfolio overlays, event-driven earnings trading, and 0DTE intraday trading. We further propose a hierarchical metric suite that evaluates action validity, decision quality, risk characteristics, and outcome-level performance. Experiments show that current agents often fail to achieve competitive returns in most scenarios. LiveOption offers a principled testbed for evaluating structured decision-making beyond outcome-based metrics.

</details>


### [93] [PPG-LM: A Photoplethysmography-Language Model with Multi-Level Clinical Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33516)
*Xiaoda Wang,Minxiao Wang,Maxwell A Xu,Patrick Langer,Kaiqiao Han,Defu Cao,Xiao Luo,Yuzhe Yang,Yan Liu,Xiao Hu,Yizhou Sun,Wei Wang,Carl Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPG-LM是首个PPG-语言模型家族，通过两阶段框架对齐PPG信号与来自EHR的临床上下文（片段、事件、就诊级别），在73k小时PPG上预训练，支持语言识别、跨模态检索和片段描述，并在多个数据集上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPG可大规模采集连续生理信息，但现有PPG基础模型仅依赖任务特定预测头，而LLM的医学知识难以直接迁移到波形理解。需要模型同时从信号监督和更广泛的EHR临床上下文中学习，并在不同时间尺度上对齐信息。

Method: 提出PPG-LM，首个PPG-语言模型家族。构建自动描述生成流程，从信号测量和结构化EHR生成片段级、事件级、就诊级文本。采用两阶段框架：第一阶段通过对比学习和波形条件描述建立片段-语言对应；第二阶段通过时间感知聚合和时间语句匹配扩展到事件与就诊级对齐。

Result: 在约73k小时PPG上预训练，支持语言识别、跨模态检索和片段描述。在MC-MED、MIMIC-III和VitalDB上，检索和描述事实性优于语言模型基线，并在多个临床预测任务上优于PPG和时间序列基础模型。

Conclusion: PPG-LM通过将PPG信号与多尺度EHR临床上下文对齐，实现了生理表征学习，为大规模临床评估提供了可扩展的PPG-语言模型方案。

Abstract: Photoplethysmography (PPG) is widely recorded by clinical monitors and consumer wearables, providing a scalable source of continuous physiological information. These recordings offer an opportunity for physiological assessment at scale, but realizing this potential requires models to learn from both signal-derived physiological supervision and broader clinical context captured in electronic health records (EHRs). This involves aligning information spanning local observations, care events, and entire visits with PPG representations at corresponding temporal scales. However, existing PPG foundation models primarily rely on task-specific prediction heads, while the medical knowledge of large language models does not necessarily translate into waveform understanding. To bridge this gap, we introduce PPG-LM, the first PPG-language model family to learn physiological representations from both signal-derived supervision and broader clinical context captured in EHRs. To construct clinically grounded captions, we develop an automatic captioning pipeline that generates segment-, event-, and visit-level descriptions from signal measurements and structured EHR records. We then learn from these pairs through a two-stage framework that first establishes segment-language correspondence through contrastive learning and waveform-conditioned captioning, then extends alignment to events and visits through time-aware aggregation and temporal statement matching. Pretrained on approximately 73k hours of PPG, PPG-LM supports language-based recognition, cross-modal retrieval, and segment captioning. Experiments on MC-MED, MIMIC-III, and VitalDB show improved retrieval and caption factuality over language-model baselines and gains over PPG and time-series foundation models on multiple clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [94] [OSCC: Certified Observation-Safe Coupling Optimization for Gradient-Noise Control in Imperfect-Information Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33543)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Rui Chen,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出观测安全反事实耦合(OSCC)框架，定义合法耦合类并导出梯度感知选择准则，用于不完全信息环境下的策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实动作比较中，耦合式rollout可降低噪声，但标准公共随机数构造存在两大问题：一是非法耦合可能泄露隐藏状态、同步内生策略随机性或使机会事件错位；二是多动作优化中，目标并非降低收益对比方差，而是经策略梯度几何投影后的收益协方差矩阵。

Method: OSCC通过边际保持、信息状态安全、分支局部策略随机性、语义事件对齐、trace-before-oracle回放定义合法耦合类；导出梯度感知耦合准则，证明边际保持下策略梯度噪声变化由策略雅可比加权的非对角收益协方差决定；据此提出OSCC-Select，一种仅需校准的选择器，在独立、仅根、仅续、全耦合rollout间用安全与增益证书选择，增益目标结合投影梯度噪声与实测采样成本，未通过同时下置信界认证时回退独立采样。

Result: Leduc 10万固定根比较中，全耦合CP-GRPO将收益对比方差从41.1158降至18.1441(降55.87%)并保持分支边际；三动作下OSCC-Select选continuation coupling，梯度噪声trace 0.0783优于收益方差选择的0.0917；校准组从64增至2048，认证率由0.327升至0.995。

Conclusion: OSCC为不完全信息博弈提供可认证的反事实耦合学习原语，兼顾安全性与梯度增益，OSCC-Select可在有限校准下自适应选择耦合方式并可靠回退。

Abstract: Coupled rollouts can reduce the noise of counterfactual action comparisons, but two issues prevent standard common-random-number constructions from serving as a general learning primitive in imperfect-information environments. First, an invalid coupling may expose hidden state, synchronize endogenous policy randomness, or misalign chance events after counterfactual histories diverge. Second, in multi-action policy optimization, lower return-contrast variance is not by itself the relevant objective: the optimizer depends on the return covariance matrix after projection through the local policy-gradient geometry. We introduce observation-safe counterfactual coupling (OSCC), a framework that defines an admissible class through marginal preservation, information-state safety, branch-local policy randomness, semantic event alignment, and trace-before-oracle replay. We derive a gradient-aware coupling criterion showing that, for marginal-preserving couplings, policy-gradient noise changes are determined by policy-Jacobian-weighted off-diagonal return covariance. This motivates OSCC-Select, a calibration-only selector that chooses among independent, root-only, continuation-only, and fully coupled rollouts using separate safety and gain certificates. Its gain target combines projected gradient noise with measured physical sampling cost and falls back to independent sampling whenever a simultaneous lower confidence bound does not certify improvement. On 100,000 fixed-root Leduc comparisons, the fully coupled CP-GRPO instantiation reduces return-contrast variance from 41.1158 to 18.1441, a 55.87% reduction, while preserving the declared branch marginals. With three actions, OSCC-Select chooses continuation coupling and attains gradient-noise trace 0.0783 versus 0.0917 for return-variance selection. Increasing calibration from 64 to 2,048 groups raises certification from 0.327 to 0.995.

</details>


### [95] [EHRAdapt: Adapting Pretrained Language Models to Electronic Health Records with Semantic Priors for Rare Clinical Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34007)
*Andre R Goncalves,Vincent Liu,Priyadip Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EHRAdapt是一个轻量适配器，将EHR的(time, modality, code)元组直接映射进冻结LLM的嵌入空间，通过语义先验与证据残差互补表示事件向量，仅训练0.1–0.6%参数即可在多个任务上超越文本序列化与计数基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR天然是(time, modality, code)元组结构，而预训练语言模型只接受文本token；将其序列化为文本会显著拉长序列长度并冗余编码结构信息。同时，临床词表呈长尾分布，罕见事件观测太少，难以直接学习可靠的事件向量。

Method: 提出EHRAdapt适配器：将元组直接映射进冻结LLM嵌入空间——模态用学习嵌入、时间间隔用学习注意力偏置、事件码用专用向量。事件向量表示为“语义先验+证据残差”：先验来自生物医学语言模型对临床描述文本的冻结嵌入，经共享可学习投影映射入模型输入空间；残差为低秩的事件特定修正。在约400万患者记录上对三个冻结骨干(OLMo2 1B、Llama3.2 1B、OLMo2 7B)做持续预训练，仅训练适配器。

Result: 完整适配器在每个骨干的留出下一事件预测上均优于所有消融。移除语义通路对罕见事件的损害是最频繁事件的十倍以上；移除残差通路损害整体预测却改善最罕见事件。在法定传染病与症状监测下游分类任务上，EHRAdapt优于基于文本的LLM和基于计数的基线，且两条通路均提升罕见疾病判别能力。

Conclusion: 语义先验与证据残差两条通路扮演互补角色：前者支撑罕见事件，后者利于极罕见事件的精细刻画，这种互补性只有在按事件频率分层分析时才可见，而非被平均值掩盖。

Abstract: Electronic health records (EHRs) encode clinical histories as (time, modality, code) tuples, whereas pretrained language models expect text tokens. Serializing them as text inflates sequence length and redundantly encodes structure. We introduce EHRAdapt, an adapter that maps tuples directly into a frozen language model's embedding space. Modality receives a learned embedding, time gaps enter through learned attention biases, and event codes receive dedicated vectors. Learning event vectors is the central challenge: clinical vocabularies are long-tailed, leaving rare events too few observations for reliable estimates. EHRAdapt therefore represents each event vector as the sum of a semantic prior and an evidence residual. The prior is a frozen embedding of the event's clinical description from a biomedical language model trained on clinical ontologies, mapped into the model's input space by a shared learned projection, so it supplies clinical meaning even when observations are scarce. The residual, a learned low-rank event-specific correction, refines it as evidence accumulates. We run continued pretraining on about 4 million patients' records with three frozen LLM backbones (OLMo2 1B, Llama3.2 1B, and OLMo2 7B), training only the adapter (0.1--0.6% of all parameters). The full adapter outperforms all ablations in held-out next-event prediction on every backbone. Removing the semantic pathway hurts rare events over ten times more than the most frequent ones, whereas removing the residual hurts overall prediction but improves it for the rarest events. On reportable infectious-disease and syndromic downstream classification tasks, EHRAdapt outperforms text-based LLM and count-based baselines, and both pathways improve rare-disease discrimination. The two pathways therefore play complementary roles, visible only when results are broken down by event frequency rather than averaged.

</details>


### [96] [Towards Certificate-Driven Software Porting: A Self-Improving Agentic Harness for Scientific Program Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34069)
*Piyush Jha,Aishik Ghosh,Vijay Ganesh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出证书驱动的进化搜索(CDES)，通过记录失败经验的"证书"约束后续候选，用于将Geant4 CPU代码翻译为GPU实现，实现13.78x与23.54x加速，正确率由55%提升至90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型科学代码库的升级与重写一直是重大挑战。现有基于LLM的进化搜索虽能移植和加速遗留代码，但仅在提示中加入修复反馈，无法阻止后续候选重复相同错误。

Method: CDES在进化搜索中引入可执行的约束，来源为失败候选，记录为"证书"（假设、检查器证据、正当化限制）。控制逻辑通过拒绝、回溯和针对性修复来强制这些约束，同时保留兼容的编辑。应用于Geant4两个粒子模拟函数的CPU到GPU翻译，评测采用超越单元测试的harness，结合形式化检查、数值比较、物理检查与GPU安全性测试。

Result: 生成的实现相比CPU代码在函数级分别实现13.78x和23.54x加速（含数据转换与传输）；其中一个函数的GPU吞吐量超过专家实现14.9%，结合互补组件后达16.1%。消融实验中，证书反馈使通过必需正确性检查的候选比例从55%提升到90%。

Conclusion: 证书驱动的进化搜索能有效防止错误重复，提升LLM代码移植的成功率与性能，在多维度严格评测下优于专家实现。

Abstract: The upgrade and rewriting of large scientific codebases has traditionally been a major challenge. While evolutionary search with large language models (LLMs) can port and accelerate legacy code, repair feedback in prompts alone does not prevent subsequent candidates from repeating the same errors. We introduce Certificate-Driven Evolutionary Search (CDES), which extends evolutionary search with enforceable restrictions derived from failed candidates, recorded as certificates of assumptions, checker evidence, and justified restrictions. Its control logic enforces these restrictions through rejection, backtracking, and targeted repair while preserving compatible edits. We apply CDES to CPU-to-GPU translation of two particle-simulation functions from the Geant4 toolkit, evaluated with a harness that goes beyond unit tests to combine formal checks, numerical comparisons, physics checks, and GPU safety tests. Generated implementations achieve 13.78x and 23.54x function-level speedups over CPU code, including data conversion and transfers; for one function, GPU throughput exceeds an expert implementation by 14.9%, reaching 16.1% when complementary components are combined. In an ablation over execution settings, certificate feedback increases the fraction of candidates passing required correctness checks from 55% to 90%.

</details>


### [97] [QuantaSpike: Short-Window Spike-Driven Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34259)
*Bang Hu,Guowei Zhu,Changze Lv,Xiaoqing Zheng,Fengzhe Zhang,Fan Zhang,Wei Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: QuantaSpike 是一个短窗口脉冲驱动量化框架，基于对数三值积分放电(LTIF)神经元，实现高精度、低能耗的 LLM 脉冲推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 推理依赖密集 MAC 运算，能耗高；SNN 虽事件驱动、能耗低，但脉冲驱动 LLM 推理困难，因为激活存在大量离群值，需长放电窗口或额外非脉冲路径。

Method: 提出 LTIF 神经元，采用三值事件与 2 的幂膜响应量子，提升单次放电信息量并保持 shift-ACC 兼容计算；结合组自适应增益与选择性离群值准入：正常值用残差 LTIF 步，准入离群值额外发放一次起始脉冲后进入相同残差动态。

Result: 在 OPT 和 Llama-2 上取得脉冲驱动 LLM 量化方法中 SOTA 或具竞争力的困惑度与零样本精度；在相同四步放电窗口下，Llama-3-8B 与 Qwen3-8B 上接近 FP16；线性能耗投影显示相比 SpikeQuant，OPT 降低约 80.0%、Llama-2 降低约 67.1% 的单次线性变换能耗。

Conclusion: QuantaSpike 为 LLM 推理提供了精确且节能的脉冲驱动路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance across many tasks but rely on dense multiply-accumulate (MAC) operations during inference, resulting in high energy cost. Spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer an event-driven alternative in which synaptic integration uses lightweight accumulation. However, spike-driven LLM inference remains difficult because outlier-heavy activations typically require long firing windows or auxiliary non-spiking paths. We propose QuantaSpike, a short-window spike-driven quantization framework for LLMs built around Logarithmic Ternary Integrate-and-Fire (LTIF) neurons. LTIF uses ternary events with power-of-two membrane-response quanta, improving the information represented by each firing step while retaining shift-ACC-compatible computation. QuantaSpike combines this neuron with group-adaptive gain and selective outlier admission: normal values use residual LTIF steps, whereas admitted outliers receive one additional onset spike before entering the same residual dynamics. Across OPT and Llama-2, QuantaSpike achieves state-of-the-art or competitive perplexity and zero-shot accuracy among spike-driven LLM quantization methods. It also transfers to newer dense LLMs, remaining close to the FP16 reference on Llama-3-8B and Qwen3-8B under the same four-step firing window. Analytical linear-energy projections show that QuantaSpike reduces the energy of one linear transformation by about $80.0\%$ on OPT models and $67.1\%$ on Llama-2 models relative to SpikeQuant, providing an accurate and energy-efficient spike-driven path for LLM inference.

</details>


### [98] [BIABench: Evaluating AI agents on real-world bioimage analysis tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34274)
*Zixuan Pan,Davide Panzeri,Lukas Johanns,Marilin Moor,Yu Zhou,Hedi Peterson,Yiyu Shi,Jianxu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BIABench是首个端到端评测AI agent进行真实生物图像分析的基准，含16项源自已发表研究的任务。常规2D任务表现良好，但3D/时序任务得分极低（≤0.19），且结果可靠性差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent有望自动化生物图像分析，但缺少能评估其端到端完成真实分析能力的基准。真实分析困难在于2D/3D/时序图像过大无法作为上下文读取，agent需通过代码、专用软件和渲染视图选择并执行分析。已发表研究将原始图像与同行评审结果配对，使该能力可测。

Method: 构建BIABench，含16个从已发表生物学研究重建的任务，保留原始科学问题、成像数据和ground truth，覆盖11个分析子任务和从H&E组织学到单分子定位显微的多种模态。每份提交获两项评分：结果分数（用领域标准指标对比输出文件与ground truth）和过程分数（由视觉语言模型依据专家编写的评分标准评判方法选择和质量控制）。评测通用及生物学专用agent，跨多个语言模型并重复运行每个任务。

Result: 常规2D任务被较好地解决，但涉及第三维或时间轴的任务中，无agent得分超过0.19。生物学专业化、更强模型或详细专家指令均未能弥合该差距。agent不可靠：同一agent重复运行间的分数差异大于不同agent间的差异；且无ground truth时，无法通过过程分数或耗时区分正确与错误的运行。

Conclusion: BIABench公开数据与代码，为评估并最终训练agent进行可靠的长时程生物图像分析提供了可验证框架。

Abstract: Artificial-intelligence (AI) agents hold promise for automating bioimage analysis, yet no benchmark evaluates whether they can carry out real-world analyses end to end. Such analyses are hard for agents because 2D images, 3D volumes and time-lapse sequences are often too large to read as context, so an agent must choose and run an analysis through code, specialized software and rendered views. Published studies make this capability testable, because each pairs raw images with a peer-reviewed result. We introduce BIABench, a benchmark of 16 tasks reconstructed from published biological studies that retain their scientific questions, imaging data and ground truth. The tasks span eleven analysis subtasks and modalities from H&E histology to single-molecule localization microscopy. Each submission receives an outcome score, which compares the output files with the ground truth using field-standard metrics, and a process score, in which a vision-language model judges method choice and quality control against an expert-written rubric. We evaluated general-purpose and biology-specific agents across several language models, with repeated runs of every task. Routine two-dimensional tasks were solved well, but on some tasks that added a third dimension or a time axis no agent scored above 0.19. Neither biological specialization, stronger models nor detailed expert instructions closed this gap. The agents were also unreliable, with scores varying more between repeated runs of one agent than between different agents, and without ground truth a correct run could not be told from a wrong one by its process score or by the time spent. Released openly with its data and code, BIABench provides a verifiable framework for evaluating, and eventually training, agents for reliable long-horizon bioimage analysis.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond End-to-End Black Box Mapping: An Intentional Agent Framework for Cognitive-driven Facial Reaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34419)
*Hanzhong Zhang,Jindong Wang,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Intentional Agent，将自动面部反应生成（FRG）从直接刺激-反应映射重构为经显式内部状态中介的生成过程，通过内部动力学模型与 Inner Thought Flow 实现拟人化面部反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能进行人机交互的智能系统需要自动的拟人面部反应生成。然而多数现有 FRG 方法采用端到端架构，直接将说话者行为映射为听者表情，缺乏显式中间内部状态，难以反映人类交互中的潜在评估与情感过程。

Method: 将 FRG 重构为结构化内部状态中介的生成任务，提出 Intentional Agent。设计内部动力学模型，将情感驱动力与迭代的 Inner Thought Flow（ITF）整合进结构化中间状态，该状态可在对话沉默期间持续更新；并将 FRG 形式化为从潜在思维流到面部表情的下游情感映射，以衔接抽象内部状态与生理动作。此外提出 Reaction Quality Scorer（RQS）作为自动指标。

Result: 在 REACT 2025 数据集上达到 FRDist 72.39、FRDiv 0.5057。96 个反应的盲测人类评分显示，Full 方法与真实值均值无显著差异（5.527 vs. 5.195，pHolm=.076），而 Full 显著优于 Event-Triggered 与 Heuristic-Only（两者 pHolm<.001）。RQS 与人类判断强相关（Pearson r=.855；Spearman ρ=.821，均 p<.05）。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了内生动力学在构建高度自主、拟人化智能体方面的巨大潜力。RQS 可作为有效的自动评估指标。

Abstract: Automatic human-like facial reaction generation (FRG) is essential for building intelligent systems that can engage in human-computer interaction (HCI). While diverse and context-appropriate facial reactions can reflect latent appraisal and affective processes in human interaction, most existing FRG methods rely on end-to-end architectures that directly map speaker behaviours to listener expressions without an explicit intermediate internal state. We reformulate FRG as generation mediated by a structured internal-state process and propose the \textbf{Intentional Agent}, which shifts FRG from direct stimulus-response mapping to stimulus-grounded generation through explicit intermediate states. To represent temporal internal-state evolution, we propose an internal dynamics model that integrates emotional drives with an iterative Inner Thought Flow (ITF) within a structured intermediate state used for subsequent generation. This state can continue to update during conversational silences. Furthermore, to bridge abstract internal states with physiological actions, we formulate FRG as a downstream affective mapping from this latent thought flow to facial expressions. Experiments on the REACT 2025 dataset show an FRDist of 72.39 and an FRDiv of 0.5057; perceptual plausibility is evaluated separately through blinded human ratings. A blinded human evaluation of 96 reactions found no significant difference in mean score between Full and ground truth ($5.527$ vs.\ $5.195$, $p_{\mathrm{Holm}}=.076$), while Full significantly outperformed Event-Triggered and Heuristic-Only (both $p_{\mathrm{Holm}}<.001$). The Reaction Quality Scorer (RQS) correlated strongly with human judgements (Pearson $r=.855$; Spearman $ρ=.821$, both $p<.05$), supporting its use as an automatic metric. These results underscore the immense potential of endogenous dynamics in building highly autonomous, human-like agents.

</details>


### [100] [LLMs for Executable Multi-Agent System Specification Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34619)
*Andreas Kouvaras,Periklis Mantenoglou,Alexander Artikis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出genRTEC，利用预训练大语言模型从自然语言描述生成可执行的MAS规范（RTEC语言），支持复杂层次与循环依赖，实验表明其生成规范预测精度高且不损失推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAS规范需表达智能体动作、环境效应及时间现象（如动作执行区间），且需可执行以支持运行时监控。但构建规范需形式语言专业知识，而机器学习方法依赖罕见的标注数据，二者均存在门槛。

Method: genRTEC借助预训练LLM，从概念的简短自然语言描述出发，自动生成Run-Time Event Calculus（RTEC）语言的可执行MAS规范，能够处理具有复杂层次和循环依赖的规范结构。

Result: 对多种MAS规范进行了广泛实证评估，包含定性与定量分析，结果显示genRTEC生成的规范具有高预测精度，同时未损害推理效率。

Conclusion: genRTEC证明了用LLM自动生成可执行MAS规范是可行且有效的，无需形式语言专家或大量标注数据，为运行时监控场景下的规范构建提供了新途径。

Abstract: MAS specifications express the effects of the actions of the agents and their environment, as well as other temporal phenomena, such as the intervals during which an agent may perform an action. The specification of a MAS should also be executable in order to allow for run-time monitoring. Constructing the specification of a MAS requires formal language expertise, while machine learning techniques depend on labelled data which are rarely available. To address these issues, we propose `genRTEC', a method that leverages pre-trained Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate executable MAS specifications, in the language of the `Run-Time Event Calculus' (RTEC), from natural language descriptions. genRTEC constructs MAS specifications with complex hierarchical and cyclic dependencies based only on short natural language descriptions of the concepts involved. We present an extensive empirical evaluation of genRTEC, spanning various MAS specifications, including both a qualitative and a quantitative assessment. Our results demonstrate that genRTEC constructs executable MAS specifications of high predictive accuracy without compromising reasoning efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [WebPageBench: Event-Level Verification and Controlled UI-Variant Generation for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35026)
*Anton Emelyanov,Maria Tikhonova,Zaven Martirosian,Sergei Averkiev,Alena Fenogenova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WebPageBench 是基于界面事件日志验证任务成功与否的 web agent 评测框架，无需判分模型或页面抓取，含 152 个任务、多变体与公开榜单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 web agent 评测依赖判分模型或渲染页面抓取，易受主观与界面形式干扰，难以在固定任务规格下衡量对界面形态的敏感度。

Method: 六个去品牌化的插桩 mock 站点在用户/agent 操作时发出带参数的typed事件；任务声明所需事件，通过事件匹配判定成功。同一插桩支持受控 UI 变体：一次开关即可以不同控件实现重渲染任务，而提示与成功条件完全不变。

Result: 发布含 152 个任务（65 个规范场景 + 87 个明暗模式控制变体）、共同 runner（6 种浏览器/DOM harness 配置与 5 类纯截图 GUI agent 系列）、24 个模型-harness 对的公开榜单。在榜单上，agent 自称完成与日志确认真实的差距达 41 个百分点（某配置声称全部完成，实际满足条件仅 59%）。

Conclusion: WebPageBench 以事件日志验证提供了无需判分模型、可测界面敏感度的 web agent 评测方式，并揭示了 agent 自报完成度与真实成功率的显著落差。

Abstract: We present WebPageBench, an open framework for evaluating web agents in which every task is verified from the interface's own event log. Six instrumented mock sites with brand identifiers removed (a marketplace, a bookstore, a grocery service, rail ticketing, hotel search and a document cabinet) emit typed events with parameters as a user or an agent acts. A task declares the events it requires, and success is decided by matching them, with no judge model and no scraping of rendered pages. The same instrumentation supports controlled UI variation: one configuration switch re-renders a task through a different implementation of a single control while the prompt and the success conditions stay completely identical, so sensitivity to interface form can be measured under a fixed task specification. The WebPageBench release consists of three components: 152 tasks, divided into 65 canonical scenarios and 87 control variants across light/dark UI-modes; a common runner evaluated with six browser/DOM harness configurations and five screenshot-only GUI-agent families; and a public leaderboard of 24 model-harness pairs. On the public 152-task leaderboard the gap between what agents declare finished and what the log confirms reaches 41 points (one configuration declares every task finished and satisfies the conditions on 59%).

</details>


### [102] [ResonAct: Streaming Metrics for Runtime Diagnosis and Self-Healing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34701)
*Tarun Chintada,Neelamadhav Gantayat,Ishaan Romil,Renuka Sindhgatta,Soujanya Soni,Sameep Mehta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ResonAct，一个面向多智能体系统的运行时自愈框架，通过流式指标实现执行中的持续监控、诊断与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在自动化企业工作流中易因工具退化、上下文传播错误、协调崩溃等问题失败；现有可观测性框架多在执行结束后才诊断和修复，错失运行时的恢复机会。

Method: ResonAct 将执行轨迹、智能体交互和工具调用输入流式分析层，持续计算任务进度、上下文健康和工具可靠性指标；以这些指标作为运行时控制信号，基于结构化故障模型检测异常并定位根因；据此动态选择修复策略并执行动作。框架作为外部控制平面，无需修改应用智能体或编排逻辑。

Result: 在企业工作流场景和 AppWorld 基准上评估：基于流式指标的分析能识别执行退化并定位故障；策略驱动修复使任务完成率最高提升10.00个百分点；检测精确率70.59%–82.91%，召回率63.09%–100%，恢复率10.48%–46.67%，运行时开销为-0.25%–14.12%。

Conclusion: ResonAct 证明了运行时自愈的可行性：通过流式指标与外部控制平面可在不侵入系统的前提下检测、定位并修复多智能体故障，有效提升任务完成率且开销可控。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) are increasingly used to automate enterprise workflows involving multiple specialized agents, external tools, and long-running task execution.
