Failures may arise from tool degradation, context propagation errors, coordination breakdowns, or repeated agent interactions that prevent task completion. While existing observability frameworks provide traces and logs, diagnosis and remediation are largely performed after execution completes, limiting opportunities for recovery during runtime. We present ResonAct, a runtime self-healing framework that enables continuous monitoring, diagnosis, and remediation of multi-agent systems through streaming operational metrics. ResonAct ingests execution traces, agent interactions, and tool invocations into a streaming analytics layer that continuously derives task progress, context health, and tool reliability metrics. These metrics serve as runtime control signals for detecting anomalous execution patterns and localizing root causes using a structured failure model. Based on the diagnosed failure, ResonAct dynamically selects remediation policies and performs actions. The framework operates as an external control plane, enabling intervention without modifying application agents or orchestration logic. We evaluate ResonAct across enterprise workflow scenarios and AppWorld benchmarks. The results show that the streaming metric-based analysis identifies execution degradations and localizes faults. Furthermore, policy-driven remediation improves task completion rates by up to 10.00 percentage points, with detection precision ranging from 70.59% to 82.91%, recall from 63.09% to 100%, recovery rates from 10.48% to 46.67%, and runtime overhead ranging from $-0.25%$ to 14.12% across the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [103] [From Human Narrative to Harmonic Structure: A Human-Centered Investigation of Algorithmic Music Generation through the Chord Wheel Diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34735)
*Josef Pavlíček,Petra Pavlíčková,Irena Štrausová*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究比较了Bob Dylan、Johnny Cash等六首叙事流行歌的人类编配和声与可解释计算和声器的输出，发现旋律-和弦高兼容性不一定保留人类原始和声决策模式，提出应建模和声结构身份、语境与创作意图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI音乐生成能满足调性与连贯性的形式要求，但音乐表达能否仅用数学属性描述仍是根本问题。人类作曲家受个人与文化语境影响，会刻意偏离既定模式，这部分未被现有计算和声方法捕捉。

Method: 选取Bob Dylan、Johnny Cash、Ritchie Valens的六首叙事驱动流行歌曲，将原始人类和声与仅输入旋律、不含原始和弦进行的可解释计算和声器输出对比，通过Chord Wheel Diagrams和BPMN表示，分析和声词汇、功能持续性、重复、非调式事件和张力-解决模式。

Result: 高旋律-和弦兼容性并不必然保留原人类和声决策模式。部分生成和声保留了参考曲的经济结构，另一些则改变和声多样性或抑制独特事件，同时仍与旋律兼容。

Conclusion: 研究不量化艺术质量，而是引入“叙事条件化和声结构”作为计算音乐分析的补充视角，建议生成系统不仅建模和声正确性，还应建模结构身份、语境和人类创作意图。

Abstract: Contemporary AI-based music generation can produce compositions that satisfy formal requirements of tonality and musical coherence. However, whether musical expression can be described by mathematical properties alone remains a fundamental question. Human composers operate within personal and cultural contexts that influence harmonic decisions and deliberate departures from established patterns. This study investigates six narrative-driven popular songs by Bob Dylan, Johnny Cash, and Ritchie Valens. Original human harmonies are compared with outputs of an explainable computational harmonizer operating on the same melodies without access to the original chord progressions. We examine harmonic vocabulary, functional persistence, repetition, non-diatonic events, and tension-resolution patterns using Chord Wheel Diagrams and BPMN-based representations. Results show that high melody-chord compatibility does not necessarily imply preservation of the original human harmonic decision pattern. Some generated harmonizations retain the economical structure of the reference, while others alter harmonic diversity or suppress distinctive events while remaining compatible with the melody. Rather than quantifying artistic quality, the study introduces narrative-conditioned harmonic structure as a complementary perspective for computational music analysis. The findings suggest that generative systems may benefit from modeling not only harmonic correctness, but also structural identity, context, and human compositional intention.

</details>


### [104] [Can We Trust the Teacher? Decoupled Credit Direction-Magnitude for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34848)
*Yugu Li,Zehong Cao,Peizhen Li,Yang Zhang,Siyi Hu,Jianglin Qiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DCSD，将信用方向与贡献幅度解耦，校准教师监督用于策略优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR提供可靠的轨迹级信用，OPSD提供token级稠密监督，但二者在更新步级信用方向与幅度时存在耦合，导致步骤无法获得可靠的信用方向和贡献幅度，且易受教师判断错误和偏好方差影响。

Method: DCSD将信用方向与幅度解耦为两个可靠信号并校准特权教师监督：用belief-margin probing确定信用方向，用marginal information gain量化信用幅度，实现步到token的信用分配用于策略优化。

Result: 在11个基准上总体得分最优，超过GRPO、OPSD、RLSD、RLCSD。相比基座模型，数学推理提升8.45分，多模态推理提升7.01分，纠正6%的token信用方向，token信用幅度降低1.5倍。

Conclusion: DCSD通过解耦信用方向与幅度，实现更可靠的步级信用分配，有效提升推理性能并减少信用幅度冗余。

Abstract: RLVR provides reliable trajectory-level credit, while OPSD offers dense supervision for token-level credit. This exposes a fundamental coupling when updating step-level credit direction and magnitude with teacher supervision, preventing steps from receiving reliable credit directions and contribution magnitudes, while making both vulnerable to teacher judgment errors and preference variance, as supported by our theoretical analysis. To separate credit direction from its contribution magnitude, we introduce \textit{Decoupled Credit Self-Distillation (DCSD)}, which theoretically decouples credit direction and magnitude into two reliable signals and uses them to calibrate privileged teacher supervision. Specifically, we design belief-margin probing to determine credit direction and marginal information gain to quantify credit magnitude, enabling step-to-token credit assignment for policy optimization. Across 11 benchmarks, DCSD achieves the best overall scores against GRPO, OPSD, RLSD, and RLCSD. Compared with base models, DCSD improves the overall score by 8.45 points on mathematical reasoning and 7.01 points on multimodal reasoning, while correcting the credit direction for 6\% of tokens and yielding a 1.5$\times$ reduction in token credit magnitude.

</details>


### [105] [From Migration to Calibration: Preserving Agent Capabilities across Models, Jurisdictions, and Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35149)
*Yaxiao Liu,Pengbo Liu,Yiwen Liu,Yihua Guan,Jiaxing Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将"智能体校准"形式化为跨三层（信息保存、运行环境适配、用户接受）的约束式行为适配方法，并提出相应的评估与验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署条件变化（更换驱动模型、跨辖区、跨市场扩展）时，仅靠接口兼容无法保证能力保留与目标契约满足，故需校准。

Method: 提出跨三层交互的约束式行为适配：信息校准保存经独立验证且仍适用的源内容；运行环境(harness)校准在语义检查点对齐可观察产物并迭代修复、工具替换或局部重规划；用户校准强制特定接收方的输出契约。区分可训练策略与冻结主干配置/控制器优化、证据验证与相对判断及DPO/GRPO优化。

Result: 以全球电商为例说明共享标准与站点/市场特定适配器共存；提出保留评估、源证据与检查点修复的因子测试、群体级报告，以防止总体增益掩盖局部失败。属方法论提案，尚无实现与实证。

Conclusion: 该文为方法论提案，实现与实证验证为未来工作。

Abstract: Agents need calibration when deployment conditions change: replacing a driving model, including a foundation-to-post-trained transition; crossing jurisdictions; or scaling across heterogeneous markets and sources. Interface compatibility alone does not establish capability retention or target-contract satisfaction. We formulate agent calibration as constrained behavioral adaptation across three interacting layers: information preservation, harness adaptation, and user acceptance; the layers apply to every scenario, not one-to-one to the three. The basic objective is non-degradation on prespecified capability measures while satisfying target requirements; aggregate improvement is stronger. Information calibration preserves independently validated source content still applicable to the target task. Harness calibration aligns observable artifacts at semantic checkpoints and repairs them through iteration, tool substitution, or local replanning within explicit budgets. User calibration enforces recipient-specific output contracts: templates, schemas, and section-level preferences. A global e-commerce example shows how shared standards coexist with site- and market-specific adapters and validation. We distinguish trainable policies from frozen-backbone configuration or controller optimization, and evidence verification from relative judgment and DPO/GRPO optimization. Recent harness-transfer and judge-validity studies motivate target-native execution records, separate audits of task validity and near-tie ranking, and matched target-native optimization controls. We propose held-out evaluations for model changes, cross-border adaptation, and scale, including a factorial test of source evidence and checkpoint repair and group-level reporting to prevent aggregate gains from masking local failures. This is a methodological proposal; implementation and empirical validation remain future work.

</details>


### [106] [EP-Mem: Elastic Privacy Memory for Social Relationship-Aware LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35233)
*Fengzhou Sun,Yuan Zhang,Xintong Yu,Jinyao Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EP-Mem弹性隐私记忆架构，将隐私重构为用户所有的社会角色边界控制，并构建首个长期多方隐私基准EP-Bench，显著提升隐私分类、披露判断并降低泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在人际交流中作为代理时面临隐私风险，需理解用户社会关系并遵守情境相关的信息披露边界。现有研究仅关注即时交互，忽视了长期关系性披露问题。

Method: EP-Mem包含：(1) 令牌级记忆，由用户可配置隐私策略驱动，对人物与事件分层，结合域级默认流通规则与事实级白/黑名单例外；(2) 可插拔边车隐私引擎，在摘要、细节、边界粒度上对齐披露控制与记忆，覆盖生成、存储、检索全流程。

Result: EP-Mem达到94.0%隐私分类准确率，披露许可判断从22%提升至68%，隐私泄露降低75.6%，同时保持检索性能与跨基准泛化能力。

Conclusion: EP-Mem有效解决长期多方沟通中的关系性隐私披露问题，在保护隐私的同时维持记忆检索性能，具备良好通用性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents face critical privacy risks when acting as delegates in human-agent-human communication. To prevent such breaches, agents must understand users' social relationships and adhere to context-dependent social information disclosure boundaries. Current studies on agent memory privacy focus on instantaneous interactions, leaving the long-term relational disclosure problem unexplored. In this paper, we propose EP-Mem, an Elastic Privacy Memory architecture that reframes privacy as user-owned boundary control across social roles. EP-Mem introduces (1) token-level memory driven by user-configurable a privacy policy that stratifies persons and events, combining domain-level default circulation rules with fact-level whitelist/blacklist exceptions; and (2) a pluggable sidecar with a privacy engine that aligns disclosure controls with memory across summary, detail, and boundary granularities, enforced throughout generation, storage, and retrieval. We construct EP-Bench, to our knowledge the first long-term multi-party benchmark with cross-session correlated events for policy-conditioned relational disclosure. Experiments show that EP-Mem achieves 94.0% privacy classification accuracy, improves disclosure-permission judgment from 22% to 68%, and reduces privacy leakage by 75.6%, while maintaining retrieval performance and cross-benchmark generalization.

</details>


### [107] [Continuous Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35540)
*William Hoy,Jingxuan Fan,Nurcin Celik,Xu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Continuous Context Management (CCM)：每轮都做压缩，活跃提示只保留任务、记忆与最新观测。评估显示可显著降低输入消耗与提示长度，但多数模型成功率下降；用带特权全历史蒸馏的 GRPO 训练后，CCM 性能在 WebShop 与 Endless Terminals 上优于普通 GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 LLM 智能体通常完整保留交互历史，直到超过阈值才触发压缩，导致活跃提示不断膨胀。作者希望验证"每轮即压缩"的 CCM 是否可行，并解决其带来的性能下降问题。

Method: 1) 定义 CCM：每轮输出更新后的记忆与环境动作，下一提示仅含原始任务、保留记忆与最新观测。2) 免微调评估：在 TerminalBench-2 上用 Claude Sonnet 4.6、Claude Opus 4.6、GLM-5、Kimi K3 测试。3) 训练改进：用 GRPO 结合特权全历史蒸馏——冻结的初始学生模型基于该学生 rollout 重建的完整历史，对采样动作打分，提供密集的 action-token 监督，无需独立教师 rollout 或参考答案。

Result: 免微调下 CCM 大幅降低累计输入用量与活跃提示规模，但多数模型任务成功率下降，仅 Kimi K3 保持性能。在 WebShop 上，蒸馏目标在两个模型规模上都显著优于 GRPO，且 Qwen3-4B-Instruct 超过全历史 GRPO（8B 未超过）。在 Endless Terminals 上略优于 GRPO，且两种 CCM 策略均超过未训练的全历史基线。

Conclusion: CCM 是一种可行的推理范式，可在大幅减少保留上下文的条件下运行；其性能可通过带特权全历史蒸馏的强化学习得到提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents commonly retain their complete interaction history until compaction is triggered at a predefined threshold. We study Continuous Context Management (CCM), which performs compaction at every turn to prevent interaction history from accumulating in the active prompt. At each turn, a CCM agent emits an updated memory together with an environment action; its next prompt contains the original task, retained memory, and newest observation rather than the complete transcript. We first evaluate CCM without fine-tuning on TerminalBench-2 using Claude Sonnet 4.6, Claude Opus 4.6, GLM-5, and Kimi K3. CCM substantially reduces cumulative input usage and active-prompt size, although it lowers task success for most models while preserving performance for Kimi K3. We use GRPO with privileged full-history distillation to improve CCM in open-weight models. A frozen copy of the student's initial model scores each sampled student action under the complete history reconstructed from that student's rollout, providing dense action-token supervision without a separate teacher rollout or reference solution. On WebShop, this objective substantially improves CCM over GRPO at both evaluated model scales and surpasses full-history GRPO for Qwen3-4B-Instruct, though not for Qwen3-8B. On Endless Terminals, the augmented method provides a modest improvement over GRPO, with both CCM policies outperforming the untrained full-history baseline. These results demonstrate that CCM is a viable inference paradigm for agents operating with substantially reduced retained context and that its performance can be improved through reinforcement learning with privileged full-history distillation.

</details>


### [108] [RareDx: Controlled Knowledge Integration and Graph-Grounded Policy Optimization for Rare-Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35549)
*Bo Zhang,Yuchen Wang,Dongbai Li,Matthew Yu Heng Wong,Qingkai Zeng,Lijun Wang,Tien-Yin Wong,Peng Cui,Tianyu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RareDx，将受控证据使用与知识图谱策略优化结合，把紧凑模型打造为长尾罕见病诊断排序器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见病诊断属长尾推理问题：表型不全、病例稀疏、证据散落于本体/基因注释/文献，语言模型倾向常见病、漏掉罕见候选或生成貌似合理的错误病名。

Method: RareDx-Harness 统一异构记录为排序诊断任务，比较直接推理、静态检索、自适应工具与结构化表型-基因-疾病推理。训练采用 Top-10 后训练与 RareDx-KGPO，奖励将预测投影到规范疾病图，融合分级相关性、本体邻近度、生物医学相似度与表型一致性，并加词表与输出长度约束。

Result: 八个基准上，Qwen3.5-9B 系统达 38.34 macro Hit@10，比 GPT-5.5 高 1.60 分；独立验证选择审计保持 6.80 分路由增益；27B 系统 Hit@1/5/10 为 23.53/36.56/40.76。

Conclusion: 受控消融显示检索并非普遍有效，受控路由是增益关键；结构化医学知识可将紧凑模型变为异构长尾临床场景下有竞争力的诊断排序器。

Abstract: Rare-disease diagnosis is a long-tail reasoning problem: phenotypes are incomplete, individual disorders are sparsely documented, and relevant evidence is distributed across ontologies, gene annotations, and biomedical text. Language models consequently favor common conditions, miss rare candidates, or produce plausible but invalid names. We introduce RareDx, which couples controlled evidence use with knowledge-graph-grounded policy optimization. RareDx-Harness normalizes heterogeneous records into one ranked-diagnosis task and compares direct inference, static retrieval, adaptive tools, and structured phenotype-gene-disease reasoning over a shared knowledge layer. The training pipeline combines Top-10 post-training with RareDx-KGPO, our knowledge-graph-grounded policy optimization method. Its reward projects predictions into a canonical disease graph and integrates curated graded relevance, ontology proximity, biomedical similarity, and phenotype consistency. Vocabulary and output-budget constraints prevent dense partial credit from rewarding fabricated or overlong differentials. Across eight benchmarks, the complete RareDx system centered on Qwen3.5-9B reaches 38.34 macro Hit@10, 1.60 points above GPT-5.5 under the archived protocol; a disjoint validation-selection audit retains a 6.80-point routing gain over Direct on held-out cases. The 27B system reaches 23.53/36.56/40.76 at Hit@1/5/10. Controlled ablations show that retrieval is not uniformly helpful and that controlled routing is central to the gain. These results indicate that structured medical knowledge can turn a compact model into a competitive diagnostic ranker across heterogeneous long-tail settings in clinical practice.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [IndicFDB: Benchmarking Full-Duplex Voice Agents across Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31967)
*Rajarshi Roy,Shobhit Banga,Jonathan Raiman,Supriya Paul,Bhaskar Singh,Manmeet Kaur,Sagar Jain,Hanuman Sidh,Pranav Sharma,Aditya Singh,Aaditya Pareek,Manas Dhir,Adi Margolin,Niket Agarwal,Bryan Catanzaro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndicFDB将全双工语音基准Full-Duplex-Bench扩展到印度10种语言、12,350个样本（约17倍），用VAD事件挖掘、语言无关时序评估和翻译+LLM评分流程解决多语言评测难题，并揭示商用与开源语音代理的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Full-Duplex-Bench仅支持英语，且依赖带词时间戳的ASR和英语提示的LLM评判，难以扩展到印度语言。需要构建覆盖印度多语言、支持暂停处理、轮流、backchannel和中断行为的全双工评测基准。

Method: 从约50,000小时声道分离对话中用VAD挖掘暂停处理、轮流和backchannel样本，并构建经人工校验的合成用户中断样本；采用语言无关的VAD启发式评估时序；通过开源转写与翻译流程将响应转为英语，再用LLM评分相关性和质量。

Result: 构建IndicFDB，覆盖印度10种语言、12,350个样本，规模约为原基准的17倍。评测7个语音代理：商用API跨语言行为意外一致，但要么快、要么抗暂停，无法兼得；单语开源全双工模型在backchannel、响应质量和延迟间存在进一步权衡。

Conclusion: IndicFDB有效解决多语言全双工语音代理评测中的事件发现、时序评估和跨语言评判问题，为印度语言提供可扩展基准，并揭示现有语音代理在延迟、鲁棒性和质量之间的根本权衡。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must handle pauses, take turns, backchannel, and respond to user interruptions in real time. Full-Duplex-Bench evaluates these behaviors, but its English-only corpus and reliance on word-timestamped ASR and an English-prompted LLM judge make it difficult to extend to Indian languages. We introduce IndicFDB, which extends it to ten languages spoken in India with 12,350 samples, nearly 17 times as many as the original. We address three challenges: finding conversational events in multilingual speech, evaluating their timing without reliable word-level alignment, and judging responses across languages. We mine pause handling, turn taking, and backchanneling samples from roughly 50,000 hours of channel-separated conversations using voice activity detection (VAD), and construct human-validated synthetic user interruption samples. Language-independent VAD heuristics evaluate timing, while an open-weight transcription and translation pipeline converts responses to English for LLM ratings of relevance and quality. Across seven voice agents, commercial APIs show unexpectedly consistent behavior across languages but are either fast or robust to pauses, never both, while monolingual open full-duplex models expose further tradeoffs among backchanneling, response quality, and latency.

</details>


### [110] [TRAP: Understanding and Mitigating Privacy Memorization in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32293)
*Muhammed Ustaomeroglu,Ziyue Xu,Hanshen Xiao,Peter Cnudde,Guannan Qu,Holger R. Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TRAP方法，通过在微调时对token级Target Reference Advantage（TRA）施加单侧惩罚，在不知敏感span的情况下显著降低敏感信息记忆，且效用损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调语言模型可能使其复现敏感记录。作者希望理解记忆何时产生，并在事先不知道哪些span敏感的情况下加以防止。现有方法（如差分隐私）代价高或效果有限。

Method: 以在语料另一半上训练的模型为参考，定义per-token的TRA信号，区分模型对特定记录的拟合与跨记录学习。分析微调中驱动记忆的因素，并提出TRAP：对token级TRA的单侧惩罚，仅在目标模型概率超过参考时生效。在带标注个人信息的作文和带患者标识的临床案例上评估。

Result: TRA廉价且可微。记忆在验证最优后仍持续增长，在小数据集、高学习率、更难任务上更大。早停可去除部分记忆，但对罕见、难预测的敏感span帮助最小。TRAP将记忆降至接近未训练模型水平，效用损失小；通用正则化器几乎无效，差分隐私则牺牲大部分微调收益。

Conclusion: 以TRA为核心的TRAP能有效抑制敏感信息记忆，无需预先知道敏感span，优于通用正则化和差分隐私，兼顾效用。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on sensitive records can leave it able to reproduce them. We ask when this memorization arises and how to prevent it without knowing in advance which spans are sensitive. Our starting point is that most memorization scores and attacks share one statistical core: whether the model assigns a token more probability than some reference would. Taking as the reference a model trained on the complementary half of the same corpus gives the Target Reference Advantage (TRA), a per-token signal that separates what a model fit to a particular record from what it learned across records, and is cheap and differentiable. We then study what drives memorization during fine-tuning: it keeps growing well past the validation minimum, is larger on small datasets and at higher learning rates, and higher when the underlying task is harder. Early stopping removes much of it, but because it is chosen by aggregate validation loss it helps least for rare, hard-to-predict spans embedded in otherwise learnable text, which is exactly what sensitive information tends to be. We therefore introduce TRAP, a one-sided penalty on tokenwise TRA that acts only where the target model pulls ahead of its reference. On student essays with annotated personal information and clinical cases with patient identifiers, TRAP brings memorization near the level of an untrained model at little utility cost, where generic regularizers barely move and differential privacy gives up most of what fine-tuning bought.

</details>


### [111] [Shared Worlds, Private Minds: Structured Memory for Long-Form Writing as World Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32401)
*Qiuyu Tian,Xiaowen Gu,Hang Su,Jianghan Chao,Haojie Yin,Fan Guo,Xin Zhang,Jinjing Shen,Ewing Luo,Youyong Kong,Yingce Xia,Zequn Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NarraWorld，一个面向长篇虚构写作的结构化记忆系统，将记忆构建视作"世界创造"，通过共享证据图派生四类互联视图（世界事实、角色信念、未决发展、假设分支），结合分层聚合与计划式重建检索，在三个写作基准上取得最佳综合结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理写长篇虚构时需显式记忆不断演化的故事世界以保持一致性。此类记忆须：区分异构叙事信息、跨粒度整合剧情、恢复写作请求中隐含的依赖关系。

Method: NarraWorld将记忆构建视为世界创建。从共享的证据支撑图派生四个互联视图：世界事实、每个角色的信念、未决发展、假设分支（可能世界延续）。采用带原子闭包的分层聚合，将事件整合为场景、情节线、情节，并保持每个高层节点可追溯到其来源片段。检索时使用计划式重建：从当前叙事情境和记忆预览推断查询依赖，并在token预算内组装相关记录。

Result: 在三个写作基准上，NarraWorld取得最强综合结果。其记忆可迁移至情境化角色扮演，且在通用长期记忆基准上基本保持召回率。

Conclusion: 该系统为代理在多样化叙事任务中维持连贯故事世界铺平道路。

Abstract: LLM agents that write long-form fiction need an explicit memory of the evolving storyworld to keep new events consistent with established facts. Such memory must keep heterogeneous narrative information distinct, integrate story developments across granularities, and recover dependencies that a writing request leaves implicit. We present NarraWorld, a structured memory system for long-form writing that treats memory construction as world creation. From a shared evidence-grounded graph, NarraWorld derives four connected views: world facts, per-character beliefs, open developments, and hypothetical branches (possible-world continuations). Hierarchical aggregation with atomic closure consolidates events into scenes, plotlines, and plots, keeping each higher-level node traceable to its constituent source spans. For retrieval, planned reconstruction infers a query's dependencies from the current narrative situation and a preview of memory, then assembles the relevant records within a token budget. Across three writing benchmarks, NarraWorld achieves the strongest aggregate results. Its memory also transfers to situated role-playing and largely preserves recall on a general-purpose long-term memory benchmark, paving the way for agents that sustain coherent storyworlds across diverse narrative tasks.

</details>


### [112] [From Knowing to Abstaining: Bridging the Representation-Action Gap in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32653)
*Jialuo He,Huangxun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VAD-R基准（通过捷径过滤与质量验证消除可答性泄露，并标注逐步理由和因果证据缺口标签）和Rep2Act方法（将对齐隐层表征中的可答性意识转化为显式弃答决策），显著提升VLM弃答能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM弃答基准存在两大缺陷：(1)样本含捷径线索（图像/问题暗示可答性）且提供显式"不可答"选项，无法评估自发弃答；(2)训练数据仅有二值标签，缺乏细粒度解释。同时VLM自发弃答能力很弱。

Method: 1) VAD-R基准：两阶段流水线——捷径过滤+质量验证，防止可答性泄露；每例标注逐步理由与因果证据缺口标签。2) Rep2Act：表征到动作对齐方法，将隐层中潜在的可答性意识转化为显式弃答决策（探测分析发现某些层隐状态可区分可答性，但未体现在最终回答中）。

Result: SOTA开源/闭源VLM在VAD-R上自发弃答召回率仅11.4%和16.3%。Rep2Act将动作准确率从56.67%提升至86.33%（Qwen2.5-VL-3B）、从59.33%至88.67%（Qwen2.5-VL-7B）。在OOD的TUBench上，仅用3B模型取得平均F1 53.3%，超过GPT-4 Turbo（16.2%）和GPT-4o（1.1%）。

Conclusion: 通过消除可答性泄露的基准VAD-R揭示VLM自发弃答能力不足；Rep2Act有效将潜在可答性表征转化为弃答行为，显著提升弃答性能并具良好泛化性。

Abstract: The ability of vision-language models (VLMs) to abstain from unanswerable questions is as important as their ability to answer answerable ones accurately. Recently, several benchmarks have emerged to evaluate and improve VLM abstention, but they have substantial limitations. First, samples often contain shortcut cues in images or questions that reveal answerability, while an explicit "unanswerable" option further prevents accurate assessment of spontaneous abstention. Second, as training data, they generally provide only binary labels without fine-grained explanations for deeper supervision. To address these limitations, we introduce Visual Answerability Diagnosis with Rationales (VAD-R), a benchmark constructed through a two-stage pipeline of shortcut filtering and quality verification to prevent answerability leakage. Each example is annotated with step-by-step rationales and causal evidence-gap labels. Evaluation of state-of-the-art open- and closed-source VLMs on VAD-R reveals limited spontaneous abstention, with average recall rates of only 11.4% and 16.3%, respectively. Probing analyses show that hidden-state representations in certain layers can effectively distinguish answerability, yet this distinction fails to manifest in final responses. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Rep2Act, a representation-to-action alignment method that translates latent answerability awareness into explicit abstention decisions. Rep2Act improves action accuracy on VAD-R from 56.67% to 86.33% for Qwen2.5-VL-3B and from 59.33% to 88.67% for Qwen2.5-VL-7B. On the out-of-distribution TUBench, Rep2Act achieves an average F1 score of 53.3% with only a 3B model, surpassing the closed-source GPT-4 Turbo and GPT-4o by 16.2% and 1.1%, respectively.

</details>


### [113] [Linger and Lose: Knowledge Collapse in Low-Bit Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32902)
*Prashanna Mani Paudel,Shivanand Venkanna Sheshappanavar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 三元权重语言模型在常规损失/精度指标下看似仅小幅退化，但在知识容量（每参数字节的事实存储）上损失可高达94%。该失败源于输出头的“知识崩溃”不稳定，可通过WSD调度和降低输出头学习率大幅缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度训练评估依赖损失和下游客准确率，这些指标掩盖了小精度模型在知识存储能力上的巨大失败，需要以知识容量作为更敏感的评价标准。

Method: 在合成传记（信息量已知）上从头训练2.5M–50M参数的GPT-2风格模型，覆盖五种精度，测量每参数事实比特（知识容量）；全程跟踪训练动态，定位崩溃为输出头学习率驻留不稳定，并测试WSD调度、降低输出头学习率及后训练量化。

Result: 标准余弦调度下三元模型仅保留fp16容量的6%，且随规模扩大差距增大而困惑度仅升1.4–1.6倍；模型先获得容量后丧失大部分。学习率持在1–2×10⁻⁴不衰减时，数百次曝光内崩溃且不可恢复；崩溃位于输出头对不可预测值的权重失控。WSD（10%冷却）使25M容量提升2.7倍、50M提升4.3倍，低精度增益更大；降低输出头学习率可防崩溃；<4 bit后训练量化无法恢复可测容量。

Conclusion: 应以训练中保留的知识容量而非最终损失来评判低精度训练，三元量化的真实知识损失远超传统指标所示。

Abstract: Training language models with ternary weights is commonly judged by loss and downstream accuracy, which record only a modest cost relative to full precision. We show that these metrics can conceal a much larger failure. We instead measure knowledge capacity, the factual bits stored per parameter, on synthetic biographies with known information content. We train GPT-2-style models from scratch with 2.5M to 50M parameters at five precisions. Under the standard cosine schedule, ternary models retain as little as 6% of an identically trained fp16 model's capacity. The deficit widens with model size while perplexity rises by only 1.4 to 1.6 times. Measured throughout training, these models first acquire capacity and then lose most of it. We identify this knowledge collapse as a learning-rate dwell instability. Held near $1$--$2\times10^{-4}$ with no decay, a pre-collapse model collapses within a few hundred exposures, and returning to a safe rate does not restore capacity. We then locate the collapse in the output head. It happens at a value the model can never predict. The weights there grow unchecked, while every other prediction the model makes is unchanged. We find that making the value predictable removes the collapse, regardless of which attribute carries it. A warmup-stable-decay schedule with a 10% cooldown increases ternary capacity by 2.7 times at 25M and 4.3 times at 50M. Gains are larger at lower precision. Cutting the output head's learning rate prevents failure when training from scratch. The post-training quantization methods we tested recover no measurable capacity below 4 bits. Our findings suggest judging low-precision training by retained capacity during training rather than final loss.

</details>


### [114] [From Position Risks to Block Survival: Faster Generation for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33390)
*Siwei Chen,Yuxiang Wan,Yifan Yu,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BRISK-DLM框架，解决扩散语言模型中并行预测与生成贡献不匹配的问题，通过优化提议学习与选择提升端到端吞吐量达37.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型可并行预测多个词元以加速生成，但存在两个不匹配：并行预测无法基于同一块内先前选中的词元进行条件建模，且错误高度不对称——早期拒绝会阻断所有后续提议的解码进展。

Method: BRISK-DLM在自生成序列上训练，用风险-奖励加权按位置对验证进展和解码成本的影响动态排序；推理时引入轻量前缀条件校正器，利用已选词元及从模型自身验证器蒸馏的偏好对候选重排序，复用主干并行表示，无需额外主干评估。

Result: 端到端吞吐量最高提升37.4%，同时保持任务质量，建立了DLM生成新的质量-吞吐量前沿。

Conclusion: BRISK-DLM通过同时解决提议学习与选择的不匹配，在保持质量的前提下显著提升扩散语言模型的生成效率。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) can accelerate generation by predicting multiple tokens in parallel, but there is a mismatch between how these tokens are predicted and how they ultimately contribute to generation. Parallel predictions can hardly condition on the tokens selected earlier within the same block, even though their validity depends on this realized prefix. Under the popular proposal-verification decoding, this mismatch makes errors highly asymmetric: an early rejection prevents all subsequent proposals from contributing decoding progress. We introduce BRISK-DLM, a framework that addresses both mismatches by optimizing proposal learning and selection for verified progress. BRISK-DLM trains on self-generated sequences, using risk-reward weighting to dynamically prioritize positions by their impact on verified progress and decoding cost. During inference, a lightweight prefix-conditioned corrector reranks existing candidates using previously selected tokens and preferences distilled from the model's own verifier. The corrector reuses the backbone's parallel representations and requires no additional backbone evaluation, while fused execution keeps its overhead small. BRISK-DLM improves end-to-end throughput by up to 37.4% while preserving task quality, establishing a new quality-throughput frontier for DLM generation.

</details>


### [115] [Grounding Memory Summarization in Utility Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33417)
*Zhenyu Lei,Mingjia Shi,Xingbo Fu,Haoyu He,Qi R. Wang,Jundong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemSuit：将记忆系统摘要目标从对人类友好（如忠实度）转向面向下游查询的效用。通过以查询-答案对为条件的自蒸馏框架，教师基于问答对生成效用感知的记忆条目，学生从原始对话重现该能力；并将每块拆分为多个自包含条目以防证据丢失，且对比微调嵌入模型。多类对话查询上均超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统摘要器以忠实度等面向人类的标准优化，与其真正目标——保留支持未来查询所需证据——不一致。论文发现以查询-答案对为条件能显著提升答案质量，且这种效用感知行为可跨查询迁移。

Method: 提出MemSuit自蒸馏框架：教师摘要器以观察到的查询-答案对为条件，产生效用感知记忆条目；学生学习仅从原始对话重现。为避免单一问答对条件化导致的附带擦除（丢失其他合理查询的证据），教师将每个块分解为多个自包含条目，保留不同查询相关侧面作为可独立检索单元。进一步用教师条目监督的对比目标微调嵌入模型，以对齐检索器与紧凑、事实密集的条目风格。

Result: 在多种会话查询类型上，MemSuit一致优于最先进基线，证实将记忆扎根于下游效用具有价值。

Conclusion: 以查询效用为导向的记忆摘要优于面向人类的标准；自蒸馏+多条目分解+嵌入对齐可有效提升记忆系统在下游查询中的表现。

Abstract: Existing summarizers for memory systems are typically optimized for human-facing criteria such as faithfulness, which misaligns with their true objective: preserving the evidence needed to support future queries. We show that conditioning summarization on query-answer pairs substantially improves answer quality, and that this utility-aware behavior is transferable across queries. Motivated by these findings, we propose MemSuit, a self-distillation framework in which a teacher summarizer, conditioned on observed query-answer pairs, produces utility-aware memory entries that a student learns to reproduce from the raw conversation alone. To prevent collateral erasure where conditioning on a single query-answer pair discards evidence relevant to other plausible queries, the teacher decomposes each block into multiple self-contained entries that preserve distinct query-relevant facets as independently retrievable units. To align the retriever with the compact, fact-dense style of teacher entries, we further fine-tune the embedding model with a contrastive objective supervised by teacher entries. Across a diverse suite of conversational query types, MemSuit consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, confirming the value of grounding memory in downstream utility.

</details>


### [116] [Yorùbá in Unicode: An Overview of a Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33734)
*Kólá Túbòsún*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 约鲁巴语等非洲语言依赖变音符号，但Unicode未编码所需预组合字符，导致写作与数字系统长期出问题。本文记录该失败并提出编码请求作为解决路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约鲁巴语在互联网和计算机上的书写长期存在难解问题：该语言（及其他依赖变音符号消歧的非洲语言）需要一小部分Unicode未编码的预组合字符，迫使作者与数字系统依赖跨平台行为不一致、字体替换时损坏、搜索失败的组合字符序列。

Method: 作者结合个人经验与实证证据，记录该失败在多种真实场景中的表现，涵盖已出版书籍、网络平台和移动键盘。并分析其结构性成因，指出Unicode的NFC规范化稳定性政策是阻碍直接修复的结构性约束。

Result: 揭示了组合字符序列在跨平台、字体替换和搜索场景中的系统性失败，并确认NFC规范化稳定性政策为根本性结构约束，使问题难以简单修复。

Conclusion: 主张Unicode联盟直接介入解决这一活跃问题，并提出为四个核心约鲁巴字符提交正式编码请求，视为最持久的解决方案。

Abstract: There is a recurrent problem in the writing of Yorùbá on the internet and on the computer that has proven intractable over the years. The language, along with other African languages that depend on diacritics for disambiguation, requires a small set of precomposed characters that Unicode does not encode. This has forced writers and digital systems to rely on combining character sequences that behave inconsistently across platforms, corrupt under font substitution, and fail in search. This paper documents that failure across a range of real world contexts, from published books to web platforms to mobile keyboards, using personal and empirical evidence. It identifies Unicode's NFC normalization stability policy as the structural constraint that prevents a straightforward fix, arguing for direct intervention of the Consortium in solving the active problem, proposing a formal encoding request for the four core Yorùbá characters as the most durable path to resolution.

</details>


### [117] [Positions Are Not Facts: The Mismatch Between KV Caches and Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33759)
*Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Shiyang Zhang,Jun Gao,Zhen Li,Hua Wu,Hanchao Yu,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究语言模型KV缓存中事实更新时应如何调整历史记录，比较了隐藏整条记录、隐藏被替换值、删除旧文本重算缓存等方法。发现掩码能让模型更偏好新值，但常因单位错误等丢失完整答案；保留单位可挽救当前答案；后续状态仍保留早期记录的有用信息，重建这些状态会大幅降低历史准确率，而移动已有状态影响很小。识别是独立难题，学习读出能恢复固定相似度遗漏的区分，自然文本上检测器选掩码无明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事实变化时，KV缓存作为可更新记忆该如何更新历史记录尚不明确。隐藏旧记录成本低，但可能仍含所需细节，或仍需回答关于过去的问题。需要厘清更新时除被替换值之外，哪些信息必须保留。

Method: 在受控数量任务中比较三种策略：隐藏整条记录、仅隐藏被替换值、删除旧文本并重算缓存。分析掩码对模型偏好新值及完整答案的影响；在多跳更新中测试重建与移动已有状态对历史准确率的影响；用学习读出与固定缓存相似度对比识别能力；在自然文本上用文本检测器选掩码与随机掩码对比。

Result: 掩码使全部八个模型更强烈偏好新值，但六个模型因单位错误或未停止而丢失完整答案，保留单位可保全当前答案。多跳更新中，在未变位置重建状态使历史准确率下降20-41个百分点，移动已有状态影响很小。保留对象依赖和未变修订段落可避免许多损失。学习读出在合成记录对上恢复了固定缓存相似度遗漏的区分。自然文本上，14组同模型检测器-生成器比较中，检测器选掩码相对随机掩码无明确优势。查询相关访问可避免部分损失，但需额外存储或访问成本。

Conclusion: 当把KV缓存用作可更新记忆时，更新不应只替换值：必须保留单位、对象依赖与未变修订段落等信息，并谨慎处理后续状态与识别问题。查询相关访问虽有帮助但有成本。

Abstract: When a fact changes, how should a language model update the history stored in its key-value (KV) cache? Hiding the old record is cheap, but it may still contain needed details or answer questions about the past. We compare hiding whole records, hiding only replaced values, and deleting old text and recomputing the cache. In a controlled quantity task, masking makes all eight models prefer the new value more strongly, yet six lose complete answers through unit errors or failure to stop; keeping the unit preserves all current answers. Later states also retain useful information from earlier records: on multi-hop updates, rebuilding these states at unchanged positions lowers historical accuracy by 20-41 percentage points, whereas moving the existing states has little effect. Keeping object dependencies and unchanged revision passages prevents many losses. Recognition is a separate challenge. Learned readouts recover distinctions missed by fixed cache similarities on synthetic record pairs. On natural text, text-detector-selected masks show no clear advantage over random masks at the same rate in 14 same-model detector-generator comparisons. Query-dependent access can avoid some losses, with additional storage or access costs. These findings identify what must be preserved beyond the replaced value when using a KV cache as updatable memory.

</details>


### [118] [Opera: A Verbal Critic Framework for Long-horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33987)
*Kai Mei,Zhiyuan Hu,Yutong Dai,Juntao Tan,Yifan Zhang,Dingjie Song,Dimitris N. Metaxas,Silvio Savarese,Ran Xu,Zeyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Opera 是一个"持久化口头批评"框架，将每次纠正当作一条持续跟踪的笔记，直到问题真正解决，而非仅生成反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期编码智能体需要及时纠错，但反馈若误判当前工作或未触及根本问题则无效甚至有害。现有批评者只评估轨迹并生成反馈，很少追踪反馈之后的实际效果。

Method: Opera 通过周期性/事件驱动触发决定何时审查；用类型化算子诊断问题；在反馈交付前对照可见证据审计；并追踪智能体后续动作，区分"表面服从"与"真正解决"。

Result: 作为测试时批评者，在 Terminal-Bench 2.1、SWE-Bench Pro 子集、DeepSWE v1.1 上分别将非批评智能体的解决率最多提升 12.4、15.0、8.9 个百分点（四种策略模型），在三个基准上均取得竞争性基线中最高平均解决率；自我批评时亦有效。用 Opera 引导的轨迹微调 Qwen3.5-9B，在其未见的 SWE-Bench Pro 仓库上提升 10.2 个百分点（推理时不带批评者），媲美用更强模型数据微调，且切换 harness（Openhands→Terminus-2）时性能保持。

Conclusion: 将反馈建模为"持续追踪直至解决"的持久笔记，可同时提升推理期纠错效果与训练数据质量，且鲁棒性优于以往方法。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents need timely corrections, yet feedback can be ineffective or even harmful when it misjudges ongoing work or fails to address the underlying problem. Existing critics focus on evaluating trajectories and generating feedback, but rarely track what happens after feedback is delivered. We present Opera, a verbal critic framework that treats each correction as a persistent note, followed until the diagnosed problem is resolved. Opera decides when to review through periodic and event-driven triggers, diagnoses issues with typed operators, audits feedback against visible evidence before delivery, and tracks the agent's subsequent actions to distinguish mere compliance from actual resolution. As a test-time critic, Opera improves the resolve rate of non-critic agents by up to 12.4, 15.0, and 8.9 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, a SWE-Bench Pro subset, and DeepSWE v1.1, respectively, across four policy models, and achieves the highest mean resolve rate among competitive critic baselines on all three benchmarks, and also improves policy models when the policy critiques itself. Beyond inference, Opera-guided rollouts provide approximately on-policy training data: fine-tuning Qwen3.5-9B on them improves its resolve rate on held-out SWE-Bench Pro repositories by 10.2 percentage points without a critic at inference time, matching fine-tuning on rollouts from a stronger model, while preserving its performance when switching harness, i.e., from Openhands to Terminus-2, which the latter substantially degrades.

</details>


### [119] [Reciprocal Guidance: Orchestrating Draft and Verify Budgets for Advancing the Diffusion-AR Self-Speculation Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34388)
*Linye Wei,Shutian Zheng,Haoyu Zeng,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RecGuide框架，通过草案与验证的互预测性动态编排推理，提升自推测解码吞吐，最高1.8倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自推测解码中聚合与单请求吞吐的帕累托前沿未被充分探索：低并发下顺序草案-验证需两轮前向，限制TPF；高并发下长草案计算昂贵，限制推测预算。

Method: 基于草案logits可预测验证失配、验证结果可预测草案效用与合适块大小的观察，提出RecGuide运行时编排框架：低并发时用验证重叠草案利用空闲算力，高并发时动态分配请求级草案块大小。

Result: 在多种并发水平下相较vanilla自推测解码持续提升吞吐，最高达1.8倍加速。

Conclusion: RecGuide通过互预测引导的运行时编排，有效适配不同服务负载，兼顾低并发与高并发场景的效率。

Abstract: Diffusion drafting with autoregressive (AR) verification has emerged as a promising paradigm for efficient speculative decoding. Recent self-speculation models, represented by Nemotron-Labs-Diffusion, further simplify the speculative pipeline by unifying drafting and verification within a shared backbone, while enabling longer acceptance lengths. However, the Pareto frontier between aggregate and per-request throughput remains underexplored. At low concurrency, sequential draft-verify execution requires two model forward passes per round, limiting the effective tokens per forward (TPF). By contrast, at high concurrency, longer drafts incur increasingly expensive computation, forcing individual requests to operate under constrained speculation budgets and preventing full exploitation of the full-backbone drafter. Our key observation indicates that drafting and verification exhibit reciprocal predictability. Draft logits can anticipate likely verification mismatches, while recent verification outcomes predict future drafting utility and suitable block sizes. Building on this observation, we introduce Reciprocal Guidance (RecGuide), a runtime draft-verify orchestration framework that adapts speculative decoding to varying serving loads. RecGuide exploits spare compute capacity through verification-overlapped drafting at low concurrency, while dynamically allocating request-specific draft block sizes as the workload becomes increasingly compute-intensive. Experiments across a wide range of concurrency levels demonstrate consistent throughput improvements over vanilla self-speculation, achieving up to $1.8\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [120] [RGDT-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Reasoning for Rule-Governed Decisions and Their Justifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34455)
*Jianpeng Zhao,Haihua Xu,Haoyang Zhang,Shuang Qian,Yixiang Tang,Xintao Wang,Kun Sun,Pei Wu,Shuhan Zhong,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RGDT-Bench基准，评估规则治理决策任务中模型决策与理由依据的完整性，发现理由不完整普遍存在且难检测，并通过带依据监督的奖励模型改善检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策、合同、合规等场景要求模型依据外部规则对案例事实进行决策并给出理由。现有数学和逻辑推理任务侧重演绎能力，而RGDT还需解释规则适用性、从证据评估条件、综合规则与例外，并提供可核查的理由依据，因此需要同时评估决策及其依据的基准。

Method: 提出RGDT-Bench，包含202.1K条件级监督槽，覆盖四个任务轨道与八种任务-探针组合，变化支持信息的可及性。采用标签盲抽取与确定性检查生成依据完整性标签：来源引用覆盖度与所述决策依据的一致性。将失败归因于四个过程层：规则使用、条件、证据、聚合，并检查最终结果。另训练一个带依据监督的简单奖励模型。

Result: 在可评估的正确回答中，六个被评LLM及支持的组合平均依据不完整率为40.2%。十七个现有评估器中最佳仅达57.69%任务平均AUROC（随机为50%）。带依据监督的奖励模型达69.24%，超过匹配的结果监督基线10.37个百分点，超过最佳现有评估器11.55个百分点。

Conclusion: 依据不完整普遍存在且具潜在安全风险且难以检测。带依据监督的奖励模型在完整性评估及多数响应选择比较中优于结果监督基线，支持RGDT-Bench的依据监督用于RGDT推理。

Abstract: We study reasoning in Rule-Governed Decision Tasks (RGDTs), where models apply external rules to case facts and justify decisions, as required in policy, contract, and compliance settings. Beyond the deductive capability emphasized by standard mathematical and logical reasoning tasks, RGDTs require interpreting rules and their applicability, assessing conditions from evidence, combining judgments under rules and exceptions, and providing checkable justifications. These demands motivate a benchmark assessing both decisions and their stated grounds. We introduce RGDT-Bench, providing 202.1K condition-level supervision slots across four task tracks and eight supported task-probe combinations that vary access to supporting information. Label-blind extraction and deterministic checks produce labels for warrant completeness: source-referenced coverage and consistency of stated decision grounds. The benchmark attributes failures to four process layers: rule use, condition, evidence, and aggregation, and checks the final outcome. Among evaluable correct responses, warrant incompleteness averages 40.2% across six evaluated LLMs and supported task-probe combinations. Such warrant incompleteness poses potential safety risks and remains difficult to detect: the best of seventeen existing evaluators reaches only 57.69% (random: 50%) task-averaged area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC). To address this difficulty, we train a simple reward model with warrant supervision. It achieves 69.24% task-averaged AUROC among correct answers, exceeding the matched outcome-supervised baseline by 10.37 pp (percentage points) and the best existing evaluator by 11.55 pp. Beyond completeness assessment, the model outperforms both outcome-supervised baselines across nearly all response-selection comparisons, supporting RGDT-Bench's warrant supervision for RGDT reasoning.

</details>


### [121] [Papers Without Code: Availability of GitHub Repositories Linked in *CL Publications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34534)
*Selina Meyer,Michael Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了NLP领域CL/ACL会议论文中GitHub仓库的长期可用性，发现近期论文的仓库失效比例与旧论文相近，部分原因是空仓库和占位仓库增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP研究日益依赖GitHub共享代码和数据，但相关仓库的长期可用性尚未被系统评估，这关系到研究可复现性和成果可持续性。

Method: 统计并分析过去十年CL期刊及ACL及其同期会议上论文所链接的GitHub仓库的可用性，并与其他*CL会议及非GitHub平台做对比。

Result: 与预期相反，较新的ACL论文所链接的GitHub仓库失效比例与较旧论文相近，部分原因是空仓库和占位仓库增多；类似趋势存在于其他*CL会议，但非GitHub平台未出现该现象。

Conclusion: GitHub上研究工件仓库的长期可用性并未随时间改善，反而因空/占位仓库增加而持续存在问题，需引起NLP社区对代码共享可持续性的关注。

Abstract: Source code and data published at computational linguistics (*CL) venues are increasingly being shared via GitHub. While this generally is a favourable development for the accessibility and potential reusability of research artifacts in natural language processing (NLP), the long-term availability of such repositories has not been evaluated. In this squib, we discuss the availability of repositories linked in papers published in the Computational Linguistics (CL) journal as well as at ACL and its co-located events over the past ten years. Contrary to our expectations, we find that GitHub repositories linked in more recent ACL publications are unavailable at similar rates as in older publications, in parts due to an increase in empty and placeholder repositories. Similar trends hold for other *CL venues, but not for platforms other than GitHub.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Token Alignment: Event Completion for Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34738)
*Jiacheng Liu,Jingwei Song,Qituan Zhang,Siheng Chen,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Event-Set Completion Distillation (ESCD)，用于跨分词器on-policy蒸馏，通过对"字节兼容完成集"的归一化监督，避免教师token概率在学生分词器上的不合理拆分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同分词器下，一个教师token可能对应多个学生token。学生生成教师token的部分前缀后，多个学生token都能完成剩余字节，但教师只给定了所需完成内容，未指明如何在这些有效续接间分配概率。现有跨分词器方法只做token或文本span对齐，未解决这一部分生成后的概率拆分问题。

Method: ESCD在跨分词器概率对齐之外，补充完成集监督：聚合与前缀相关的教师事件，监督与字节兼容的一步学生完成的总概率，避开对单个token的依赖分词器的概率拆分。方法复用学生轨迹和预测，无需额外rollout，也不改变学生词表。

Result: 在数学、代码和科学推理任务上，跨模型族与分词器均取得稳定增益，并扩展到1T教师到35B学生的MoE大规模蒸馏。局部分析表明：保留完成集能更好匹配参考监督；在部分事件进入后，一步完成覆盖了所研究分词器对中超过99%的可观测兼容教师质量。

Conclusion: 事件进入(event entry)与事件完成(event completion)可作为跨分词器知识迁移的互补监督目标。代码将在GitHub发布。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers knowledge between language models through teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories. With different tokenizers, a single teacher token may require multiple student tokens to generate, creating intermediate states where the event is entered but not yet completed. Existing cross-tokenizer methods align tokens or text spans to construct comparable prediction targets. We study a complementary problem after partial generation: once the student produces a prefix of a teacher token, multiple next tokens may complete the same remaining bytes, but the teacher only specifies the required completion rather than how probability should be divided among these valid continuations. We introduce Event-Set Completion Distillation (ESCD), which complements cross-tokenizer probability alignment with completion-set supervision. ESCD aggregates prefix-related teacher events and supervises the total probability of byte-compatible one-step student completions, avoiding tokenizer-dependent probability splits among individual tokens. The method reuses student trajectories and predictions, requiring neither additional rollouts nor changes to the student vocabulary. Experiments demonstrate consistent gains in mathematics, code, and scientific reasoning across model families and tokenizers, extending to large-scale MoE distillation from a 1T teacher to a 35B student. Local analyses show that retaining completion sets better matches the reference supervision, while one-step completion covers over 99% of observed compatible teacher mass after partial event entry in the studied tokenizer pairs. These findings support event entry and event completion as complementary supervision targets for cross-tokenizer knowledge transfer. Code will be released on GitHub.

</details>


### [123] [SCBO: Semantically Coherent Batching and Ordering for LLM-Based Social Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35250)
*Yuanzi Li,Lingjie Wang,Zihang Tian,Lei Wang,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SCBO框架，通过语义连贯的批处理与排序，用LLM模拟问卷受访者，降低token消耗与推理时间并提升预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逐题预测方式重复编码相同上下文、每题参考样本有限，且后续预测无法利用先前答案信息。

Method: SCBO为免训练框架：用LLM提取问卷题项的紧凑语义表示并过滤模板噪声；将相关问题分组批量处理并构建共享参考库（目标特定检索+质心补全）；按易到难排序目标问题，并按语义对齐排列参考。

Result: 在四个大规模问卷数据集和四种LLM上，SCBO显著降低token消耗与推理时间，且总体上优于非批处理基线的预测准确率。

Conclusion: 多题批处理结合语义连贯分组与合理排序，可有效提升LLM问卷模拟的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a scalable way to simulate survey respondents using demographic profiles and observed reference responses. However, the conventional approach of predicting one question per prompt repeatedly encodes the same context, limits each target to a narrow set of reference responses, and prevents later predictions from using information in earlier answers. Predicting multiple questions in one prompt can reduce these costs, share a broader pool of references, and let later predictions build on earlier ones. This requires forming coherent batches, selecting shared references, and ordering questions and references effectively. We propose Semantically Coherent Batching and Ordering (SCBO), a training-free framework that addresses these challenges. SCBO first uses an LLM to extract compact semantic representations from survey items and filter out template noise. It then groups related questions into batches and builds a shared reference bank using target-specific retrieval and centroid-based completion. Finally, it orders target questions from easy to hard and arranges references according to their semantic alignment with those questions. Experiments on four large-scale survey datasets and four LLMs show that SCBO substantially reduces token consumption and inference time while generally improving prediction accuracy over a non-batched baseline. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SCBO-41D8.

</details>


### [124] [Measuring Collapse and Correction in Homogeneous-Panel LLM Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35279)
*Xin Li,Mengbing Liu,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出可审计的多智能体LLM辩论协议，用转变账本（崩溃/修正/起始/带符号干预效用）替代仅看最终准确率，揭示防崩溃与保修正之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估只看最终答案是否提升，混淆了两种相反机制：讨论可能挽救错的多数，也可能破坏对的多数。

Method: 在MMLU-Pro上对6,925场同质辩论记录转变账本；用留一模型探针门控冻结做回放实验，并测试辩论前8探针筛选。

Result: 识别253次崩溃与对应修正；门控冻结防住29次崩溃却损失108次修正。8探针与条件崩溃风险强相关(G=7, rho=0.893, p=0.0123)，但与初始多数准确率接近(rho=0.821)，不能作校准预测。

Conclusion: 仅防崩溃会推荐错误策略；需用带符号效用账本统一比较，并释放可重放工具支持未来模型-脚手架对比。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) debate is often evaluated by whether final answers improve, but movement is not necessarily improvement: the same discussion can rescue an initially wrong majority or destroy an initially correct one. Standard final-accuracy evaluations conflate these opposing mechanisms. We introduce an auditable protocol for homogeneous debate on multiple-choice questions (MCQs) that records each run as a transition ledger over collapse, correction, onset, and signed intervention utility. On 6,925 MMLU-Pro debates, the protocol identifies 253 collapses and a parallel correction ledger that changes how interventions should be judged. Replay experiments reveal the central tradeoff: a leave-one-model-out probe-gated freeze prevents 29 collapses but loses 108 corrections under equal weights, so collapse prevention alone can recommend the wrong policy. A compact pre-debate 8-probe screen is a triage signal: its unadjusted family-level association with conditional-collapse risk is high (G=7, Spearman rho=0.893, exact two-sided p=0.0123), but initial-majority accuracy is a close comparator (rho=0.821; family partial rho=0.767, p=0.0877), so we do not treat it as calibrated or capability-adjusted prediction. Round-level traces localize many collapses to the first debate round, where early disagreement can precede both harmful cascades and useful recovery. We release replayable schemas, coders, audits, cost cards, and zero-API rebuild scripts so future model-scaffold rows can be compared under the same denominators and signed utility ledger.

</details>


### [125] [Can LLMs Value the Right Evidence? Evidence-Value Misalignment in Dynamic Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35627)
*Kehua Feng,Yunsheng Lu,Yitong Qiao,Tiantian He,Lei Liu,Yue Shen,Jian Wang,Jinjie Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Evidence-Value Misalignment (EVM)概念，区分"证据是否充分"与"诊断是否正确"，并构建MedEVM基准与EVD-Harness框架，证明验证证据支持可提升诊断可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有诊断评测以结果准确率为导向，可能奖励"侥幸猜对"。模型可能在证据不足或误导的情况下得出正确诊断，掩盖了证据与决策不匹配的隐患。因此需要将"证据支撑"与"诊断准确"解耦评估。

Method: 提出EVM概念及MedEVM动态基准（1050案例、24个疾病系统），观察逐轮到达，模型需决定等待证据或提交诊断。据此设计EVD-Harness：将诊断生成与提交解耦，包含离线Contrastive Diagnostic Wiki及三阶段在线控制（观察管理、提案与证人验证、诊断提交控制）。

Result: 9个LLM呈现四种模式：证据追踪失准（推理模式更差）、提交失准、证据顺序影响诊断、误导证据持续影响决策。EVM可预测错误，阻止过早提交可提升准确率。EVD-Harness在5个LLM上准确率提升12.0–51.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 在提交诊断前验证证据支持，能有效缓解EVM相关失败，使诊断决策更可靠。

Abstract: A correct diagnosis reached from insufficient or misleading evidence can pose a clinical hazard, yet outcome-based accuracy may reward such lucky guesses. We call this mismatch between diagnostic decisions and the value of available evidence Evidence-Value Misalignment (EVM). To disentangle evidential grounding independently from diagnostic accuracy, we introduce MedEVM, a dynamic benchmarking environment comprising 1,050 cases across 24 disease systems. Observations arrive turn by turn, requiring models to continuously calibrate its decision by deciding whether to wait for more evidence or submit a diagnosis. Across 9 LLMs, four interesting patterns are observed. (1) Miscalibrated evidence tracking. Making a diagnosis often fails to calibrate evidence sufficiency, even in more capable models, and even worsens in reasoning mode. (2) Misaligned diagnosis submission. Confidence in the correct diagnosis often fails to ensure timely submission despite sufficient evidence. (3) Evidence order matters. Reordering the same evidence changes diagnoses even when model confidence remains similar. (4) Misleading evidence remains influential. Added misleading evidence redirects diagnoses even after prior evidence becomes sufficient. We further verify that EVM predicts errors and that preventing premature submission improves accuracy. These findings motivate Evidence-Verified Diagnosis Harness (EVD-Harness). It decouples diagnosis generation from submission through an offline Contrastive Diagnostic Wiki and three online control stages, namely observation management, proposal and witness verification, and diagnosis submission control. Across five LLMs, EVD-Harness improves accuracy by 12.0--51.1 percentage points while mitigating EVM-related failures. Our results demonstrate that verifying evidential support before submission can make diagnostic decisions more reliable.

</details>


### [126] [QuanReview: Offline, Auditable Reconciliation of Human and LLM Span Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35685)
*Matteo Musacchio,Juan Cruz Giner Pulero,Isabel Castañeda,Naomi Couriel,Yelena Mejova,Mariano G. Beiró,Kyriaki Kalimeri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: QuanReview 是一个开源系统，用于审计和修正结构化跨度标注（如数量、单位、不确定性修饰、事件类别），通过字符级对齐、策略化自动决策与浏览器端人工裁决，提升 LLM 参与下标注层的可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化跨度标注创建成本高，且在语言模型介入后难以保持可信；需要一种机制来审计和纠正这些标注层，调和人工与 LLM 两路标注流之间的冲突。

Method: 系统对同一文档的两路标注流进行字符级对齐；对无歧义情况按显式且可记录的策略自动解决；将候选冲突送入浏览器端裁决界面，供审阅者选择任一方、构建字段级混合或标记重新标注。活动管理器支持多标注者、可配置冗余度、文档/跨度级一致性计算、全体一致文档自动合并，并按原始文件格式导出修正后的标注层。

Result: 在包含 4,457 条记录的人道主义基准与一 LLM 抽取流上：8% 文档被完全自动合并，另有 1,513 条记录应用了自动策略决策，将人工注意力集中在 3,131 个候选冲突上，平均每个审阅文档 5.4 个冲突。

Conclusion: QuanReview 能有效降低人工审阅负担，聚焦真正需要判断的冲突，并可直接替换原始标注文件，适用于 LLM 参与的结构化标注审计与修正。

Abstract: Structured span annotations, such as quantities with their units, uncertainty modifiers, and event classes, are expensive to create and hard to keep trustworthy once language models enter the loop. We present QuanReview, an open-source system for auditing and correcting such annotation layers. QuanReview aligns two annotation streams over the same documents at character level, resolves unambiguous cases by an explicit and logged policy, and routes candidate conflicts to a browser-based adjudication interface where reviewers accept either side, build field-level hybrids, or flag items for re-annotation. A campaign manager assigns documents to multiple annotators with configurable redundancy, computes agreement at document and span level, auto-merges unanimous documents, and exports the corrected layer in the original file format, so that it can replace the original annotation files directly. Applied to a 4,457-record humanitarian benchmark and an LLM extraction stream, the system fully auto-merged 8% of documents, applied automatic policy decisions to a further 1,513 records, and concentrated human attention on 3,131 candidate conflicts, a mean of 5.4 per reviewed document.

</details>


### [127] [Scaling Long-Form Story Generation via Narrative State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35759)
*Zhennan Wan,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 NstAgent 框架，通过跟踪结构化叙事状态，使 LLM 能生成长达 10 万字且叙事一致性不随长度下降的长篇小说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在创意写作上表现强，但扩展到全长篇小说时叙事一致性难以维持；现有故事生成方法多局限于约 1 万字以内的故事，向长篇小说扩展的能力尚缺乏探索。

Method: 提出 Narrative State Tracking Agent（NstAgent），一个无需训练的智能体框架，让 LLM 跟踪包含角色、过往事件和未来需求的结构化叙事状态；并扩展已有基准以比较不同长度下的叙事一致性，结合写作质量基准进行评估。

Result: 在 1 万至 10 万字范围的系统评测中，NstAgent 随故事变长取得更好的叙事一致性和写作质量，且两者随长度增加均无明显退化。

Conclusion: NstAgent 为将故事生成扩展到全长篇小说提供了一条有效途径。

Abstract: LLMs have demonstrated strong capabilities in creative writing. However, scaling them to full-length novels remains challenging, as maintaining narrative consistency becomes increasingly difficult. Existing story-generation methods typically focus on stories of up to about ten thousand words, leaving their ability to scale to full-length novels underexplored. In this work, we introduce Narrative State Tracking Agent (NstAgent), a training-free agentic framework that allows LLMs to track a structured narrative state including characters, past events and future requirements. We extend an existing benchmark to compare narrative consistency across lengths, and use it together with a writing-quality benchmark to systematically evaluate stories ranging from 10K to 100K words. We show that NstAgent achieves better narrative consistency and writing quality as stories grow longer, and neither of them degrades noticeably as length increases, suggesting that it provides an effective approach to scaling story generation toward full-length novels.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Humanoid Badminton: Learning Dynamic Racket Skills from Limited Human Motion Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31840)
*Jingzhi Cui,Zhexiong Wang,Bangjie Xu,Pengyu Zhao,Youyuan Li,Zhi Su,Peng Ren,Mengdi Xu,Chao Yu,Yi Wu,Luyang Wang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出三阶段分层强化学习框架，实现真实人形机器人羽毛球多技能对打，含跳杀回球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速球类运动要求人形机器人具备实时决策、精准击球与全身动态协调。羽毛球轨迹变化快，需在短接触窗口内精准控制球拍位姿与速度，且击球空间广。人类动作数据可提供先验，但羽毛球可用参考有限且不完美：直接跟踪难以覆盖多样球况，纯任务驱动优化又易产生不自然动作。

Method: 三阶段分层强化学习框架：(1) 任务随机化动作增强，将稀疏标注击球事件扩展为目标条件化的可执行击球变体，形成连续隐技能空间；(2) 高层规划器根据观测到的球状态在线输出连续隐技能码，组合技能；(3) 上下文条件对抗正则化器，提升规划器技能使用的自然性同时保持回球性能。

Result: 部署于真实人形机器人，实现与人类选手的持续多技能对打，包括正手、反手及高动态跳跃回球。

Conclusion: 首个在真实世界中展示多技能人-机器人对打（含高动态跳杀回球）的人形机器人拍类运动系统。

Abstract: High-speed racket sports provide a demanding testbed for humanoid robots, requiring time-critical decisions, precise striking, and dynamic whole-body coordination. In badminton, fast-changing shuttle trajectories require timely contact decisions, while successful returns demand precise racket pose and velocity within a brief contact window and across a broad three-dimensional striking workspace. Human motion data provide valuable priors for such athletic skills, but usable badminton references are limited and imperfect. Direct tracking provides insufficient executable variation for diverse shuttle conditions, while purely task-driven optimization may produce unnatural motion. To address these challenges, we present a three-stage hierarchical reinforcement learning framework for dynamic humanoid badminton. First, task-randomized motion augmentation expands sparse annotated hitting events into executable target-conditioned stroke variations, forming a continuous latent skill space. Second, a high-level planner outputs continuous latent skill codes to compose these skills online according to the observed shuttle state. Third, a context-conditioned adversarial regularizer encourages more natural planner-level skill usage while preserving return performance. When deployed on a real humanoid robot, our system achieves sustained multi-skill rallies with human players, including forehand, backhand, and highly dynamic jump returns. This is the first real-world humanoid racket-sport system to demonstrate multi-skill human--robot rallies including highly dynamic jump returns.

</details>


### [129] [REALM: A Coarse-to-Fine Generative Framework for Embodied Reactive Listening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33095)
*Peizhen Li,Longbing Cao,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REALM：一种用于音频驱动反应式聆听面部动作的粗到细生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聆听者面部反应建模面临两大挑战：需考虑说话人线索的时序（反应存在时间滞后）并与聆听者已有动作保持连续；同时需在整体运动轨迹基础上捕捉局部可变的面部事件（如短暂表情与眨眼），这些难以确定性预测。这促使结合历史感知的时序对齐与随机表情细化。

Method: 提出REALM粗到细框架：1) Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion模块，通过延迟中心注意力先验与自适应门控融合聆听者动作历史与说话人音频；2) 粗解码器预测基础运动轨迹；3) 在表情子空间中以音频条件随机残差进行细化，同时保留粗姿态参数。

Result: 在ViCo与L2L数据集上，多项运动质量指标优于所评估基线。额外分析考察了延迟敏感性、门控行为与眨眼动态。在Ameca人形机器人上部署并通过用户感知研究，验证生成行为可用于物理实体。

Conclusion: REALM通过结合历史感知时序对齐与随机表情细化，有效实现音频驱动的反应式聆听面部动作生成，并具备向实体机器人迁移的实用价值。

Abstract: Generating responsive listener facial motion is an important task for embodied conversational AI. Two modeling challenges are central: accounting for the timing of speaker cues while maintaining continuity with the listener's ongoing motion, and capturing locally variable facial events alongside the overall motion trajectory. Listener responses may follow preceding cues with a temporal lag, while brief expressions and blinks introduce variation that is difficult to predict deterministically. These challenges motivate a framework that combines history-aware temporal alignment with stochastic expression refinement. We propose REALM (Reactive Embodied Audio-driven Listening Model), a coarse-to-fine framework for audio-driven reactive listening. A Reactive Gated Speaker-Listener Fusion module combines listener motion history with speaker audio through a delay-centered attention prior and adaptive gating. A coarse decoder predicts a base motion trajectory, which is augmented by audio-conditioned stochastic residuals in the expression subspace while retaining the coarse pose parameters. Evaluations on ViCo and L2L show improvements over the evaluated baselines across multiple motion-quality metrics. Additional analyses examine delay sensitivity, gate behavior, and blink dynamics. Finally, deployment on an Ameca humanoid robot and a perceptual user study demonstrate the applicability of the generated behavior to physical embodiment.
  Code: https://github.com/lipzh5/REALM Demo: https://youtu.be/Tf5mpd5S8VQ

</details>


### [130] [Learning with Object-centric Representations of Tactile Interactive Perception for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33235)
*Xinyi Yang,Zilin Si,Zhuowei Xu,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出以物体为中心、上下文感知的操作框架，通过触觉探索学习任务无关的物体表征，用于指导机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号稀疏且短暂，视觉难以直接推断材料、容器内容、柔软度等隐式属性，如何提取有效触觉事件并融入操作仍具挑战。

Method: 使用token learner从长时程探索中自主选择代表性触觉片段；通过与描述性文本嵌入的对比对齐学习捕捉多种物理属性的潜空间；将学到的表征作为语义上下文指导物体中心操作策略。

Result: 属性估计在已见/未见物体上达93%/84%准确率；在多物体重排、倒水、开箱三任务中，任务成功率已见物体从41%提升至92%，未见物体从19%提升至67%。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效学习触觉物体表征并改善目标选择与依赖属性的操作自适应，显著提升视觉模糊场景下的任务成功率。

Abstract: Implicit object properties that are difficult to directly infer from vision, such as material, container contents, or softness, can be revealed through tactile sensing and exploratory interactions. However, because tactile signals are transient and sparse, extracting informative tactile events and effectively incorporating them into robotic manipulation remains a challenge. In this work, we present an object-centric context-aware manipulation framework that learns task-agnostic object representations through tactile exploration. A token learner autonomously selects representative tactile segments from long-horizon exploration, while contrastive alignment with descriptive text embeddings enables a latent space that captures multiple physical object properties. These learned representations are then used as semantic context to guide object-centric manipulation policies and adapt strategies based on object properties. Experiments show that the learned representations achieve 93% and 84% property estimation accuracy on seen and unseen objects. Evaluated on three tasks involving visually ambiguous objects, i.e. multi-object rearrangement, pouring, and box opening, the proposed framework improves both target selection and property-dependent manipulation adaptation, raising task success, aggregated over all evaluation trials, from 41% to 92% on seen objects and from 19% to 67% on unseen objects over baseline policies without object-context conditioning. Videos and additional results are available at https://xinyiyxyx.github.io/tactile-object-centric/.

</details>


### [131] [Beyond Retrieval Relevance: Scene-Grounded Risk Entailment for Vision-Language Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34145)
*Jiaxin Liu,Ruilin Yu,Liang Peng,Jingkai Wang,Chengxiang Zhao,Zhenxin Zhu,Bing Wang,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Hong Wang,Jun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出驾驶风险知识图谱(DRKG)与SWRL推理阶段，在VLM决策前将检索到的风险知识转化为场景适用的事件与风险关系证据，提升安全加权规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG为视觉-语言驾驶系统提供外部安全知识，但检索到的风险规则可能相关却不适用于当前场景。VLM需自行接地对象、跨时间绑定实体并验证关系，导致风险结论支撑隐含、可靠性不足，形成"相关性-适用性"鸿沟。

Method: 构建驾驶风险知识图谱(DRKG)，并引入SWRL语义网规则推理阶段。结构化感知实例化场景事实，当规则前件共同满足时，SWRL规则推导事件与有向风险关系；将识别的事件、绑定后的风险关系及被激活规则的语义描述组成紧凑证据，用于条件化VLM与扩散规划器。

Result: 在nuReasoning的匹配对比中，相较基于相关性检索的基线，规划分数(NPS)提升1.30分，非自身过失碰撞分数(NC)提升2.76分。

Conclusion: 结果表明，相较于语义检索得到的风险知识，场景适用的风险证据能更有效改善安全加权规划，验证了通过符号推理弥合相关性-适用性鸿沟的价值。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) gives vision--language driving systems access to external safety knowledge, yet a retrieved risk rule may be relevant without applying to the current scene. A vision--language model (VLM) receiving such knowledge must ground objects, bind entities across time, and verify relations before deciding how to act, leaving the support for risk conclusions implicit. We address this relevance--applicability gap with a Driving-Risk Knowledge Graph (DRKG) and Semantic Web Rule Language (SWRL) reasoning stage before VLM decision-making. Structured perception instantiates scene facts, from which SWRL rules derive events and directed risk relations when their antecedents are jointly satisfied. Recognized events, bound risk relations, and semantic descriptions of activated rules form compact evidence that conditions the VLM and diffusion planner. In matched comparisons on nuReasoning, our method improved the nuReasoning planning score (NPS) by 1.30 points and the non-at-fault collision score (NC) by 2.76 points over the relevance retrieval-based baseline. These gains indicate that scene-applicable risk evidence improves safety-weighted planning relative to semantically retrieved risk knowledge.

</details>


### [132] [Predictive Semantic Safety: From Visual Physical Reasoning to Safety-Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34356)
*Taekyung Kim,Salem Fradi,Yanning Dai,Mateusz Ostaszewski,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PSS框架将视觉物理推理与基于备份的安全过滤结合，预测未来物理事件并生成安全约束，在MuJoCo实验中将安全成功率从43.3%提升至99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理交互可能产生当前几何环境无法体现的未来危险，传统仅依赖当前障碍几何的安全方法难以应对。

Method: 用VLM预测物理事件及其时序或物体位移；显式运动模型将事件假设转为轨迹；分离式共形预测跨物体、观测时间与未来时间校准位置误差；几何形状边界将位置区域转为预测占据；对规定备份机动评估占据并导出输入仿射约束，最小化修改名义输入同时保持备份可行性。

Result: MuJoCo中Unitree Go1实验涵盖坠落装置、冲击导致支撑丢失、接触传播，PSS安全成功率99.3%，而仅用当前障碍几何的备份控制障碍函数基线为43.3%。

Conclusion: PSS通过结合视觉物理推理与备份安全过滤，有效预测并应对交互引发的未来危险，显著优于仅依赖当前几何的基线方法。

Abstract: Physical interactions can create future hazards that are not apparent from the robot's current geometric surroundings. We present a framework termed Predictive Semantic Safety (PSS), which connects visual physical reasoning to backup-based safety filtering. A vision-language model (VLM) predicts physical events and their timing or directly predicts object displacements. An explicit motion model converts event hypotheses into object trajectories. Split conformal prediction calibrates position errors jointly across specified objects, observation times, and future times; geometric shape bounds convert the resulting position regions into predicted object occupancy. PSS evaluates a prescribed backup maneuver against this occupancy and derives input-affine constraints for minimally modifying the nominal input while preserving backup feasibility under the robot dynamics and input limits. MuJoCo experiments with a Unitree Go1 consider falling fixtures, impact-driven support loss, and contact propagation. PSS achieves a safe episode rate of 99.3%, compared with 43.3% for a Backup Control Barrier Function baseline that only uses current obstacle geometry.

</details>


### [133] [ARS: Agentic Reward System for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34484)
*Sheng Hu,Weiyi Lu,Lingbing Zeng,Gan Weng,Weiwei Zhang,Kai Xie,Xiaofeng Mou,Yi Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ARS 是无需额外训练奖励模型的推理框架，用通用 VLM 通过自适应视觉检查做机器人进度奖励建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的进度估计需区分有意义状态变化、失败尝试与任务无关动作，现有奖励基线在错对象操作上会给出虚假进度。

Method: 用离线轨迹和任务指令，子代理提出任务相关事件时间线，主代理验证修订后估计逐帧进度；可选终端结果标签与视觉参考，并能审计外部奖励模型。

Result: 在语义错配基准上用 27B VLM 评估，ARS 更好抑制虚假进度错误，仿真策略学习中优于基线；并在真实机器人多螺钉紧固任务中支持混合质量离线经验的长时程策略学习。

Conclusion: 结构化推理与验证可提升通用 VLM 用于机器人奖励建模的实用性。代码已开源。

Abstract: Progress reward modeling is the problem of estimating how a robot's behavior changes task progress over time. Reliable estimation requires distinguishing meaningful state changes from failed attempts and task-irrelevant actions. We introduce the Agentic Reward System (ARS), an inference framework for progress reward modeling with general-purpose vision-language models (VLMs), without additional reward-model training. Given an offline trajectory and a task instruction, ARS uses adaptive visual inspection for both event proposal and verification. A subagent proposes a task-relevant event timeline, which a primary agent verifies and revises before estimating per-frame progress. ARS can incorporate optional terminal outcome labels and visual references to inform its judgments. It can also audit progress estimates from external reward models. We evaluate ARS with a 27B VLM on a controlled semantic-mismatch benchmark and downstream policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. The benchmark reveals that several evaluated reward baselines assign spurious progress to wrong-object manipulation even in simple pick-and-place scenes. ARS better suppresses these errors and outperforms these baselines in simulation policy learning. We further demonstrate that ARS supports long-horizon policy learning from mixed-quality offline experience on real-robot multi-screw fastening in a full-scale laboratory replica of an industrial washing-machine assembly line. These results suggest that structured inference and verification can improve the usefulness of general-purpose VLMs for robot reward modeling. Code is at https://github.com/midea-ai/ars

</details>


### [134] [Model-Informed Safe Reinforcement Learning for Bipedal Locomotion via Step-to-Step Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34486)
*Victor Paredes,Ayonga Hereid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模型引导的强化学习框架，基于ALIP模板与离散指数控制屏障函数（DECBF），为双足机器人提供步态安全证书，用作训练塑形与运行时动作过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人需在复杂环境中安全移动。传统模型方法可解释但鲁棒性差，强化学习鲁棒但缺乏原则性安全保障，二者需要结合。

Method: 以解析ALIP模板为锚，用DECBF给出逐步安全证书；作为训练期塑形信号和运行时动作过滤器，最小化调整摆动脚落点以满足模板级约束；在MuJoCo的Digit全身控制器上评估。

Result: 相比无约束基线，在外部扰动试验中减少了安全违规事件；但较大横向速度瞬变时出现安全与跟踪的权衡。

Conclusion: 模型引导的RL结合DECBF可在模板层面提供安全保障并改善安全性，但在强横向扰动下安全性与任务跟踪存在折中，需进一步平衡。

Abstract: Humanoid robots promise versatile mobility in cluttered, human-centric environments, but real deployment demands principled safety. Classical model-based gait generators yield interpretable motions but often lack the robustness and adaptability of modern reinforcement learning (RL) based approaches. We propose a model-informed reinforcement learning framework anchored to the analytical Angular Momentum Linear Inverted Pendulum (ALIP) template. We provide a step-to-step safety certificate for ALIP stepping via a discrete exponential control barrier function (DECBF) and use it as (i) a training-time shaping signal and (ii) a runtime action filter that minimally adjusts swing-foot placement to satisfy template-level constraints. Full-order safety is evaluated empirically on the Digit humanoid in MuJoCo with a whole-body controller stack. Compared to an unconstrained baseline, our approach reduces safety-violation events in the reported external-disturbance trial, while larger lateral-velocity transients reveal a safety-tracking tradeoff.

</details>


### [135] [Where Memory Belongs: Ledger, an Object Ledger for Memory-Augmented VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34554)
*Tanguy Dieudonné,Jack B. Jedlicki,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Ledger框架，将机器人操作中的记忆按类型分离：短期感知记忆置于策略内，长期物体记忆作为外部可读记录，在RoboMME上以64.3%的四套件平均成绩超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野部分可观测的机器人操作需要记忆（物体位置、事件历史等）。现有VLA模型将记忆直接嵌入策略内部，但基准测试显示没有任何单一策略内机制能覆盖所有时空维度，且大幅落后于oracle方法。作者认为记忆类型决定了记忆应存放的位置。

Method: Ledger基于单个微调的π0.5策略，将记忆分为两部分：短期感知记忆（重复、计时、回溯）放在策略内作为帧采样记忆；长期物体记忆（持久空间状态、容器关系、事件历史）作为外部ledger，由SAM3追踪器和VLM字幕器从演示中构建，并由LLM规划器在步骤边界决策读取。运行时根据指令和记录选择记忆来源，无需任务级路由器。

Result: 在RoboMME上，Ledger取得所评估方法中最高的四套件平均64.3%（最强先前方法为45.9%），物体引用60.7%（vs 40.3%），物体永存性86.7%（vs 56.2%），且仅使用单套权重。

Conclusion: 按记忆类型分离存储位置（策略内短期记忆+外部长期记忆）能显著提升长视野机器人操作性能，单一权重即可覆盖多任务，且无需任务级路由器。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon, partially observed robotic manipulation: a robot must remember which object was placed in a drawer, whose cup it moved, or how many action cycles have elapsed. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models embed memory directly inside the policy, but benchmarks show no single in-policy mechanism covers all spatio-temporal dimensions, trailing oracle methods by a wide margin. We argue that memory type dictates where memory should reside: short-term perceptual memory (repetition, timing, retracing) belongs inside the policy, while long-term object memory (persistent spatial state, containment, event history) belongs outside as an explicit, readable record. We present Ledger, a harness that realizes this split over a single fine-tuned $π_{0.5}$ policy by pairing an in-policy frame-sampling memory with an external spatio-temporal object memory, the ledger, built from a SAM3 tracker and a VLM captioner of the demonstration and read by an LLM planner that decides at step boundaries. On RoboMME, Ledger reaches the highest four-suite average among the evaluated methods, 64.3% (vs. 45.9% for the strongest prior method under identical evaluation), leading object reference (60.7% vs. 40.3%) and object permanence (86.7% vs. 56.2%) using a single set of weights. Choosing the memory source at runtime, from the instruction and the record, removes the need for a task-level router.

</details>


### [136] [ReCAT: Remember, Count, and Time: Structured Recurrent Memory for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35200)
*Pankhuri Vanjani,Mostafa Hatab,Can Mizrakli,Vaisakh Shaj,Zhuoyue Li,Moritz Reuss,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCAT是一种语言条件机器人策略，采用结构化循环记忆（Mamba-2+因果注意力）处理记忆依赖任务，在LIBERO、RMBench及真实机器人任务上均取得最佳或并列最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆依赖操作要求机器人利用当前传感器已无法获取的信息（如回忆早期视觉线索、跟踪任务进度、计数重复事件、估计经过时间）做决策，现有短历史基线难以胜任。

Method: 指令条件编码器从当前观测提取特征；循环记忆用Mamba-2层加一层因果注意力整合观测流；流匹配Transformer解码器在每个块中通过独立交叉注意力分别读取当前与历史表征。

Result: LIBERO平均成功率95.3%，RMBench 62.4%（9个任务中6个最佳或并列最佳）；三项真实机器人任务（空间回忆、事件计数、间隔计时）最佳变体平均66.7%，而最强短历史基线仅8.3%。消融显示观测编码器与每块记忆条件化均为必需；加法更新在计数与计时上成功率最高，delta-rule更新在空间回忆上最佳。

Conclusion: 结构化循环记忆对记忆依赖的机器人操作至关重要；同时为高效序列建模设计的更新规则在作为机器人记忆时表现各异，需按任务类型选择。

Abstract: Memory-dependent manipulation requires robots to make decisions using information that is no longer available to their current sensors, such as recalling an earlier visual cue, tracking task progress, counting repeated events, or estimating elapsed time. We present ReCAT, a language-conditioned policy with structured recurrent memory. An instruction-conditioned encoder forms features from the current observation. A recurrent memory integrates the observation stream through Mamba-2 layers and one causal attention layer. A flow-matching Transformer decoder reads the current and the historical representation through separate cross-attention in every block. ReCAT reaches 95.3\% average success on LIBERO and 62.4\% on RMBench, with the best or tied-best result on six of nine tasks. On three real-robot tasks probing spatial recall, event counting, and interval timing, the best ReCAT variant reaches 66.7\% average success, against 8.3\% for the strongest short-history baseline. Controlled comparisons within ReCAT show that the observation encoder and every-block memory conditioning are needed for this performance. They also show that update rules developed for efficient sequence modeling behave differently as robot memory: additive updates have the highest observed success on counting and timing, and delta-rule updates on spatial recall. Project website is at https://intuitive-robots.github.io/ReCAT

</details>


### [137] [DexAgent: An Agentic Human2Sim2Robot Framework for Dexterous Manipulation with Self-Evolving Tool Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35318)
*Youhui Wang,Yunzhu Li,Li Fei-Fei,Jiajun Wu,Huang Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexAgent是一个自演进的Human2Sim2Robot智能体框架，将单段自我中心人类视频与任务提示转化为物理合理的机器人轨迹，用于策略训练，在11个真实任务上成功率比基线高3.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频是可扩展的灵巧操作演示来源，但现有Human2Sim2Robot流水线依赖预定义流程，难以适应多样物体属性与交互，尤其是关节体和可变形物体。

Method: 框架分四阶段：1）人类视频语义理解；2）基于属性的仿真重建；3）机器人轨迹优化；4）机器人数据生成。每阶段DexAgent从工具库选择合适技能或在需要时开发新技能；属性特定的验证器评估阶段输出是否物理合理并满足任务需求，提供反馈用于修正以阻止误差传播。最终阶段变化物体与机器人状态以生成多样轨迹，并对渲染观测重贴纹理以促进sim-to-real迁移；新技能与验证器存入工具库实现自演进。

Result: 在11个真实世界任务中，用DexAgent生成数据训练的策略成功率比竞争基线高3.5倍。

Conclusion: 自适应、验证引导的流程使DexAgent能处理多样物体与长时程任务，随处理更多人类视频而积累可复用能力、减少处理时间。

Abstract: Human videos offer a scalable source of demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation. However, existing human-to-simulation-to-robot (Human2Sim2Robot) pipelines rely on predefined procedures that struggle to accommodate diverse object properties and interactions, particularly those involving articulated and deformable objects. We introduce DexAgent, an agentic Human2Sim2Robot framework that converts a single egocentric human video and a task prompt into physically grounded robot trajectories for policy training. It operates through four stages: semantic understanding of human videos, property-based simulation reconstruction, robot trajectory optimization, and robot data generation. At each stage, DexAgent adapts its approach to the task and object properties by selecting suitable skills from its tool library or developing new ones when needed. Property-specific verifiers assess stage outcomes for physical validity and task-specific requirements and provide feedback for refinement, preventing error propagation through the workflow. This adaptive, verification-guided process allows DexAgent to process diverse objects and long-horizon tasks. In the final stage, DexAgent varies object and robot states in simulation to generate diverse robot trajectories from a single human video, then retextures the rendered observations to facilitate sim-to-real transfer. Newly developed skills and verifiers are retained in its tool library, making it self-evolving to accumulate reusable capabilities. This reduces processing time as DexAgent encounters more human videos. Across eleven real-world tasks, policies trained with DexAgent-generated data achieve a 3.5x higher success rate than competing baselines. Project website: https://dexagent123.github.io/.

</details>
